"""SQLite storage under $DATAPULSE_HOME (default `.datapulse/`). Older databases are upgraded on startup."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator


def home() -> Path:
    return Path(os.getenv("DATAPULSE_HOME", ".datapulse")).resolve()


def upload_dir() -> Path:
    return home() / "uploads"


def samples_dir() -> Path:
    """Sample SQLite sources created by "Try it with sample data"; an allowed SQLite folder."""
    return home() / "samples"


def source_files_dir() -> Path:
    """Files uploaded as data sources: <upload id>/<original name>."""
    return home() / "source_files"


def configure_sources() -> None:
    """Point the file and SQLite plug-ins at this server's folders (the sample databases are SQLite files)."""
    folders = [d for d in os.getenv("DATAPULSE_SQLITE_DIR", "").split(os.pathsep) if d]
    if str(samples_dir()) not in folders:
        os.environ["DATAPULSE_SQLITE_DIR"] = os.pathsep.join([*folders, str(samples_dir())])
    os.environ["DATAPULSE_SOURCE_FILES"] = str(source_files_dir())


def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat()


@contextmanager
def connect() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    home().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db = sqlite3.connect(home() / "datapulse.sqlite3")
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        with db:  # commit on success, roll back on error
            yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    api_key_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    config_yaml TEXT NOT NULL,
    baseline_json TEXT,
    baseline_dataset_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS datasets (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    filename TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
    columns_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    dataset_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES datasets(id),
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    issue_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
    report_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS runs_project_created ON runs(project_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sources (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    settings_json TEXT NOT NULL,       -- includes secrets; never returned to the browser
    recipe_json TEXT,                  -- approved mapping steps (NULL: not mapped yet)
    proposal_json TEXT,                -- the latest AI mapping proposal, waiting for review
    is_standard INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- this source's data defines the project's standard format
    last_pulled_at TEXT,
    last_run_id TEXT,
    created_by TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS destinations (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    settings_json TEXT NOT NULL,       -- url (secret, never returned to the browser), table, mode
    last_sent_at TEXT,
    last_result TEXT,
    created_by TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
    token_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
"""

# Columns added after the first release: (table, column, definition)
ADDED_COLUMNS = [
    ("runs", "created_by", "TEXT"),          # who ran the check: a user's name, or "API"
    ("runs", "review_status", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open'"),  # open | acknowledged | resolved
    ("runs", "review_by", "TEXT"),
    ("runs", "review_at", "TEXT"),
]


def init_db() -> None:
    upload_dir().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with connect() as db:
        db.executescript(SCHEMA)
        for table, column, definition in ADDED_COLUMNS:
            existing = {r["name"] for r in db.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")}
            if column not in existing:
                db.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {table} ADD COLUMN {column} {definition}")

"""Destinations: the company's own databases that unified data is delivered to.

Unify sends to every destination automatically, but only when the unified data passes its checks; data that fails
is held back and the report says why. Connection URLs stay on the server.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

import pandas as pd
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from datapulse.destinations import check_settings, describe_target, send
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.sources import public_settings, type_of
from datapulse.sources.database import detect

from .core import dataset_rows, project_row
from .db import connect, now_iso

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api")


class DestinationForm(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    url: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)  # empty on edit: keep the saved one
    table: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=130)
    mode: Literal["replace", "append"] = "replace"


class TestForm(BaseModel):
    url: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)
    table: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=130)
    mode: Literal["replace", "append"] = "replace"
    destination_id: Optional[str] = None


def _row(project_id: str, destination_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    with connect() as db:
        row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM destinations WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?",
                         (destination_id, project_id)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Destination not found")
    return row


def _public(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    settings = json.loads(row["settings_json"])
    return {"id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "type": type_of("database", settings),
            "table": settings["table"], "mode": settings["mode"],
            "saved_secrets": public_settings("database", {"url": settings["url"]})["saved_secrets"],
            "last_sent_at": row["last_sent_at"], "last_result": row["last_result"],
            "created_by": row["created_by"], "created_at": row["created_at"]}


def _settings(form, previous: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> Dict[str, str]:
    url = form.url.strip() or (previous or {}).get("url", "")
    return {"url": url, "table": form.table.strip(), "mode": form.mode}


def deliver(project_id: str, doc: Dict[str, Any], df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Send a unified table to every destination of the project, if it passed its checks. One entry per destination."""
    with connect() as db:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM destinations WHERE project_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, rowid",
                          (project_id,)).fetchall()
    results = []
    for row in rows:
        settings = json.loads(row["settings_json"])
        entry = {"id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "type": type_of("database", settings), "table": settings["table"]}
        if doc["status"] != "passed":
            results.append({**entry, "ok": False, "held": True,
                            "message": "Held back: the unified data failed its checks, so nothing was sent."})
            continue
        try:
            entry.update(ok=True, held=False, message=send(settings, df))
        except DataPulseError as exc:
            entry.update(ok=False, held=False, message=str(exc))
        with connect() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE destinations SET last_sent_at = ?, last_result = ? WHERE id = ?",
                       (now_iso(), entry["message"], row["id"]))
        results.append(entry)
    return results


@router.get("/projects/{project_id}/destinations")
def list_destinations(project_id: str) -> list:
    project_row(project_id)
    with connect() as db:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM destinations WHERE project_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, rowid",
                          (project_id,)).fetchall()
    return [_public(r) for r in rows]


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/destinations/test")
def test_destination(project_id: str, payload: TestForm) -> dict:
    """Connect and report what's in the table now. Nothing is written."""
    project_row(project_id)
    previous = json.loads(_row(project_id, payload.destination_id)["settings_json"]) if payload.destination_id else None
    try:
        return {"ok": True, "message": describe_target(_settings(payload, previous))}
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        return {"ok": False, "message": str(exc)}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/destinations")
def add_destination(project_id: str, payload: DestinationForm, request: Request) -> dict:
    project_row(project_id)
    settings = _check(_settings(payload))
    destination_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("INSERT INTO destinations (id, project_id, name, settings_json, created_by, created_at) "
                   "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", (destination_id, project_id, payload.name.strip(), json.dumps(settings),
                                                 request.state.user["name"], now_iso()))
    return _public(_row(project_id, destination_id))


@router.put("/projects/{project_id}/destinations/{destination_id}")
def edit_destination(project_id: str, destination_id: str, payload: DestinationForm) -> dict:
    row = _row(project_id, destination_id)
    settings = _check(_settings(payload, json.loads(row["settings_json"])))
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE destinations SET name = ?, settings_json = ? WHERE id = ?",
                   (payload.name.strip(), json.dumps(settings), destination_id))
    return _public(_row(project_id, destination_id))


@router.delete("/projects/{project_id}/destinations/{destination_id}")
def delete_destination(project_id: str, destination_id: str) -> dict:
    _row(project_id, destination_id)
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("DELETE FROM destinations WHERE id = ?", (destination_id,))
    return {"deleted": True}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/runs/{run_id}/send")
def send_run(project_id: str, run_id: str) -> dict:
    """Send a unified check's data again (e.g. after adding a destination). Only data that passed is sent."""
    project_row(project_id)
    with connect() as db:
        run = db.execute("SELECT dataset_id, report_json FROM runs WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?",
                         (run_id, project_id)).fetchone()
    if run is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Run not found")
    doc = json.loads(run["report_json"])
    if not doc.get("unified"):
        raise HTTPException(409, "Only unified data is sent to destinations")
    df = dataset_rows(run["dataset_id"])
    if df is None:
        raise HTTPException(409, "The data for this check is no longer stored")
    doc["delivered"] = deliver(project_id, doc, df)
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE runs SET report_json = ? WHERE id = ?", (json.dumps(doc), run_id))
    return {"delivered": doc["delivered"]}


def _check(settings: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
    try:
        detect(settings["url"])
        return check_settings(settings)
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(exc)) from exc

"""Encrypt source credentials at rest with Fernet (DATAPULSE_SECRET_KEY)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError

_ENV = "DATAPULSE_SECRET_KEY"


def _fernet():
    try:
        from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(
            "cryptography is required to store source secrets. Install with: pip install 'datapulse[web]'"
        ) from exc
    raw = os.getenv(_ENV, "").strip()
    if not raw:
        # Dev fallback: derive a stable key from DATAPULSE_HOME so local installs work
        # without extra setup. Production should set DATAPULSE_SECRET_KEY explicitly.
        home = os.getenv("DATAPULSE_HOME", ".datapulse")
        digest = hashlib.sha256(f"datapulse-dev:{home}".encode()).digest()
        key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(digest)
    elif len(raw) == 44 and raw.endswith("="):
        key = raw.encode()  # already a Fernet key
    else:
        key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).digest())
    return Fernet(key)


def encrypt_secrets(secrets: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[str]:
    if not secrets:
        return None
    token = _fernet().encrypt(json.dumps(secrets).encode("utf-8"))
    return token.decode("ascii")


def decrypt_secrets(blob: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not blob:
        return {}
    try:
        from cryptography.fernet import InvalidToken
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(
            "cryptography is required to read source secrets. Install with: pip install 'datapulse[web]'"
        ) from exc
    try:
        raw = _fernet().decrypt(blob.encode("ascii"))
    except InvalidToken as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(
            "Could not decrypt source secrets. Was DATAPULSE_SECRET_KEY changed?"
        ) from exc
    data = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise DataPulseError("Stored secrets are corrupt")
    return data


def mask_secrets(secrets: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Shape returned to the UI: keys present, values replaced with a mask."""
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, value in secrets.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            out[key] = {k: "••••••••" for k in value}
        elif value:
            out[key] = "••••••••"
        else:
            out[key] = value
    return out

"""Data sources: connect databases and links to a project, map each onto the standard format, pull, and unify.

Secrets (passwords, connection URLs) stay in the server's database and are never sent back to the browser.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import shutil
import sqlite3
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Literal, Optional

import pandas as pd
from fastapi import APIRouter, File, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.io import ARCHIVES, INPUTS, load_dataframe
from datapulse.sources.connected import read_any
from datapulse.unifier.documents import is_document
from datapulse.compare import compare_mapping
from datapulse.samples import write_samples
from datapulse.sources import catalog, clean_settings, fetch_source, public_settings, try_connection, type_of
from datapulse.unify import SOURCE_COLUMN, apply_recipe, combine, propose_mapping, validate_recipe

from .core import MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, ROOT, ai_call, dataset_rows, loads, project_config, project_row, run_check, save_baseline
from .destinations import deliver
from .db import configure_sources, connect, now_iso, samples_dir, source_files_dir, upload_dir

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api")
UPLOADABLE = tuple(e for e in INPUTS if e not in ARCHIVES)


class SourceForm(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    kind: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    settings: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class TestForm(BaseModel):
    kind: str
    settings: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    source_id: Optional[str] = None  # testing an edit: blank secrets fall back to the saved ones


class SampleRequest(BaseModel):
    kind: str


# Public files the Add source form can fill in, so a new source can be tried without credentials.
REMOTE_SAMPLES = {
    "link": ("Sample states file",
             {"url": "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/plotly/datasets/master/2014_usa_states.csv"}),
    "rest": ("Sample users API", {"url": "https://jsonplaceholder.typicode.com/users"}),
    "web_page": ("Example page", {"url": "https://example.com"}),
}


class Pull(BaseModel):
    role: Literal["candidate", "baseline"] = "candidate"


class Recipe(BaseModel):
    operations: list = Field(max_length=100)


class Compare(BaseModel):
    operations: Optional[list] = Field(default=None, max_length=100)  # None: the proposal, else the saved recipe


def _source(project_id: str, source_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    with connect() as db:
        row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM sources WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?", (source_id, project_id)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Source not found")
    return row


def _public(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    recipe = loads(row["recipe_json"])
    settings = json.loads(row["settings_json"])
    return {
        "id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "kind": row["kind"], "type": type_of(row["kind"], settings),
        "settings": public_settings(row["kind"], settings),
        "status": "standard" if row["is_standard"] else "mapped" if recipe is not None else "needs_mapping",
        "recipe": recipe or [], "proposal": loads(row["proposal_json"]),
        "last_pulled_at": row["last_pulled_at"], "last_run_id": row["last_run_id"],
        "created_by": row["created_by"], "created_at": row["created_at"],
    }


def _fail(exc: DataPulseError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(400, str(exc))


def _fetch(row: sqlite3.Row) -> pd.DataFrame:
    try:
        return fetch_source(row["kind"], json.loads(row["settings_json"]), row["name"])
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        raise _fail(exc) from exc


def _store(df: pd.DataFrame) -> Any:
    path = upload_dir() / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.csv"
    df.to_csv(path, index=False)
    return path


def _update(source_id: str, **values) -> None:
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute(f"UPDATE sources SET {', '.join(f'{k} = ?' for k in values)} WHERE id = ?",
                   (*values.values(), source_id))


def _create(project_id: str, name: str, kind: str, settings: Dict[str, str], actor: str) -> str:
    source_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("INSERT INTO sources (id, project_id, name, kind, settings_json, created_by, created_at) "
                   "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", (source_id, project_id, name, kind, json.dumps(settings), actor, now_iso()))
    return source_id


def _make_standard(project: sqlite3.Row, row: sqlite3.Row, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """This source's format becomes the project's standard (its baseline)."""
    result = save_baseline(project, row["name"], _store(df), df)
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE sources SET is_standard = 0 WHERE project_id = ?", (project["id"],))
    _update(row["id"], is_standard=1, recipe_json=json.dumps([]), proposal_json=None, last_pulled_at=now_iso())
    return result


def add_sample_sources(project_id: str, actor: str) -> None:
    """Three sample databases holding the same orders in different formats; the first one is the standard."""
    configure_sources()
    orders = load_dataframe(ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders.csv")
    for n, sample in enumerate(write_samples(orders, samples_dir() / project_id)):
        source_id = _create(project_id, sample["name"], "sqlite", {"path": sample["path"], "query": sample["query"]}, actor)
        if n == 0:
            row = _source(project_id, source_id)
            _make_standard(project_row(project_id), row, _fetch(row))


def remove_uploaded_files(rows) -> None:
    """Delete the uploaded files behind File sources (when the source or its project is deleted)."""
    for row in rows:
        stored = json.loads(row["settings_json"]).get("file", "") if row["kind"] == "file" else ""
        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}/[^/\\]+", stored):
            shutil.rmtree(source_files_dir() / stored.split("/")[0], ignore_errors=True)


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/files")
async def upload_source_file(project_id: str, file: UploadFile = File(...)) -> dict:
    """Store a file for a File source and return the id to save in its settings. A document (PDF, Word, image...)
    is read once here: the AI extracts its records into a table, so later pulls don't call the AI again."""
    project_row(project_id)
    name = re.sub(r"[^\w.\- ]", "_", Path(file.filename or "data.csv").name).strip() or "data.csv"
    if Path(name).suffix.lower() not in UPLOADABLE:
        raise HTTPException(415, "Supported files: CSV, Excel, JSON, Parquet, PDF, Word, text, Markdown, email, "
                                 "HTML and images")
    content = await file.read(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1)
    if len(content) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(413, "The file is larger than 25 MB")
    upload_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    folder = source_files_dir() / upload_id
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / name).write_bytes(content)
    if not is_document(name):
        return {"file": f"{upload_id}/{name}", "name": name, "note": None}
    try:
        rows = ai_call(lambda: read_any(folder / name, name))
    except HTTPException:
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)
        raise
    table = f"{Path(name).stem}.csv"
    rows.to_csv(folder / table, index=False)
    return {"file": f"{upload_id}/{table}", "name": name,
            "note": f"Read {name}: {len(rows):,} record(s) with {len(rows.columns)} fields."}


def _file_sample() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """The example orders CSV, stored like an uploaded File source."""
    configure_sources()
    upload_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    folder = source_files_dir() / upload_id
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / "orders.csv").write_bytes((ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders.csv").read_bytes())
    return {"name": "Sample orders", "settings": {"file": f"{upload_id}/orders.csv"}}


@router.get("/sources/catalog")
def source_catalog() -> list:
    return catalog()


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/sample")
def source_sample(project_id: str, payload: SampleRequest) -> dict:
    """Settings that make Test connection succeed, for the Add source form."""
    project_row(project_id)
    if payload.kind == "file":
        return _file_sample()
    found = REMOTE_SAMPLES.get(payload.kind)
    if found is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "This source has no test data. Enter your own connection details.")
    name, settings = found
    return {"name": name, "settings": settings}


@router.get("/projects/{project_id}/sources")
def list_sources(project_id: str) -> list:
    project_row(project_id)
    with connect() as db:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM sources WHERE project_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, rowid", (project_id,)).fetchall()
    return [_public(r) for r in rows]


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/test")
def test_connection(project_id: str, payload: TestForm) -> dict:
    """Try a connection without saving it. Failures are a normal answer here, not an error."""
    project_row(project_id)
    previous = None
    if payload.source_id:
        previous = json.loads(_source(project_id, payload.source_id)["settings_json"])
    try:
        return {"ok": True, "message": try_connection(payload.kind, clean_settings(payload.kind, payload.settings, previous))}
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        return {"ok": False, "message": str(exc)}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources")
def add_source(project_id: str, payload: SourceForm, request: Request) -> dict:
    project_row(project_id)
    try:
        settings = clean_settings(payload.kind, payload.settings)
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        raise _fail(exc) from exc
    source_id = _create(project_id, payload.name.strip(), payload.kind, settings, request.state.user["name"])
    return _public(_source(project_id, source_id))


@router.put("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}")
def edit_source(project_id: str, source_id: str, payload: SourceForm) -> dict:
    row = _source(project_id, source_id)
    if payload.kind != row["kind"]:
        raise HTTPException(422, "A source's type can't change; add a new source instead")
    try:
        settings = clean_settings(row["kind"], payload.settings, json.loads(row["settings_json"]))
    except DataPulseError as exc:
        raise _fail(exc) from exc
    if row["kind"] == "file" and settings.get("file") != json.loads(row["settings_json"]).get("file"):
        remove_uploaded_files([row])  # a newer version replaced it
    _update(source_id, name=payload.name.strip(), settings_json=json.dumps(settings))
    return _public(_source(project_id, source_id))


@router.delete("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}")
def delete_source(project_id: str, source_id: str) -> dict:
    remove_uploaded_files([_source(project_id, source_id)])
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("DELETE FROM sources WHERE id = ?", (source_id,))
    return {"deleted": True}


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}/map")
def map_source(project_id: str, source_id: str) -> dict:
    """Propose a recipe that turns this source into the standard format. Saved as a proposal for review."""
    project, row = project_row(project_id), _source(project_id, source_id)
    if not project["baseline_json"]:
        raise HTTPException(409, "Set the standard format first: pull one source as the baseline")
    df = _fetch(row)
    proposal = ai_call(lambda: propose_mapping(df, json.loads(project["baseline_json"]),
                                               project_config(project["config_yaml"]),
                                               reference=dataset_rows(project["baseline_dataset_id"])))
    proposal["at"] = now_iso()
    _update(source_id, proposal_json=json.dumps(proposal))
    return proposal


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}/compare")
def compare_source(project_id: str, source_id: str, payload: Compare) -> dict:
    """Their columns next to the standard's, after the given steps (the proposal or saved recipe by default)."""
    project, row = project_row(project_id), _source(project_id, source_id)
    if not project["baseline_json"]:
        raise HTTPException(409, "Set the standard format first: pull one source as the baseline")
    if payload.operations is not None:
        try:
            recipe = validate_recipe(payload.operations)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(422, str(exc)) from exc
    else:
        proposal = loads(row["proposal_json"])
        recipe = proposal["operations"] if proposal else loads(row["recipe_json"]) or []
    return compare_mapping(_fetch(row), json.loads(project["baseline_json"]), project_config(project["config_yaml"]),
                           recipe, reference=dataset_rows(project["baseline_dataset_id"]))


@router.delete("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}/proposal")
def discard_proposal(project_id: str, source_id: str) -> dict:
    _source(project_id, source_id)
    _update(source_id, proposal_json=None)
    return _public(_source(project_id, source_id))


@router.put("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}/recipe")
def save_recipe(project_id: str, source_id: str, payload: Recipe) -> dict:
    """Approve a recipe (usually the reviewed proposal). Every later pull replays it without AI."""
    _source(project_id, source_id)
    try:
        recipe = validate_recipe(payload.operations)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(422, str(exc)) from exc
    _update(source_id, recipe_json=json.dumps(recipe), proposal_json=None)
    return _public(_source(project_id, source_id))


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/sources/{source_id}/pull")
def pull_source(project_id: str, source_id: str, payload: Pull, request: Request) -> dict:
    """Pull the source now. As the baseline it defines the standard format; otherwise its recipe runs and the
    result is checked like an upload."""
    project, row = project_row(project_id), _source(project_id, source_id)
    if payload.role == "candidate" and not project["baseline_json"]:
        raise HTTPException(409, "Set the standard format first: pull one source as the baseline")
    df = _fetch(row)
    actor = request.state.user["name"]
    if payload.role == "baseline":
        return _make_standard(project, row, df)
    recipe = loads(row["recipe_json"])
    mapped, effects, problems = apply_recipe(df, json.loads(project["baseline_json"])["profile"], recipe or [])
    extra = {"source": {"id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "kind": row["kind"],
                        "type": type_of(row["kind"], json.loads(row["settings_json"])), "rows_pulled": len(df),
                        "mapped": recipe is not None or bool(row["is_standard"]), "steps": effects, "problems": problems}}
    doc = run_check(project, row["name"], _store(mapped), mapped, False, actor, extra)
    _update(source_id, last_pulled_at=now_iso(), last_run_id=doc["run_id"])
    return doc


@router.post("/projects/{project_id}/unify")
def unify(project_id: str, request: Request) -> dict:
    """Pull every mapped source, convert each to the standard format, combine them and check the result."""
    project = project_row(project_id)
    if not project["baseline_json"]:
        raise HTTPException(409, "Set the standard format first: pull one source as the baseline")
    profile = json.loads(project["baseline_json"])["profile"]
    with connect() as db:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM sources WHERE project_id = ? AND (recipe_json IS NOT NULL OR is_standard = 1) "
                          "ORDER BY created_at, rowid", (project_id,)).fetchall()
    if not rows:
        raise HTTPException(409, "No mapped sources yet. Map at least one source first")
    frames, report = {}, []
    for row in rows:
        try:
            df = fetch_source(row["kind"], json.loads(row["settings_json"]), row["name"])
        except DataPulseError as exc:
            report.append({"name": row["name"], "kind": row["kind"], "type": type_of(row["kind"], json.loads(row["settings_json"])), "rows": 0, "error": str(exc), "problems": []})
            continue
        mapped, _, problems = apply_recipe(df, profile, loads(row["recipe_json"]) or [])
        label = row["name"] if row["name"] not in frames else f"{row['name']} ({row['id']})"
        frames[label] = mapped
        _update(row["id"], last_pulled_at=now_iso())
        report.append({"name": row["name"], "kind": row["kind"], "type": type_of(row["kind"], json.loads(row["settings_json"])),
                       "rows": len(mapped), "error": None, "problems": problems})
    if not frames:
        raise HTTPException(502, "None of the sources could be read: "
                            + "; ".join(f"{r['name']}: {r['error']}" for r in report))
    combined = combine(frames, list(profile["columns"]))
    name = f"Unified data ({len(frames)} source{'s' if len(frames) != 1 else ''})"
    doc = run_check(project, name, _store(combined), combined, False, request.state.user["name"],
                    {"unified": {"sources": report}}, checked=combined.drop(columns=[SOURCE_COLUMN]), combined=True)
    doc["delivered"] = deliver(project_id, doc, combined)
    if doc["delivered"]:
        with connect() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE runs SET report_json = ? WHERE id = ?", (json.dumps(doc), doc["run_id"]))
    return doc

FILE [25/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\server\specs.py

"""Storage for unify specs: one current spec per project source, with history."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from datapulse.unifier import StoredSpec, UnifySpec, to_json_schema

from .db import connect, now_iso

SOURCE_LABELS = {"upload": "Dashboard uploads", "api": "API pushes"}


def latest_row(project_id: str, source_key: str) -> Optional[sqlite3.Row]:
    with connect() as db:
        return db.execute("SELECT * FROM unify_specs WHERE project_id = ? AND source_key = ? "
                          "ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1", (project_id, source_key)).fetchone()


def get_row(spec_id: Optional[str]) -> Optional[sqlite3.Row]:
    if not spec_id:
        return None
    with connect() as db:
        return db.execute("SELECT * FROM unify_specs WHERE id = ?", (spec_id,)).fetchone()


def stored(row: Optional[sqlite3.Row]) -> Optional[StoredSpec]:
    if row is None:
        return None
    return StoredSpec(spec=UnifySpec.from_dict(json.loads(row["spec_json"])), origin=row["origin"],
                      fingerprint=row["fingerprint"], stale=bool(row["stale"]))


def save(project_id: str, source_key: str, spec: UnifySpec, origin: str, fingerprint: str,
         paths: Optional[List[str]] = None, sample: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
         actor: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    spec_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("INSERT INTO unify_specs (id, project_id, source_key, mode, fingerprint, paths_json, sample_json, "
                   "spec_json, origin, stale, created_at, created_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)",
                   (spec_id, project_id, source_key, spec.mode, fingerprint,
                    json.dumps(paths) if paths is not None else None,
                    json.dumps(sample, default=str) if sample is not None else None,
                    json.dumps(spec.to_dict()), origin, now_iso(), actor))
    return spec_id


def mark_stale(spec_id: str) -> None:
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE unify_specs SET stale = 1 WHERE id = ?", (spec_id,))


def source_label(project_id: str, source_key: str) -> str:
    if source_key in SOURCE_LABELS:
        return SOURCE_LABELS[source_key]
    with connect() as db:
        row = db.execute("SELECT name FROM sources WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?",
                         (source_key, project_id)).fetchone()
    return row["name"] if row else source_key


def public(row: sqlite3.Row, project_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    spec = UnifySpec.from_dict(json.loads(row["spec_json"]))
    return {"id": row["id"], "source_key": row["source_key"], "source": source_label(project_id, row["source_key"]),
            "mode": row["mode"], "origin": row["origin"], "stale": bool(row["stale"]),
            "created_at": row["created_at"], "created_by": row["created_by"],
            "spec": spec.to_dict(), "json_schema": to_json_schema(spec),
            "can_regenerate_now": row["mode"] == "map" and bool(row["sample_json"])}


def current_for_project(project_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    with connect() as db:
        keys = [r["source_key"] for r in db.execute(
            "SELECT source_key, MAX(created_at) AS latest FROM unify_specs WHERE project_id = ? "
            "GROUP BY source_key ORDER BY latest DESC", (project_id,))]
    return [public(latest_row(project_id, k), project_id) for k in keys]

def make_df(n: int = 500, seed: int = 0) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rs = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "order_id": np.arange(1000, 1000 + n),
        "customer_id": [f"C{i:04d}" for i in rs.randint(0, 120, n)],
        "status": rs.choice(["pending", "shipped", "delivered", "cancelled"], n, p=[0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.1]),
        "amount": np.round(rs.lognormal(4, 0.5, n), 2),
        "quantity": rs.randint(1, 6, n),
        "country": rs.choice(["US", "CA", "GB", "DE"], n),
    })

"""A scripted stand-in for the Anthropic client: returns queued responses and records every request."""
import copy
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace


def text(body: str, stop_reason: str = "end_turn"):
    return SimpleNamespace(content=[SimpleNamespace(type="text", text=body)], stop_reason=stop_reason)


def tool_calls(*calls):
    """calls: (name, input) pairs; returned in one assistant turn like parallel tool use."""
    blocks = [SimpleNamespace(type="tool_use", id=f"tu_{n}", name=name, input=args) for n, (name, args) in enumerate(calls)]
    return SimpleNamespace(content=blocks, stop_reason="tool_use")


class FakeClaude:
    def __init__(self, responses):
        self.responses = list(responses)
        self.calls = []
        self.beta = SimpleNamespace(messages=SimpleNamespace(create=self._create))

    def _create(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(copy.deepcopy(kwargs))  # the caller keeps appending to its message list
        return self.responses.pop(0)

    def sent(self) -> str:
        """Everything sent to the API, as text, for privacy assertions."""
        return json.dumps(self.calls, default=lambda o: getattr(o, "__dict__", str(o)))

from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def test_action_yml_is_a_valid_composite_action():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    assert action["runs"]["using"] == "composite"
    assert action["inputs"]["data-file"]["required"] is True
    steps = {s.get("id") or s.get("name"): s for s in action["runs"]["steps"]}
    assert "check" in steps
    text = (ROOT / "action.yml").read_text()
    assert "--baseline-ref" in text and "datapulse comment" in text and "github.token" in text
    assert "pull-requests" not in text.split("runs:")[1]  # permissions are the caller's job


def test_example_workflow_uses_declared_inputs_only():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    wf = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "examples/workflows/datapulse.yml").read_text())
    used = wf["jobs"]["data-quality"]["steps"][1]["with"]
    assert set(used) <= set(action["inputs"])
    assert wf["permissions"]["pull-requests"] == "write"
    assert "pull_request" in wf[True]  # YAML parses the bare key `on` as boolean True

FILE [30/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\tests\test_ai_and_github.py

import json

import numpy as np
import pytest

from datapulse import ai, github
from datapulse.checks import run_checks
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.profile import profile_dataframe
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text

SECRET = "ZZ-SECRET-VALUE-123"


def report_with_secret(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur.loc[:20, "status"] = SECRET
    cur.loc[cur.sample(frac=0.5, random_state=0).index, "customer_id"] = np.nan
    return run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)


def test_ai_payload_has_aggregates_only(baseline, cfg, df):
    report = report_with_secret(baseline, cfg, df)
    assert any(SECRET in e for i in report.issues for e in i.examples)  # visible locally...
    payload = json.dumps(ai.build_explain_payload(report))
    assert SECRET not in payload  # ...but never sent
    assert "ZZ-SECRET" not in payload and "C0001" not in payload and '"examples"' not in payload
    suggest_payload = json.dumps(ai.build_suggest_payload(profile_dataframe(df)))
    assert "delivered" not in suggest_payload and "category_count" in suggest_payload


def test_without_api_key_deterministic_explanations_remain(baseline, cfg, df, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, raising=False)
    report = report_with_secret(baseline, cfg, df)
    note = ai.explain_issues(report, cfg)
    assert "not set" in note
    assert all(i.explanation and i.ai_explanation is None for i in report.issues)


def test_ai_explanations_are_attached_and_labelled(baseline, cfg, df, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, "k")
    fake = FakeClaude([text(json.dumps({"explanations": [{"index": 0, "text": "Likely a failed join."}]}))])
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai, "get_client", lambda cfg: fake)
    report = report_with_secret(baseline, cfg, df)
    assert "AI explanations added" in ai.explain_issues(report, cfg)
    assert report.issues[0].ai_explanation == "Likely a failed join."
    assert SECRET not in fake.sent() and fake.calls[0]["fallbacks"] == "default"
    from datapulse.report import render_markdown
    assert "AI-generated explanation" in render_markdown(report)


def test_ai_failure_falls_back_gracefully(baseline, cfg, df, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, "k")
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai, "get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([text("not json")]))
    report = report_with_secret(baseline, cfg, df)
    assert "unavailable" in ai.explain_issues(report, cfg)
    assert all(i.ai_explanation is None for i in report.issues)


def test_pr_comment_is_created_then_updated(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    store = []

    def fake_request(method, url, token, payload=None):
        calls.append((method, url))
        if method == "GET":
            return list(store)
        if method == "POST":
            store.append({"id": 7, "body": payload["body"]})
        else:
            store[0]["body"] = payload["body"]
        return {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(github, "_request", fake_request)
    assert github.upsert_pr_comment("first", "o/r", 3, "tok") == "created"
    assert github.upsert_pr_comment("second", "o/r", 3, "tok") == "updated"
    assert len(store) == 1 and "second" in store[0]["body"] and github.MARKER in store[0]["body"]
    assert calls[-1][0] == "PATCH" and calls[-1][1].endswith("/issues/comments/7")


def test_comment_command_uses_event_context(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    from datapulse.cli import app
    report = tmp_path / "r.md"
    report.write_text("hello")
    event = tmp_path / "event.json"
    event.write_text(json.dumps({"pull_request": {"number": 42}}))
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_TOKEN", "t")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "o/r")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_EVENT_PATH", str(event))
    got = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.cli.upsert_pr_comment",
                        lambda body, repo, pr, token, api: got.update(repo=repo, pr=pr, token=token) or "created")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["comment", str(report)])
    assert r.exit_code == 0 and got == {"repo": "o/r", "pr": 42, "token": "t"}
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_TOKEN")
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["comment", str(report)]).exit_code == 2


def test_workspace_header_is_sent_only_when_configured(cfg, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, "k")
    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE_ID", raising=False)
    assert "anthropic-workspace-id" not in ai.get_client(cfg).default_headers
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE_ID", "wrkspc_123")
    assert ai.get_client(cfg).default_headers["anthropic-workspace-id"] == "wrkspc_123"


def test_pasted_key_whitespace_is_trimmed(cfg, monkeypatch):
    """A key pasted with a trailing newline used to fail every request as "Connection error"."""
    monkeypatch.setenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, "  sk-ant-test\r\n")
    assert ai.get_api_key(cfg) == "sk-ant-test"
    assert ai.get_client(cfg).api_key == "sk-ant-test"
    monkeypatch.setenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, " \n")
    assert ai.get_api_key(cfg) is None  # whitespace only counts as "not set"

"""The learned drift check, the AI triage agent and the plain-English rules assistant."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse import ai
from datapulse.agent import TriageContext, run_triage
from datapulse.checks import run_checks
from datapulse.cli import app
from datapulse.config import parse_config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.ml import drift_model
from datapulse.profile import profile_dataframe
from datapulse.report import render_json, render_markdown
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text, tool_calls

SECRET = "ZZ-SECRET-VALUE-123"
TRIAGE = {
    "headline": "Amount looks like it switched from dollars to cents.", "verdict": "block",
    "verdict_reason": "Revenue dashboards would be 100x too high.",
    "root_causes": [{"hypothesis": "Unit change upstream", "confidence": "high", "evidence": "mean x100"}],
    "actions": [{"step": "Ask the exporter whether amount is now in cents", "owner": "data producer"}],
    "notify": [{"who": "churn_model (ds)", "why": "reads amount"}],
    "message": "Heads up: today's orders batch is blocked.",
}


# ---------- ML drift ----------

def test_ml_drift_flags_a_shift_and_names_the_column(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = make_df(seed=1)
    cur["amount"] = cur["amount"] * 100
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg, reference=df)
    issue = next(i for i in report.issues if i.check == "ml_drift")
    assert issue.measured["drift_auc"] > 0.9 and issue.columns[0] == "amount"
    assert "order_id (identifier)" in issue.measured["columns_not_modelled"]  # sequential ids never count
    assert "churn_model" in {c["name"] for c in issue.consumers}  # drivers map to downstream consumers


def test_ml_drift_is_quiet_on_same_distribution_and_off_without_reference(baseline, cfg, df):
    report = run_checks(baseline, make_df(seed=7), cfg, reference=df)
    assert "ml_drift" in report.checks_run and not [i for i in report.issues if i.check == "ml_drift"]
    assert "ml_drift" not in run_checks(baseline, df, cfg).checks_run


def test_ml_drift_catches_a_change_per_column_checks_cannot_see():
    rs = np.random.RandomState(0)
    q = rs.randint(1, 6, 2000)
    base = pd.DataFrame({"quantity": q, "amount": q * 20 + rs.normal(0, 3, 2000)})
    cur = base.assign(amount=rs.permutation(base["amount"].to_numpy()))  # same values, broken relationship
    assert drift_model(base, cur)["auc"] > 0.75


def test_ml_drift_skips_timestamps_and_small_files():
    rs = np.random.RandomState(0)
    mk = lambda start: pd.DataFrame({"ts": pd.date_range(start, periods=300, freq="h").astype(str),
                                     "v": rs.normal(0, 1, 300)})
    res = drift_model(mk("2026-01-01"), mk("2026-06-01"))
    assert res["skipped"] == {"ts": "timestamp"} and res["auc"] < 0.7
    assert drift_model(mk("2026-01-01").head(10), mk("2026-01-01").head(10))["auc"] is None


def test_ml_drift_config_is_validated():
    assert parse_config({"checks": {"ml_drift": {"max_auc": 0.8, "exclude": ["id"]}}}).ml_drift.max_auc == 0.8
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="ml_drift.max_auc"):
        parse_config({"checks": {"ml_drift": {"max_auc": 0.3}}})


# ---------- triage agent ----------

def _ctx(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur.loc[:20, "status"] = SECRET
    cur["amount"] = cur["amount"] * 100
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg, reference=df)
    assert any(SECRET in e for i in report.issues for e in i.examples)  # present locally...
    return TriageContext(json.loads(render_json(report)), baseline["profile"], profile_dataframe(cur),
                         history=[{"when": "yesterday", "status": "passed"}],
                         consumers=[vars(c) for c in cfg.consumers])


def test_agent_investigates_with_tools_then_returns_structured_triage(baseline, cfg, df):
    fake = FakeClaude([
        tool_calls(("list_issues", {}), ("get_run_history", {})),
        tool_calls(("get_column_stats", {"column": "amount"}), ("get_consumers", {})),
        text(json.dumps(TRIAGE)),
    ])
    out = run_triage(_ctx(baseline, cfg, df), cfg, client=fake)
    assert out["verdict"] == "block" and [s["tool"] for s in out["steps"]] == [
        "list_issues", "get_run_history", "get_column_stats", "get_consumers"]
    sent = fake.sent()
    assert SECRET not in sent  # ...but never sent: tool results carry aggregates only
    assert "p50" in sent and "churn_model" in sent  # it did see real statistics and consumers
    last = fake.calls[-1]["messages"]
    assert [m["role"] for m in last] == ["user", "assistant", "user", "assistant", "user"]
    assert all(r["type"] == "tool_result" for r in last[-1]["content"])  # parallel results in one message
    assert fake.calls[0]["output_config"]["format"]["schema"]["required"][0] == "headline"


def test_agent_reports_unknown_tools_and_stops_runaway_loops(baseline, cfg, df):
    fake = FakeClaude([tool_calls(("delete_everything", {})), text(json.dumps(TRIAGE))])
    run_triage(_ctx(baseline, cfg, df), cfg, client=fake)
    assert fake.calls[1]["messages"][-1]["content"][0]["is_error"] is True
    looping = FakeClaude([tool_calls(("list_issues", {}))] * 3)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="did not finish"):
        run_triage(_ctx(baseline, cfg, df), cfg, client=looping, max_turns=3)


def test_agent_surfaces_refusals_and_missing_keys(baseline, cfg, df, monkeypatch):
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="declined"):
        run_triage(_ctx(baseline, cfg, df), cfg, client=FakeClaude([text("", stop_reason="refusal")]))
    monkeypatch.delenv(cfg.ai.api_key_env, raising=False)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
        run_triage(_ctx(baseline, cfg, df), cfg)


def test_triage_is_rendered_in_reports(baseline, cfg, df):
    report = run_checks(baseline, df.assign(amount=df["amount"] * 100), cfg)
    report.triage = {**TRIAGE, "steps": []}
    md = render_markdown(report)
    assert "AI triage" in md and "Block this batch" in md and "- [ ] Ask the exporter" in md
    assert json.loads(render_json(report))["triage"]["verdict"] == "block"


def test_cli_triage_flag(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    make_df().to_csv("good.csv", index=False)
    make_df().assign(amount=lambda d: d["amount"] * 100).to_csv("new.csv", index=False)
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "good.csv"]).exit_code == 0
    fake = FakeClaude([tool_calls(("list_issues", {})), text(json.dumps(TRIAGE))])
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.agent.get_client", lambda cfg: fake)
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "new.csv", "--reference", "good.csv", "--triage", "--json", "r.json"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output  # warnings only: triage never changes the exit code
    assert "agent looked at list_issues" in r.output and "Block this batch" in r.output
    assert json.loads(Path("r.json").read_text())["triage"]["headline"] == TRIAGE["headline"]
    assert "ml_drift" in Path("datapulse-report.md").read_text()


# ---------- plain-English rules ----------

def test_draft_rules_returns_a_validated_config_and_self_corrects(cfg, df):
    good = "version: 1\nchecks:\n  duplicates:\n    columns: [order_id]\n"
    fake = FakeClaude([
        text(json.dumps({"config_yaml": "checks:\n  nulls: {}\n", "summary": "x", "not_supported": []})),
        text(json.dumps({"config_yaml": good, "summary": "order_id must be unique",
                         "not_supported": ["an explicit list of allowed statuses"]})),
    ])
    out = ai.draft_rules("order_id must be unique; status in a fixed list", "version: 1\n", profile_dataframe(df),
                         cfg, client=fake)
    assert out["config_yaml"] == good and out["not_supported"]
    assert "unknown key" in fake.calls[1]["messages"][-1]["content"]  # validation error fed back
    assert "delivered" not in fake.sent()  # category values from the profile are never sent


def test_draft_rules_gives_up_after_repeated_invalid_drafts(cfg):
    bad = text(json.dumps({"config_yaml": "fail_on: sometimes\n", "summary": "", "not_supported": []}))
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="could not produce a valid"):
        ai.draft_rules("fail on everything", "version: 1\n", None, cfg, client=FakeClaude([bad, bad]))

"""DataPulse must work on repositories that have nothing to do with Python."""
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import yaml

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.cli import app

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def git(cwd, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", *args], cwd=cwd, check=True,
                   capture_output=True)


def test_works_in_node_go_and_java_style_repos_with_json_output(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    repo = tmp_path / "polyglot"
    (repo / "services/api/testdata").mkdir(parents=True)
    for name, body in {"package.json": '{"name":"x"}', "go.mod": "module x\n", "pom.xml": "<project/>",
                       "Cargo.toml": "[package]\nname='x'\n"}.items():
        (repo / name).write_text(body)  # no pyproject.toml / requirements.txt anywhere
    monkeypatch.chdir(repo / "services/api")
    data = Path("testdata/users.csv")
    make_df().to_csv(data, index=False)
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", str(data)]).exit_code == 0
    git(repo, "init", "-q"); git(repo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "main"); git(repo, "add", "."); git(repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "x")
    bad = make_df()
    bad["customer_id"] = np.nan
    bad.to_csv(data, index=False)
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", str(data), "--baseline-ref", "main", "--json", "out/report.json"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output
    doc = json.loads(Path("out/report.json").read_text())
    assert doc["schema_version"] == 1 and doc["status"] == "failed" and doc["exit_code"] == 1
    assert doc["counts"]["critical"] >= 1 and doc["rows"] == {"baseline": 500, "current": 500}
    issue = next(i for i in doc["issues"] if i["check"] == "null_rate")
    assert issue["columns"] == ["customer_id"] and "suggested_explanation" in issue
    assert set(issue) >= {"measured", "measured_summary", "consumers", "ai_explanation", "examples"}


def test_json_report_for_passing_run(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    make_df().to_csv("d.csv", index=False)
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "d.csv"])
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "d.csv", "--json", "r.json"]).exit_code == 0
    doc = json.loads(Path("r.json").read_text())
    assert doc["status"] == "passed" and doc["issues"] == [] and doc["exit_code"] == 0


def test_action_is_isolated_and_language_agnostic():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    text = (ROOT / "action.yml").read_text()
    assert "working-directory" in action["inputs"] and "setup-python" in action["inputs"]
    assert {"exit-code", "report-markdown", "report-json"} <= set(action["outputs"])
    install = next(s for s in action["runs"]["steps"] if "Install" in s.get("name", ""))
    assert "venv" in install["run"]  # never installs into the user's environment
    for forbidden in ("requirements.txt", "pyproject", "npm ", "pip install -r", "cache:"):
        assert forbidden not in text.replace("pip install --quiet", "")
    check = next(s for s in action["runs"]["steps"] if s.get("id") == "check")
    assert check["working-directory"] == "${{ inputs.working-directory }}"


def test_example_workflows_for_other_languages_use_declared_inputs():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    for name in ("node", "go", "java"):
        wf = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / f"examples/workflows/{name}.yml").read_text())
        uses = [s for j in wf["jobs"].values() for s in j["steps"] if str(s.get("uses", "")).startswith("your-org/datapulse")]
        assert uses, name
        assert set(uses[0]["with"]) <= set(action["inputs"]), name


def test_dockerfile_installs_git_and_has_entrypoint():
    text = (ROOT / "Dockerfile").read_text()
    assert "ENTRYPOINT [\"datapulse\"]" in text and "install -y --no-install-recommends git" in text
    assert "safe.directory" in text


def test_action_supports_a_python_free_binary_install():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    assert {"binary-url", "binary-sha256"} <= set(action["inputs"])
    setup = next(s for s in action["runs"]["steps"] if str(s.get("uses", "")).startswith("actions/setup-python"))
    assert "inputs.binary-url == ''" in setup["if"]  # no Python at all when a binary is used
    install = next(s for s in action["runs"]["steps"] if s.get("name") == "Install DataPulse")
    run = install["run"]
    assert "sha256" in run.lower() and "exit 1" in run and run.index("DP_BINARY_URL") < run.index("-m venv")


def test_release_workflow_builds_all_platforms_and_checksums():
    wf = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / ".github/workflows/release.yml").read_text())
    assets = {m["asset"] for m in wf["jobs"]["build"]["strategy"]["matrix"]["include"]}
    assert {"datapulse-linux-x64", "datapulse-macos-arm64", "datapulse-windows-x64.exe"} <= assets
    text = (ROOT / ".github/workflows/release.yml").read_text()
    assert ".sha256" in text and "scripts/build_binary.sh" in text

FILE [33/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\tests\test_checks.py

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.checks import run_checks


def by_check(report, prefix):
    return [i for i in report.issues if i.check.startswith(prefix)]


def test_passing_data_has_no_issues(baseline, cfg):
    fresh_sample = make_df(seed=1)  # same distribution, different rows
    report = run_checks(baseline, fresh_sample, cfg)
    assert report.issues == []
    assert not report.failed and report.exit_code == 0
    assert set(report.checks_run) == {"schema", "row_count", "null_rate", "categorical", "duplicates", "distribution"}
    assert report.passed_checks == report.checks_run


def test_schema_drift_missing_added_and_type_change(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.drop(columns=["country"])
    cur["coupon"] = "X"
    cur["quantity"] = cur["quantity"].astype(str) + " units"
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    kinds = {i.check: i for i in report.issues if i.check.startswith("schema")}
    assert kinds["schema.missing_column"].columns == ["country"]
    assert kinds["schema.missing_column"].severity == "critical"
    assert "coupon" in kinds["schema.missing_column"].explanation  # rename hint (same type)
    assert kinds["schema.added_column"].severity == "warning"
    tc = kinds["schema.type_change"]
    assert (tc.measured["baseline_type"], tc.measured["current_type"]) == ("integer", "string")
    assert report.failed and report.exit_code == 1


def test_consumer_impact_is_mapped_by_column(baseline, cfg, df):
    report = run_checks(baseline, df.drop(columns=["country"]), cfg)
    issue = by_check(report, "schema.missing_column")[0]
    assert [c["name"] for c in issue.consumers] == ["revenue_dashboard"]
    assert issue.consumers[0]["columns"] == ["country"]


def test_dataset_wide_issue_affects_all_consumers(baseline, cfg, df):
    report = run_checks(baseline, df.head(100), cfg)  # -80% rows
    issue = by_check(report, "row_count")[0]
    assert {c["name"] for c in issue.consumers} == {"revenue_dashboard", "churn_model"}
    assert all(c["dataset_wide"] for c in issue.consumers)


def test_null_spike_is_critical(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur.loc[cur.sample(frac=0.4, random_state=0).index, "customer_id"] = np.nan
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    issue = by_check(report, "null_rate")[0]
    assert issue.columns == ["customer_id"] and issue.severity == "critical"
    assert abs(issue.measured["current_null_rate"] - 0.4) < 0.01
    assert [c["name"] for c in issue.consumers] == ["churn_model"]
    assert report.exit_code == 1


def test_integer_column_with_a_few_nulls_is_not_a_type_change(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur.loc[:2, "quantity"] = np.nan  # pandas turns the column into float64
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    assert by_check(report, "schema.type_change") == []


def test_distribution_shift_detects_unit_change(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur["amount"] = cur["amount"] * 100
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    issue = by_check(report, "distribution")[0]
    assert issue.columns == ["amount"] and issue.severity == "warning"
    assert issue.measured["psi"] > 0.2 and issue.measured["mean_shift_std"] > 1
    assert "100x" in issue.explanation
    assert report.exit_code == 0  # warnings do not fail CI by default


def test_unexpected_categories_and_case_variants(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur.loc[:9, "status"] = "Shipped"  # case variant
    cur.loc[10:24, "status"] = "refunded"  # brand-new value
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    issue = by_check(report, "categorical")[0]
    assert issue.columns == ["status"]
    assert issue.measured["unexpected_rows"] == 25
    assert issue.measured["case_or_whitespace_variants"] == 1
    assert any("refunded" in e for e in issue.examples)


def test_duplicate_keys_detected(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = pd.concat([df, df.head(5)], ignore_index=True)
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    issue = by_check(report, "duplicates")[0]
    assert issue.measured["duplicate_rows"] == 5 and issue.columns == ["order_id"]


def test_row_count_threshold_is_configurable(baseline, cfg, df):
    smaller = df.head(300)  # -40%
    cfg.row_count.max_change_pct = 50
    assert not by_check(run_checks(baseline, smaller, cfg), "row_count")
    cfg.row_count.max_change_pct = 5
    assert by_check(run_checks(baseline, smaller, cfg), "row_count")


def test_checks_can_be_turned_off(baseline, cfg, df):
    cfg.null_rate.severity = "off"
    cur = df.copy()
    cur["customer_id"] = np.nan
    report = run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg)
    assert "null_rate" not in report.checks_run and not by_check(report, "null_rate")


def test_fail_on_warning_makes_warnings_fail(baseline, cfg, df):
    cur = df.copy()
    cur["amount"] = cur["amount"] * 100
    cfg.fail_on = "warning"
    assert run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg).exit_code == 1
    cfg.fail_on = "never"
    assert run_checks(baseline, cur, cfg).exit_code == 0


def test_duplicate_examples_handle_empty_keys(baseline, df, cfg):
    bad = df.copy()
    bad["order_id"] = None  # e.g. a source whose key column didn't map: every key is empty
    issue = next(i for i in run_checks(baseline, bad, cfg).issues if i.check == "duplicates")
    assert issue.measured["duplicate_rows"] == len(bad) - 1

"""Config-from-target-branch, PR impact section, and GitHub Action outputs."""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import yaml

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.cli import app
from datapulse.report import action_outputs

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
STRICT = """\
version: 1
checks:
  null_rate: {max_increase: 0.02}
consumers:
  churn_model: {type: model, columns: [customer_id, amount]}
  revenue_dashboard: {type: dashboard, columns: [amount, country]}
"""
LOOSE = STRICT.replace("max_increase: 0.02", "max_increase: 1.0").replace("severity", "severity")


def git(cwd, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", *args], cwd=cwd, check=True,
                   capture_output=True)


@pytest.fixture
def pr_repo(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    """main = strict config + baseline. 'pr' branch = loosened config + null-spiked data (a sneaky PR)."""
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", raising=False)
    (tmp_path / "datapulse.yml").write_text(STRICT)
    make_df().to_csv("data.csv", index=False)
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"]).exit_code == 0
    git(tmp_path, "init", "-q"); git(tmp_path, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "main")
    git(tmp_path, "add", "."); git(tmp_path, "commit", "-q", "-m", "base")
    git(tmp_path, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "pr")
    bad = make_df()
    bad.loc[bad.sample(frac=0.3, random_state=0).index, "customer_id"] = np.nan
    bad.to_csv("data.csv", index=False)
    (tmp_path / "datapulse.yml").write_text(LOOSE)
    return tmp_path


def test_pr_cannot_loosen_its_own_rules_when_config_comes_from_the_target_branch(pr_repo, runner):
    # The PR's own (loosened) config lets the null spike through...
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--baseline-ref", "main"]).exit_code == 0
    # ...but the trusted config on main does not.
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--baseline-ref", "main", "--config-ref", "main"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output
    assert "datapulse.yml @ main" in r.output
    assert "datapulse.yml @ main" in Path("datapulse-report.md").read_text()


def test_deleting_the_config_in_a_pr_does_not_reset_the_rules(pr_repo, runner):
    (pr_repo / "datapulse.yml").unlink()
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--baseline-ref", "main", "--config", "datapulse.yml",
                            "--config-ref", "main"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output


def test_missing_trusted_ref_is_an_error_not_a_silent_fallback(pr_repo, runner):
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--config-ref", "origin/does-not-exist"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "git fetch" in r.output and "Refusing" in r.output

def test_github_outputs_command_and_json_fields(pr_repo, runner):
    make_df().drop(columns=["country"]).to_csv("data.csv", index=False)
    runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--config-ref", "main", "--json", "r.json"])
    doc = json.loads(Path("r.json").read_text())
    assert doc["schema_changed"] is True and "revenue_dashboard" in doc["affected_consumers"]
    out = runner.invoke(app, ["github-outputs", "r.json"]).output.strip().splitlines()
    kv = dict(line.split("=", 1) for line in out)
    assert kv["status"] == "failed" and kv["schema-changed"] == "true" and int(kv["issue-count"]) >= 1
    assert "revenue_dashboard" in kv["affected-consumers"]
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["github-outputs", "nope.json"]).exit_code == 2


def test_action_outputs_cannot_inject_extra_output_lines():
    doc = {"status": "failed", "issues": [{"check": "null_rate", "consumers": [
        {"name": "evil\nstatus=passed\x00"}]}]}
    out = action_outputs(doc)
    assert "\n" not in "".join(out.values()) and "\x00" not in "".join(out.values())
    assert out["affected-consumers"] == "evil status=passed"
    assert out["schema-changed"] == "false" and out["issue-count"] == "1"


def test_action_declares_the_new_outputs_and_input():
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    assert {"status", "issue-count", "schema-changed", "affected-consumers"} <= set(action["outputs"])
    assert action["inputs"]["config-from-base"]["default"] == "true"


@pytest.mark.skipif(shutil.which("datapulse") is None or shutil.which("bash") is None,
                    reason="needs datapulse and bash on PATH")
def test_action_step_end_to_end_blocks_a_pr_that_loosens_its_rules(pr_repo, tmp_path):
    """Run the real shell from action.yml against a local origin, as GitHub would."""
    action = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "action.yml").read_text())
    script = next(s for s in action["runs"]["steps"] if s.get("id") == "check")["run"]
    (tmp_path.parent / "sim").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    origin = tmp_path.parent / "sim" / "origin.git"
    shutil.rmtree(origin, ignore_errors=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", "--bare", str(origin)], check=True)
    git(pr_repo, "remote", "add", "origin", str(origin))
    git(pr_repo, "push", "-q", "origin", "main")
    git(pr_repo, "add", "-A"); git(pr_repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "pr change")
    runner_tmp = tmp_path.parent / "sim" / "rt"
    runner_tmp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    out_file = runner_tmp / "out"
    out_file.write_text("")
    env = {**os.environ, "RUNNER_TEMP": str(runner_tmp), "GITHUB_OUTPUT": str(out_file),
           "DP_DATA_FILE": "data.csv", "DP_CONFIG": "datapulse.yml", "DP_BASELINE": "", "DP_BASELINE_REF": "",
           "DP_AI": "false", "DP_DISCOVER": "false", "DP_BASE_REF": "main", "DP_CONFIG_FROM_BASE": "true"}
    res = subprocess.run(["bash", "-c", script], cwd=pr_repo, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, res.stderr  # the step never aborts; the gate is a later step
    kv = dict(l.split("=", 1) for l in out_file.read_text().splitlines() if "=" in l)
    assert kv["exit-code"] == "1" and kv["status"] == "failed", res.stdout + res.stderr
    assert "churn_model" in kv["affected-consumers"] and kv["schema-changed"] == "false"
    assert Path(kv["report-json"]).exists() and Path(kv["report-markdown"]).exists()
    # opt-out: with the PR's own loosened config the same data would pass
    out_file.write_text("")
    env["DP_CONFIG_FROM_BASE"] = "false"
    subprocess.run(["bash", "-c", script], cwd=pr_repo, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert "exit-code=0" in out_file.read_text()

import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.cli import app

CONFIG = """\
version: 1
checks:
  duplicates:
    columns: [order_id]
consumers:
  churn_model:
    type: model
    columns: [customer_id, amount]
"""


@pytest.fixture
def project(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", raising=False)
    (tmp_path / "datapulse.yml").write_text(CONFIG)
    make_df().to_csv(tmp_path / "data.csv", index=False)
    return tmp_path


def test_baseline_then_check_passes(project, runner):
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output
    assert "Baseline v1" in r.output
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output
    assert "PASSED" in r.output
    md = (project / "datapulse-report.md").read_text()
    assert "all checks passed" in md and "<!-- datapulse-report -->" in md


def test_rebaselining_bumps_version(project, runner):
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    assert "Baseline v2" in r.output
    assert json.loads((project / ".datapulse/baseline.json").read_text())["baseline_version"] == 2


def test_ci_fails_with_exit_code_1_on_critical_and_writes_report(project, runner):
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    bad = make_df()
    bad.loc[bad.sample(frac=0.5, random_state=0).index, "customer_id"] = np.nan
    bad.to_csv(project / "data.csv", index=False)
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--markdown", "out/report.md"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output
    assert "FAILED" in r.output
    md = (project / "out/report.md").read_text()
    assert "Null rate rose in 'customer_id'" in md
    assert "churn_model" in md  # downstream consumer shown
    assert "suggested, not verified" in md  # facts and explanations are labelled separately


def test_warnings_do_not_fail_by_default_but_fail_when_configured(project, runner):
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    df = make_df()
    df["amount"] = df["amount"] * 100
    df.to_csv(project / "data.csv", index=False)
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv"]).exit_code == 0
    (project / "datapulse.yml").write_text(CONFIG + "fail_on: warning\n")
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv"]).exit_code == 1


def test_parquet_files_are_supported(project, runner):
    make_df().to_parquet(project / "data.parquet")
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.parquet"]).exit_code == 0
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.parquet"]).exit_code == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("args,needle", [
    (["check", "missing.csv"], "not found"),
    (["baseline", "notes.txt"], "Unsupported file type"),
    (["baseline", "empty.csv"], "empty"),
    (["baseline", "data.csv", "--config", "nope.yml"], "Config file not found"),
])
def test_invalid_inputs_exit_2_with_useful_error(project, runner, args, needle):
    (project / "notes.txt").write_text("hi")
    (project / "empty.csv").write_text("")
    if args[0] == "check":
        runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    r = runner.invoke(app, args)
    assert r.exit_code == 2
    assert needle in r.output

    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "datapulse baseline" in r.output


def test_corrupt_baseline_and_bad_config_are_reported(project, runner):
    (project / ".datapulse").mkdir()
    (project / ".datapulse/baseline.json").write_text("{not json")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "not valid JSON" in r.output
    (project / "datapulse.yml").write_text("checks:\n  null_rate:\n    max_increse: 0.1\n")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])

def git(cwd, *args):
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t", *args], cwd=cwd, check=True,
                   capture_output=True)


def test_baseline_is_read_from_target_branch_not_the_pr(project, runner):
    """A PR that 'fixes' CI by re-baselining its own bad data must still fail."""
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])
    git(project, "init", "-q")
    git(project, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "main")
    git(project, "add", ".")
    git(project, "commit", "-q", "-m", "base")
    git(project, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "pr")
    bad = make_df().drop(columns=["amount"])
    bad.to_csv(project / "data.csv", index=False)
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data.csv"])  # PR overwrites its working-tree baseline
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv"]).exit_code == 0  # compares against itself
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--baseline-ref", "main"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output
    assert "amount" in r.output and "@ main" in r.output


def test_baseline_ref_errors_are_actionable(project, runner):
    git(project, "init", "-q")
    git(project, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "main")
    git(project, "add", ".")
    git(project, "commit", "-q", "-m", "no baseline")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data.csv", "--baseline-ref", "main"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "Could not read" in r.output


def test_suggest_never_modifies_config_and_output_is_loadable(project, runner):
    before = (project / "datapulse.yml").read_text()
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["suggest", "data.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0 and "Nothing was applied" in r.output
    assert (project / "datapulse.yml").read_text() == before
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["suggest", "data.csv", "--output", "suggested.yml"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0
    from datapulse.config import load_config
    cfg = load_config(project / "suggested.yml")
    assert cfg.duplicates.columns == ["order_id"]
    assert "status" in cfg.categorical.columns
    assert (project / "datapulse.yml").read_text() == before
    # refuses to clobber existing files or the user's config
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["suggest", "data.csv", "--output", "suggested.yml"]).exit_code == 2
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["suggest", "data.csv", "--output", "datapulse.yml", "--force"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "Refusing" in r.output
    assert (project / "datapulse.yml").read_text() == before


def test_init_writes_a_valid_starter_config_once(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["init"]).exit_code == 0
    from datapulse.config import load_config
    assert load_config().fail_on == "critical"
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["init"]).exit_code == 2


def test_sample_files_demonstrate_pass_and_fail(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples"
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    cfgp = str(root / "datapulse.yml")
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", str(root / "data/orders.csv"), "-c", cfgp]).exit_code == 0
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", str(root / "data/orders.csv"), "-c", cfgp]).exit_code == 0
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", str(root / "data/orders_drifted.csv"), "-c", cfgp])
    assert r.exit_code == 1
    md = Path("datapulse-report.md").read_text()
    for needle in ("revenue_dashboard", "churn_model", "fulfillment_service", "country", "coupon_code"):
        assert needle in md

import pytest

from datapulse.config import load_config, parse_config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError


def test_defaults():
    cfg = parse_config({})
    assert cfg.fail_on == "critical" and cfg.null_rate.max_increase == 0.05 and cfg.consumers == []


def test_valid_config_values_are_parsed():
    cfg = parse_config({
        "fail_on": "warning",
        "checks": {"null_rate": {"max_increase": 0.1, "columns": {"id": 0}}, "row_count": {"min_rows": 10},
                   "schema": {"added_columns": "off"}},
        "consumers": {"m": {"type": "model", "columns": ["a"]}},
    })
    assert cfg.fail_on == "warning" and cfg.null_rate.columns == {"id": 0.0}
    assert cfg.row_count.min_rows == 10 and cfg.schema.added_columns == "off"
    assert cfg.consumers[0].name == "m"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw,needle", [
    ({"fail_on": "sometimes"}, "fail_on"),
    ({"checks": {"null_rate": {"severity": "fatal"}}}, "checks.null_rate.severity"),
    ({"checks": {"null_rate": {"max_increase": "lots"}}}, "must be a number"),
    ({"checks": {"null_rate": {"max_increase": 5}}}, "<= 1"),
    ({"checks": {"nulls": {}}}, "unknown key"),
    ({"consumers": {"x": {"type": "model"}}}, "columns"),
    ({"consumers": ["x"]}, "mapping"),
    ({"report": {"show_values": "yes"}}, "true or false"),
    ({"version": 2}, "version"),
])
def test_invalid_config_has_actionable_errors(raw, needle):
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError) as e:
        parse_config(raw)
    assert needle in str(e.value)


def test_yaml_syntax_error(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "datapulse.yml"
    p.write_text("a: [unclosed")
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="not valid YAML"):
        load_config(p)
