"""File links, Google Sheets/Drive, REST, and Excel. These sit beside main's database plug-ins."""
from __future__ import annotations

import socket

import pandas as pd
import pytest

pytest.importorskip("httpx")
pytest.importorskip("openpyxl")
import httpx  # noqa: E402

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.io import load_dataframe  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.file_link import FileLinkSource  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.google_drive import GoogleDriveSource, parse_drive_file_id  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.google_sheets import GoogleSheetsSource, parse_sheet_url  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.http import MAX_BYTES, validate_url  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.rest import RestSource, extract_records  # noqa: E402
import datapulse.sources.http as http_mod  # noqa: E402
from conftest import make_df  # noqa: E402


@pytest.fixture
def mock_http(monkeypatch):
    """Install an httpx MockTransport for the duration of a test."""
    def install(fn):
        monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_TRANSPORT", httpx.MockTransport(fn))
        monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_check_host", lambda host: None)

    yield install
    monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_TRANSPORT", None)


def test_parse_sheet_url_and_gid():
    sid, gid = parse_sheet_url("https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/abc123XYZ/edit#gid=42")
    assert sid == "abc123XYZ" and gid == "42"
    sid, gid = parse_sheet_url("https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/abc123XYZ/edit?usp=sharing")
    assert sid == "abc123XYZ" and gid == "0"


def test_parse_drive_file_id():
    assert parse_drive_file_id("https://drive.google.com/file/d/FILEID99/view") == "FILEID99"
    assert parse_drive_file_id("https://drive.google.com/open?id=FILEID99") == "FILEID99"


def test_ssrf_blocks_localhost_and_private(monkeypatch):
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="localhost"):
        validate_url("http://localhost/secret")

    def fake_getaddrinfo(host, *args, **kwargs):
        ip = {"evil.local": "10.0.0.5", "link.local": "169.254.1.1", "ok.example": "93.184.216.34"}[host]
        return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", (ip, 0))]

    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, "getaddrinfo", fake_getaddrinfo)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="private"):
        validate_url("http://evil.local/x")
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="private"):
        validate_url("http://link.local/x")
    assert validate_url("https://ok.example/data.csv").startswith("https://")


def test_ssrf_blocks_non_http():
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="http"):
        validate_url("file:///etc/passwd")


def test_extract_records_and_flatten():
    rows = extract_records({"data": {"items": [{"a": 1, "n": {"b": 2}}, {"a": 3, "n": {"b": 4}}]}}, "data.items")
    assert len(rows) == 2
    assert list(pd.json_normalize(rows, sep=".").columns) == ["a", "n.b"]


def test_rest_source_auto_detect(tmp_path, mock_http):
    payload = {"results": [{"order_id": 1, "amount": 9.5}, {"order_id": 2, "amount": 1.0}]}

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        assert request.url.path == "/api/orders"
        assert request.headers.get("authorization") == "Bearer secret"
        return httpx.Response(200, json=payload, headers={"content-type": "application/json"})

    mock_http(handler)
    path = RestSource().fetch(
        {"url": "https://api.example.com/api/orders"},
        {"headers": {"Authorization": "Bearer secret"}},
        tmp_path,
    )
    assert path.suffix == ".jsonl"
    df = load_dataframe(path)
    assert len(df) == 2 and list(df.columns) == ["order_id", "amount"]


def test_rest_source_records_path(tmp_path, mock_http):
    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"meta": {}, "data": {"items": [{"id": 1}, {"id": 2}]}})

    mock_http(handler)
    path = RestSource().fetch(
        {"url": "https://api.example.com/v1", "records_path": "data.items"},
        {},
        tmp_path,
    )
    assert len(load_dataframe(path)) == 2


def test_file_link_detects_csv(tmp_path, mock_http):
    csv = b"order_id,amount\n1,2.5\n2,3.0\n"

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, content=csv, headers={"content-type": "text/csv"})

    mock_http(handler)
    path = FileLinkSource().fetch({"url": "https://cdn.example.com/orders.csv"}, {}, tmp_path)
    assert path.suffix == ".csv"
    assert list(load_dataframe(path).columns) == ["order_id", "amount"]


def test_file_link_size_cap(tmp_path, mock_http):
    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, content=b"x" * (MAX_BYTES + 1), headers={"content-type": "text/csv"})

    mock_http(handler)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="MB limit"):
        FileLinkSource().fetch({"url": "https://cdn.example.com/big.csv"}, {}, tmp_path)


def test_google_sheets_export(tmp_path, mock_http):
    csv = b"a,b\n1,2\n"

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        assert "export" in str(request.url)
        assert "gid=7" in str(request.url)
        return httpx.Response(200, content=csv, headers={"content-type": "text/csv"})

    mock_http(handler)
    path = GoogleSheetsSource().fetch(
        {"url": "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/SHEETID/edit#gid=7"},
        {},
        tmp_path,
    )
    assert path.suffix == ".csv"
    assert len(load_dataframe(path)) == 1


def test_google_sheets_private_login_page(tmp_path, mock_http):
    html = b"<html><body>Sign in</body><a href='https://accounts.google.com/ServiceLogin'>login</a></html>"

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, content=html, headers={"content-type": "text/html"})

    mock_http(handler)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="publicly readable"):
        GoogleSheetsSource().fetch(
            {"url": "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/SHEETID/edit"}, {}, tmp_path
        )


def test_google_drive_confirm_token(tmp_path, mock_http):
    csv = b"x,y\n1,2\n"
    calls = {"n": 0}

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        calls["n"] += 1
        if "confirm=" not in str(request.url):
            page = b'<html><form>confirm=TOKEN99&amp;id=FILE</form></html>'
            return httpx.Response(200, content=page, headers={"content-type": "text/html"})
        return httpx.Response(
            200, content=csv,
            headers={"content-type": "text/csv", "content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="data.csv"'},
        )

    mock_http(handler)
    path = GoogleDriveSource().fetch(
        {"url": "https://drive.google.com/file/d/FILEID99/view"}, {}, tmp_path
    )
    assert calls["n"] == 2
    assert path.suffix == ".csv"
    assert len(load_dataframe(path)) == 1


def test_rest_plugin_returns_a_table(mock_http):
    from datapulse.sources.base import plugin

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        return httpx.Response(200, json=[{"id": 1, "name": "Ada"}], headers={"content-type": "application/json"})

    mock_http(handler)
    src = plugin("rest")
    df = src.fetch({"url": "https://api.example.com/users"}, 10)
    assert list(df.columns) == ["id", "name"] and len(df) == 1
    assert src.test({"url": "https://api.example.com/users"}).startswith("Reached the API")


def test_excel_loader(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "orders.xlsx"
    make_df(20).to_excel(path, index=False)
    df = load_dataframe(path)
    assert len(df) == 20 and "order_id" in df.columns

"""Destinations: unified data written into the company's own database, only when it passed its checks."""
import json
import os
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing

import pandas as pd
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine

from datapulse import destinations
from datapulse.sources import SourceError
from datapulse.sources.mongodb import MongoDB
from conftest import make_df

URL = "postgresql://ada:Hunter2-pw@db.example.com:5432/shop"


@pytest.fixture
def warehouse(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Stand in a SQLite file for the company's database (the URL stays a PostgreSQL one)."""
    path = tmp_path / "warehouse.db"
    monkeypatch.setattr(destinations, "_sql_engine", lambda url: create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}"))
    return path


def rows(path, table):
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
        return db.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}").fetchone()[0]


def test_replace_and_append(warehouse):
    df = make_df(50)
    settings = {"url": URL, "table": "unified_orders", "mode": "replace"}
    assert "doesn't exist yet" in destinations.describe_target(settings)
    assert destinations.send(settings, df) == "Replaced the contents of 'unified_orders' in PostgreSQL: 50 rows."
    destinations.send(settings, df.head(10))
    assert rows(warehouse, "unified_orders") == 10
    destinations.send({**settings, "mode": "append"}, df.head(5))
    assert rows(warehouse, "unified_orders") == 15
    assert "has 15 rows" in destinations.describe_target(settings)


def test_bad_settings_are_refused():
    for settings, needle in [({"url": URL, "table": "orders; DROP TABLE x", "mode": "replace"}, "Name one table"),
                             ({"url": URL, "table": "t", "mode": "merge"}, "replace or append"),
                             ({"url": "", "table": "t", "mode": "replace"}, "connection URL")]:
        with pytest.raises(SourceError, match=needle):
            destinations.send(settings, make_df(3))


def test_errors_never_show_the_password():
    with pytest.raises(SourceError) as err:
        destinations.send({"url": "postgresql://ada:Hunter2-pw@127.0.0.1:1/shop", "table": "t", "mode": "replace"}, make_df(3))
    assert str(err.value).startswith("PostgreSQL:") and "Hunter2" not in str(err.value)


def test_mongodb_destination(monkeypatch):
    mongomock = pytest.importorskip("mongomock")
    client = mongomock.MongoClient()
    monkeypatch.setattr(MongoDB, "client", lambda self, settings: client)
    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "close", lambda: None)
    settings = {"url": "mongodb+srv://ada:pw@cluster.mongodb.net/", "table": "shop.unified", "mode": "replace"}
    destinations.send(settings, make_df(20))
    destinations.send({**settings, "mode": "append"}, make_df(5))
    assert client.shop.unified.count_documents({}) == 25
    assert "has 25 documents" in destinations.describe_target(settings)


@pytest.mark.skipif(not os.getenv("DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"), reason="needs DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL")
def test_real_postgres_destination():
    url = os.environ["DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"]
    settings = {"url": url, "table": "public.dp_test_unified", "mode": "replace"}
    destinations.send(settings, make_df(40))
    destinations.send({**settings, "mode": "append"}, make_df(10))
    engine = create_engine(url.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+psycopg://"))
    assert len(pd.read_sql("SELECT * FROM public.dp_test_unified", engine)) == 50


# ---------- through the API: unify sends, failing data is held back ----------

pytest.importorskip("fastapi")
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402

from server import app as server  # noqa: E402


@pytest.fixture
def client(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATAPULSE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "home"))
    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", raising=False)
    with TestClient(server.app) as c:
        assert c.post("/api/auth/setup", json={"name": "Ada", "email": "a@x.io", "password": "password123"}).status_code == 200
        yield c


def test_unify_delivers_to_destinations(client, warehouse):
    pid = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()["id"]
    base = f"/api/projects/{pid}"
    form = {"name": "Our warehouse", "url": URL, "table": "unified_orders", "mode": "replace"}
    assert client.post(f"{base}/destinations", json={**form, "table": "bad name!"}).status_code == 400
    created = client.post(f"{base}/destinations", json=form).json()
    assert created["type"] == "PostgreSQL" and created["saved_secrets"] == ["url"]
    assert "Hunter2" not in json.dumps(client.get(f"{base}/destinations").json())
    assert client.post(f"{base}/destinations/test", json={"url": "", "table": "unified_orders",
                                                           "destination_id": created["id"]}).json()["ok"]

    unified = client.post(f"{base}/unify").json()  # only the standard source is mapped: 500 rows, passes
    assert unified["status"] == "passed"
    assert unified["delivered"][0]["ok"] and rows(warehouse, "unified_orders") == 500
    assert client.get(f"{base}/runs/{unified['run_id']}").json()["delivered"][0]["table"] == "unified_orders"
    assert client.get(f"{base}/destinations").json()[0]["last_result"].endswith("500 rows.")

    client.put(f"{base}/destinations/{created['id']}", json={**form, "url": "", "mode": "append"})
    again = client.post(f"{base}/runs/{unified['run_id']}/send").json()["delivered"][0]
    assert again["message"].startswith("Added the rows") and rows(warehouse, "unified_orders") == 1000


def test_failing_data_is_held_back(client, warehouse):
    pid = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()["id"]
    base = f"/api/projects/{pid}"
    client.post(f"{base}/destinations", json={"name": "W", "url": URL, "table": "unified_orders", "mode": "replace"})
    store = next(s for s in client.get(f"{base}/sources").json() if s["name"] == "Store system")
    client.put(f"{base}/sources/{store['id']}/recipe", json={"operations": []})  # mapped, but wrongly: nothing renamed
    unified = client.post(f"{base}/unify").json()
    assert unified["status"] == "failed" and unified["delivered"][0]["held"]
    assert not warehouse.exists() or rows(warehouse, "unified_orders") == 0

"""The AI data unifier, extract path: document readers, limits, and the extraction agent."""
import io
import json
import zipfile
from email.message import EmailMessage
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from datapulse.config import Config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.unifier import documents as docs_mod
from datapulse.unifier import extract as extract_mod
from datapulse.unifier import unify_files
from datapulse.unifier.documents import Document, expand_zip, html_to_text, read_documents
from datapulse.unifier.extract import content_blocks, extract_all, propose_extraction_schema, records_schema
from datapulse.unifier.spec import UnifySpec
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text


def make_pdf(line: str) -> bytes:
    """A minimal one-page PDF with real text, so pypdf can extract it."""
    stream = f"BT /F1 12 Tf 72 720 Td ({line}) Tj ET".encode()
    objects = [
        b"<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>",
        b"<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>",
        b"<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 612 792] /Contents 4 0 R "
        b"/Resources << /Font << /F1 5 0 R >> >> >>",
        b"<< /Length %d >>\nstream\n" % len(stream) + stream + b"\nendstream",
        b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>",
    ]
    out = io.BytesIO()
    out.write(b"%PDF-1.4\n")
    offsets = []
    for n, body in enumerate(objects, 1):
        offsets.append(out.tell())
        out.write(b"%d 0 obj\n" % n + body + b"\nendobj\n")
    xref = out.tell()
    out.write(b"xref\n0 %d\n0000000000 65535 f \n" % (len(objects) + 1))
    for off in offsets:
        out.write(b"%010d 00000 n \n" % off)
    out.write(b"trailer\n<< /Size %d /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n%d\n%%%%EOF\n" % (len(objects) + 1, xref))
    return out.getvalue()


def make_docx() -> bytes:
    import docx

    d = docx.Document()
    d.add_paragraph("Statement of work for Acme Corp")
    table = d.add_table(rows=2, cols=2)
    table.cell(0, 0).text, table.cell(0, 1).text = "Item", "Price"
    table.cell(1, 0).text, table.cell(1, 1).text = "Design", "1200"
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    d.save(buf)
    return buf.getvalue()


def make_eml() -> bytes:
    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"], msg["From"], msg["To"] = "Invoice INV-7", "billing@acme.com", "ap@us.com"
    msg.set_content("Please find the invoice attached. Total due: 1,250.00")
    msg.add_attachment(make_pdf("Invoice INV-7 total 1250"), maintype="application", subtype="pdf",
                       filename="inv-7.pdf")
    msg.add_attachment(b"MZ", maintype="application", subtype="octet-stream", filename="tool.exe")
    return msg.as_bytes()


HTML = b"""<html><head><title>Price list</title><style>.x{}</style></head>
<body><script>var secret = 1;</script><h1>Widgets</h1><table><tr><td>Bolt</td><td>0.25</td></tr></table></body></html>"""


def _write(tmp_path, name, data):
    p = tmp_path / name
    p.write_bytes(data if isinstance(data, bytes) else data.encode())
    return p, name


# ---------- readers ----------

def test_readers_cover_every_document_type(tmp_path):
    files = [_write(tmp_path, "a.pdf", make_pdf("Invoice 42 total 99.50")),
             _write(tmp_path, "b.docx", make_docx()),
             _write(tmp_path, "c.md", "# Notes\n\n- one"),
             _write(tmp_path, "d.txt", "plain text"),
             _write(tmp_path, "e.eml", make_eml()),
             _write(tmp_path, "f.html", HTML),
             _write(tmp_path, "g.png", b"\x89PNG fake")]
    docs, skipped = read_documents(files)
    by = {d.name: d for d in docs}
    assert "Invoice 42 total 99.50" in by["a.pdf"].text and by["a.pdf"].pages == 1 and by["a.pdf"].data
    assert "Acme Corp" in by["b.docx"].text and "Design\t1200" in by["b.docx"].text
    assert by["c.md"].kind == "text" and by["d.txt"].text == "plain text"
    assert by["e.eml"].text.startswith("Subject: Invoice INV-7") and "1,250.00" in by["e.eml"].text
    assert "INV-7 total 1250" in by["e.eml / inv-7.pdf"].text  # attachments become their own documents
    assert not any("tool.exe" in n for n in by)
    assert by["f.html"].text.startswith("Price list") and "Bolt\t0.25" in by["f.html"].text
    assert "secret" not in by["f.html"].text
    assert by["g.png"].kind == "image" and by["g.png"].media_type == "image/png" and skipped == []


def test_unreadable_documents_are_skipped_not_fatal(tmp_path):
    docs, skipped = read_documents([_write(tmp_path, "ok.txt", "hi"), _write(tmp_path, "bad.pdf", b"not a pdf")])
    assert [d.name for d in docs] == ["ok.txt"] and skipped[0]["name"] == "bad.pdf"
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="No readable documents"):
        read_documents([_write(tmp_path, "bad2.pdf", b"nope")])


def test_html_to_text_keeps_title_and_structure():
    title, body = html_to_text("<title>T</title><p>One</p><p>Two &amp; three</p>")
    assert title == "T" and body == "One\nTwo & three"


def test_document_limits(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    files = [_write(tmp_path, f"{i}.txt", "x") for i in range(docs_mod.MAX_DOCUMENTS + 3)]
    docs, skipped = read_documents(files)
    assert len(docs) == docs_mod.MAX_DOCUMENTS and len(skipped) == 3
    monkeypatch.setattr(docs_mod, "MAX_TEXT_CHARS", 10)
    doc = read_documents([_write(tmp_path, "long.txt", "y" * 50)])[0][0]
    assert doc.truncated and len(doc.text) == 10
    monkeypatch.setattr(docs_mod, "MAX_TOTAL_BYTES", 20)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="25 MB"):
        read_documents([_write(tmp_path, "big.txt", "z" * 30)])


def test_zip_is_flattened_filtered_and_bounded(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    archive = tmp_path / "bundle.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w") as z:
        z.writestr("docs/a.txt", "alpha")
        z.writestr("../../escape.md", "beta")
        z.writestr("__MACOSX/._a.txt", "junk")
        z.writestr("tool.exe", "MZ")
    dest = tmp_path / "out"
    dest.mkdir()
    out, skipped = expand_zip(archive, dest, "bundle.zip")
    assert [label for _, label in out] == ["bundle.zip / docs/a.txt", "bundle.zip / ../../escape.md"]
    assert all(p.parent == dest for p, _ in out)  # nothing written outside dest
    assert [s["name"] for s in skipped] == ["bundle.zip / tool.exe"]
    monkeypatch.setattr(docs_mod, "MAX_TOTAL_BYTES", 3)
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="unpacks to more than"):
        expand_zip(archive, dest, "bundle.zip")


def test_pdfs_and_images_go_to_the_ai_as_files():
    pdf = content_blocks(Document(name="a.pdf", kind="pdf", data=b"%PDF", media_type="application/pdf", pages=2))
    assert pdf[1]["type"] == "document" and pdf[1]["source"]["media_type"] == "application/pdf"
    img = content_blocks(Document(name="scan.jpg", kind="image", data=b"jpg", media_type="image/jpeg"))
    assert img[1]["type"] == "image" and img[1]["source"]["media_type"] == "image/jpeg"
    txt = content_blocks(Document(name="n.txt", kind="text", text="hello"))
    assert len(txt) == 1 and txt[0]["text"].endswith("hello")


# ---------- extraction agent ----------

INVOICE_SCHEMA = {"description": "Invoice line items", "record_mode": "many_per_document",
                  "record_description": "One invoice line", "fields": [
                      {"name": "invoice_number", "type": "string", "description": "Invoice id"},
                      {"name": "invoice_date", "type": "date", "description": "Date issued"},
                      {"name": "item", "type": "string", "description": "Line item"},
                      {"name": "amount", "type": "number", "description": "Line amount"}]}


def _lines(*rows):
    return text(json.dumps({"records": [dict(zip(["invoice_number", "invoice_date", "item", "amount", "_page",
                                                  "_confidence"], r)) for r in rows]}))


@pytest.fixture
def serial(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(extract_mod, "CONCURRENCY", 1)


def test_schema_proposal_and_many_records_per_document(serial):
    docs = [Document(name="a.txt", kind="text", text="INV-1 ..."), Document(name="b.txt", kind="text", text="INV-2")]
    fake = FakeClaude([text(json.dumps(INVOICE_SCHEMA)),
                       _lines(["INV-1", "2026-03-07", "Bolts", 12.5, 1, "high"],
                              ["INV-1", "2026-03-07", "Nuts", "1,000", 1, "medium"]),
                       _lines(["INV-2", "early March", "Washers", 3, None, "low"])])
    spec = propose_extraction_schema(docs, None, Config(), client=fake)
    assert spec.mode == "extract" and spec.record_mode == "many_per_document"
    rows, stats = extract_all(docs, spec, Config(), client=fake)
    assert rows[0] == {"invoice_number": "INV-1", "invoice_date": "2026-03-07", "item": "Bolts", "amount": 12.5,
                       "_document": "a.txt", "_page": 1, "_confidence": "high"}
    assert rows[1]["amount"] is None  # "1,000" is not a plain number; never silently fixed
    assert rows[2]["_document"] == "b.txt" and rows[2]["_page"] is None and rows[2]["invoice_date"] is None
    assert stats["parse_errors"] == {"amount": 1, "invoice_date": 1} and stats["low_confidence"] == 1
    schema = fake.calls[1]["output_config"]["format"]["schema"]["properties"]["records"]["items"]
    assert set(schema["required"]) == {"invoice_number", "invoice_date", "item", "amount", "_page", "_confidence"}


def test_one_per_document_keeps_one_record_and_reports_failures(serial):
    spec = UnifySpec.from_dict({"mode": "extract", "record_mode": "one_per_document", "fields": [
        {"name": "subject", "type": "string"}, {"name": "total", "type": "number"}]})
    docs = [Document(name=n, kind="text", text="x") for n in ("a.txt", "b.txt", "c.txt")]
    two = text(json.dumps({"records": [{"subject": "A", "total": 1, "_page": None, "_confidence": "high"},
                                       {"subject": "extra", "total": 2, "_page": None, "_confidence": "high"}]}))
    fake = FakeClaude([two, text("not json"), text(json.dumps({"records": []}))])
    rows, stats = extract_all(docs, spec, Config(), client=fake)
    assert [r["subject"] for r in rows] == ["A"]
    assert stats["failed_documents"][0]["name"] == "b.txt" and stats["documents_without_records"] == ["c.txt"]
    assert "exactly one record" in fake.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"][0]["text"]


def test_records_schema_rejects_extra_keys():
    spec = UnifySpec.from_dict({**INVOICE_SCHEMA, "mode": "extract"})
    item = records_schema(spec)["properties"]["records"]["items"]
    assert item["additionalProperties"] is False and item["properties"]["amount"]["type"] == ["number", "null"]


def test_without_a_key_each_document_becomes_one_record(tmp_path):
    files = [_write(tmp_path, "notes.md", "# Kickoff\n\nWe agreed on scope."), _write(tmp_path, "m.eml", make_eml())]
    out = unify_files(files, None, Config(), use_ai=False)
    assert out.mode == "extract" and out.origin == "heuristic"
    titles = {r["_document"]: r["title"] for r in out.rows}
    assert titles["notes.md"] == "# Kickoff" and titles["m.eml"] == "Invoice INV-7"
    assert out.rows[0]["word_count"] == 6 and "No AI key" in out.stats["note"]


def test_a_pdf_of_the_orders_table_is_the_orders_table(tmp_path):
    from datapulse.baseline import build_baseline
    from datapulse.checks import run_checks
    from datapulse.config import load_config
    from datapulse.io import load_dataframe
    from datapulse.sources.anyfile import frame_from_path
    from datapulse.sources.pdfdoc import table_pdf

    orders_csv = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples" / "data" / "orders.csv"
    orders = load_dataframe(orders_csv)
    pdf = tmp_path / "orders.pdf"
    pdf.write_bytes(table_pdf(orders))
    frame = frame_from_path(pdf, "orders.pdf")
    assert list(frame.columns) == list(orders.columns)
    assert len(frame) == len(orders)
    assert frame["order_id"].tolist() == orders["order_id"].tolist()
    assert frame["customer_id"].tolist() == orders["customer_id"].tolist()
    assert frame["status"].tolist() == orders["status"].tolist()
    assert frame["country"].tolist() == orders["country"].tolist()
    assert frame["amount"].tolist() == orders["amount"].tolist()

    cfg = load_config(orders_csv.parents[1] / "datapulse.yml")
    report = run_checks(build_baseline(orders, orders_csv, 1, cfg.max_categories), frame, cfg,
                        data_file="orders.pdf", reference=orders)
    assert not report.failed, [(i.severity, i.title) for i in report.issues]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ext", [".pdf", ".docx", ".txt", ".html"])
def test_a_long_table_document_keeps_every_row(tmp_path, ext):
    import pandas as pd

    from datapulse.io import load_dataframe
    from datapulse.sources.anyfile import frame_from_path
    from datapulse.sources.pdfdoc import table_pdf
    from datapulse.unifier.documents import MAX_TEXT_CHARS

    orders = load_dataframe(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples" / "data" / "orders.csv")
    long = pd.concat([orders] * 6, ignore_index=True)
    assert len(long.to_csv(index=False)) > MAX_TEXT_CHARS
    path = tmp_path / f"long{ext}"
    cells = [list(long.columns)] + long.astype(str).values.tolist()
    if ext == ".pdf":
        path.write_bytes(table_pdf(long))
    elif ext == ".docx":
        docx = pytest.importorskip("docx")
        doc = docx.Document()
        table = doc.add_table(rows=len(cells), cols=len(long.columns))
        for row, values in zip(table.rows, cells):
            for cell, value in zip(row.cells, values):
                cell.text = value
        doc.save(path)
    elif ext == ".txt":
        path.write_text(long.to_csv(index=False))
    else:
        rows = "".join("<tr>" + "".join(f"<td>{v}</td>" for v in values) + "</tr>" for values in cells)
        path.write_text(f"<html><head><title>Orders</title></head><body><table>{rows}</table></body></html>")
    frame = frame_from_path(path, path.name)
    assert len(frame) == len(long)
    assert frame.astype(str).equals(long.astype(str))


def test_documents_can_be_added_as_a_source(tmp_path):
    from datapulse.sources.anyfile import frame_from_bytes, frame_from_path
    from datapulse.sources.link import read_bytes

    pdf = tmp_path / "invoice.pdf"
    pdf.write_bytes(make_pdf("Invoice 42 total 99.50"))
    frame = frame_from_path(pdf)
    assert len(frame) == 1 and "Invoice 42 total 99.50" in frame.loc[0, "text"]

    docx = tmp_path / "sow.docx"
    docx.write_bytes(make_docx())
    frame = frame_from_path(docx)
    assert "Acme Corp" in frame.loc[0, "text"] and frame.loc[0, "_document"] == "sow.docx"

    eml = tmp_path / "mail.eml"
    eml.write_bytes(make_eml())
    frame = frame_from_path(eml)
    assert "Invoice INV-7" in set(frame["title"]) or "Invoice INV-7" in " ".join(frame["text"])
    assert any("inv-7.pdf" in name for name in frame["_document"])

    linked = read_bytes(make_pdf("Hello from a link"), "https://cdn.example.com/note.pdf", "application/pdf")
    assert "Hello from a link" in linked.loc[0, "text"]
    linked = frame_from_bytes(make_docx(), "brief.docx")
    assert "Acme Corp" in linked.loc[0, "text"]


def test_web_page_source_saves_html(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    httpx = pytest.importorskip("httpx")
    from datapulse.sources import http as http_mod
    from datapulse.sources.web_page import WebPageSource

    monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_TRANSPORT", httpx.MockTransport(
        lambda request: httpx.Response(200, content=HTML, headers={"content-type": "text/html"})))
    monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_check_host", lambda host: None)
    path = WebPageSource().fetch({"url": "https://shop.example/prices"}, {}, tmp_path)
    assert path.suffix == ".html" and b"Price list" in path.read_bytes() and b"Bolt" in path.read_bytes()

FILE [40/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\tests\test_fix.py
================================================================================

"""The AI repair agent: safe operations only, verified by re-running the checks, original never changed."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from datapulse.baseline import build_baseline
from datapulse.cli import app
from datapulse.config import load_config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.fix import RepairError, RepairSession, apply_operations, run_repair
from datapulse.io import load_dataframe
from datapulse.profile import chart_data, profile_dataframe
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text, tool_calls

EX = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples"
SUMMARY = {"summary": "Scaled amount back to dollars, fixed quantity, removed duplicates.",
           "not_fixed": [{"issue": "country is missing", "why": "a missing column can't be recreated"}],
           "warnings": []}


@pytest.fixture
def session():
    cfg = load_config(EX / "datapulse.yml")
    good, bad = load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders.csv"), load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders_drifted.csv")
    return RepairSession(bad, build_baseline(good, EX / "data/orders.csv", 1, cfg.max_categories), cfg, reference=good)


def repair(session, name, **args):
    return session.call(name, {**args, "reason": "test"})


def test_operations_fix_the_sample_drift_and_never_touch_the_original(session):
    original = session.original.copy()
    before = len(session.list_issues()["issues"])
    repair(session, "scale_column", column="amount", factor=0.01)
    repair(session, "convert_to_number", column="quantity")  # values like "5 units"
    result = repair(session, "remove_duplicates", columns=["order_id"])
    assert result["issues_remaining"]["total"] < before
    titles = [i["title"] for i in session.list_issues()["issues"]]
    assert not any("amount" in t or "quantity" in t or "Duplicate" in t for t in titles)
    assert "A model can tell the new data from the baseline" not in titles  # ML drift gone too
    assert abs(session.working["amount"].mean() - 61.31) < 5
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(session.original, original)


def test_bad_requests_are_rejected_with_a_reason(session):
    for name, args, needle in [
        ("scale_column", {"column": "nope", "factor": 2}, "no column"),
        ("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0}, "non-zero"),
        ("rename_column", {"column": "amount", "new_name": "status"}, "already exists"),
        ("fill_missing", {"column": "amount", "strategy": "median"}, "no empty values"),
        ("standardize_categories", {"column": "amount"}, "no list of known values"),
    ]:
        with pytest.raises(RepairError, match=needle):
            repair(session, name, **args)
    with pytest.raises(RepairError, match="nothing to undo"):
        session.call("undo_last", {})


def test_undo_and_replay_are_deterministic(session):
    repair(session, "scale_column", column="amount", factor=0.01)
    repair(session, "drop_column", column="coupon_code")
    assert "Undid drop_column" in session.call("undo_last", {})
    assert "coupon_code" in session.working.columns
    replayed, effects = apply_operations(session.original, session.baseline["profile"], session.operations)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(replayed, session.working)
    assert effects == ["Multiplied 380 values in 'amount' by 0.01."]


def test_standardize_and_fill_and_drop_rows():
    base = pd.DataFrame({"status": ["paid", "open"] * 50, "n": range(100)})
    cur = pd.DataFrame({"status": [" Paid", "OPEN", "paid", None] * 25, "n": [1.0, None, 3.0, 4.0] * 25})
    cfg = load_config(EX / "datapulse.yml")
    s = RepairSession(cur, {"profile": profile_dataframe(base)}, cfg)
    assert "Standardized 50 values" in repair(s, "standardize_categories", column="status")["effect"]
    assert set(s.working["status"].dropna()) == {"paid", "open"}
    assert "Filled 25 empty values" in repair(s, "fill_missing", column="n", strategy="median")["effect"]
    assert "Removed 25 rows" in repair(s, "drop_rows_missing", column="status")["effect"]


def test_agent_loop_repairs_then_rechecks_and_sends_no_values(session):
    session.original.loc[:5, "status"] = "ZZ-SECRET-VALUE"
    fake = FakeClaude([
        tool_calls(("list_issues", {}), ("get_column_stats", {"column": "amount"})),
        tool_calls(("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01, "reason": "cents"}),
                   ("convert_to_number", {"column": "quantity", "reason": "units text"})),
        tool_calls(("remove_duplicates", {"columns": ["order_id"], "reason": "dupes"}),
                   ("drop_column", {"column": "missing_col", "reason": "x"})),  # error is reported, not raised
        text(json.dumps(SUMMARY)),
    ])
    result = run_repair(session, client=fake)
    assert [o["operation"] for o in result["operations"]] == ["scale_column", "convert_to_number", "remove_duplicates"]
    assert result["before"]["total"] == 9 and result["after"]["total"] < result["before"]["total"]
    assert result["rows"] == {"before": 380, "after": 370}
    assert result["not_fixed"][0]["issue"] == "country is missing"
    assert len(result["fixed_frame"]) == 370
    errors = [r for r in fake.calls[3]["messages"][-1]["content"] if r["is_error"]]
    assert len(errors) == 1 and "no column" in errors[0]["content"]
    assert "ZZ-SECRET" not in fake.sent()  # tool results carry counts and statistics only


def test_agent_gives_up_after_too_many_steps(session):
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="did not finish"):
        run_repair(session, client=FakeClaude([tool_calls(("list_issues", {}))] * 3), max_turns=3)


def test_chart_data_uses_the_baselines_buckets():
    good, bad = load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders.csv"), load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders_drifted.csv")
    charts = chart_data(profile_dataframe(good), bad)
    hist = charts["amount"]["hist"]
    assert len(hist["baseline"]) == len(hist["current"]) == len(hist["edges"]) + 1
    assert hist["current"][-1] == 1.0  # every drifted amount lands past the baseline's top bucket
    assert charts["country"]["null_rate"][1] is None and charts["customer_id"]["null_rate"][1] == 0.35


def test_cli_fix_writes_a_new_file(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    cfg = str(EX / "datapulse.yml")
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", str(EX / "data/orders.csv"), "-c", cfg]).exit_code == 0
    fake = FakeClaude([tool_calls(("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01, "reason": "cents"})),
                       text(json.dumps(SUMMARY))])
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.fix.get_client", lambda c: fake)
    drifted = str(EX / "data/orders_drifted.csv")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["fix", drifted, "-c", cfg, "-o", "fixed.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output
    assert "Issues: " in r.output and "Multiplied 380 values" in r.output and "left for a person" in r.output
    assert pd.read_csv("fixed.csv")["amount"].mean() < 100
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["fix", drifted, "-c", cfg, "-o", drifted]).exit_code == 2  # never the original

"""DataPulse must recognize and search projects written in many languages."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.cli import app
from datapulse.config import load_config, parse_config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.scan import scan_repo

FILES = {
    "analytics/report.py": 'import pandas as pd\ndf = pd.read_csv("data/orders.csv")\ntotal = df["amount"].sum()\n',
    "notebooks/explore.ipynb": '{"cells":[{"source":["df = pd.read_csv(\\"data/orders.csv\\")\\n","df[\\"status\\"].value_counts()"]}]}',
    "web/src/dashboard.ts": 'const r = await fetch("/data/orders.csv");\nrows.map(row => row.customerId);\n',
    "web/src/legacy.js": "const f = fs.readFileSync('orders.csv');\nconsole.log(row.country);\n",
    "billing/Loader.java": 'var p = Paths.get("data/orders.csv");\nlong id = rec.getOrderId();\n',
    "svc/main.go": 'f, _ := os.Open("data/orders.csv")\nsum += rec.Amount\n',
    "cli/src/main.rs": 'let f = File::open("data/orders.csv")?;\nlet s = row.get("status");\n',
    "etl/load.rb": 'rows = CSV.read("data/orders.csv")\nq = row[:quantity]\n',
    "stats/model.R": 'df <- read.csv("data/orders.csv")\nmean(df$amount)\n',
    "dotnet/Orders.cs": 'var t = File.ReadAllText("data/orders.csv");\nvar c = row.CustomerId;\n',
    "php/report.php": "<?php $h = fopen('data/orders.csv', 'r');\n$s = $row['status'];\n",
    "sql/revenue.sql": "SELECT country, SUM(amount)\nFROM orders\nGROUP BY country;\n",
    # decoys: must NOT count as readers
    "unrelated/utils.py": 'def total(df):\n    return df["amount"].sum()\n',
    "node_modules/lib/x.js": 'read("orders.csv"); row.amount;\n',
}
MANIFEST_FILES = ["package.json", "go.mod", "Cargo.toml", "pom.xml", "Gemfile", "composer.json", "requirements.txt"]


@pytest.fixture
def repo(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    for rel, body in FILES.items():
        p = tmp_path / rel
        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        p.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    for name in MANIFEST_FILES:
        (tmp_path / name).write_text("x")
    (tmp_path / "data").mkdir()
    make_df().to_csv(tmp_path / "data/orders.csv", index=False)
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", raising=False)
    return tmp_path


COLUMNS = ["order_id", "customer_id", "status", "amount", "quantity", "country"]


def test_recognizes_ecosystems_and_languages(repo):
    r = scan_repo(repo, "data/orders.csv", COLUMNS)
    assert {"Node.js (JavaScript/TypeScript)", "Go", "Rust", "Java (Maven)", "Ruby", "PHP", "Python"} <= set(r.ecosystems)
    assert {"Python", "Jupyter Notebook", "TypeScript", "JavaScript", "Java", "Go", "Rust", "Ruby", "R", "C#",
            "PHP", "SQL"} <= set(r.languages)


def test_finds_readers_and_columns_in_every_language(repo):
    r = scan_repo(repo, "data/orders.csv", COLUMNS)
    by_file = {x.file: x for x in r.refs}
    expected = {
        "analytics/report.py": "amount", "notebooks/explore.ipynb": "status", "web/src/dashboard.ts": "customer_id",
        "web/src/legacy.js": "country", "billing/Loader.java": "order_id", "svc/main.go": "amount",
        "cli/src/main.rs": "status", "etl/load.rb": "quantity", "stats/model.R": "amount",
        "dotnet/Orders.cs": "customer_id", "php/report.php": "status", "sql/revenue.sql": "amount",
    }
    assert set(by_file) == set(expected)  # decoys excluded
    for f, col in expected.items():
        assert by_file[f].reads_dataset and col in by_file[f].columns, f
    assert by_file["sql/revenue.sql"].via == "table name"
    assert by_file["analytics/report.py"].columns["amount"] == [3]  # line numbers are 1-based


def test_scope_all_includes_non_readers_as_text_matches(repo):
    r = scan_repo(repo, "data/orders.csv", COLUMNS, scope="all")
    extra = next(x for x in r.refs if x.file == "unrelated/utils.py")
    assert not extra.reads_dataset and extra.via == "column text match" and "amount" in extra.columns
    assert not any(x.file.startswith("node_modules") for x in r.refs)


def test_scan_command_prints_and_writes_reviewable_consumers(repo, runner):
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["scan", "data/orders.csv"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output
    assert "Files that read orders.csv: 12" in r.output and "Nothing has been applied" in r.output
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["scan", "data/orders.csv", "-o", "consumers.yml"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0
    cfg = load_config(Path("consumers.yml"))
    names = {c.name: c for c in cfg.consumers}
    assert names["analytics/report.py"].type == "python-code" and names["dotnet/Orders.cs"].type == "csharp-code"
    assert "customer_id" in names["web/src/dashboard.ts"].columns
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["scan", "data/orders.csv", "-o", "consumers.yml"]).exit_code == 2  # no clobber
    (repo / "datapulse.yml").write_text("version: 1\n")
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["scan", "data/orders.csv", "-o", "datapulse.yml", "--force"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "Refusing" in r.output


def test_check_discover_attaches_labelled_code_references(repo, runner):
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data/orders.csv"]).exit_code == 0
    bad = make_df().drop(columns=["country"])
    bad["customer_id"] = None
    bad.to_csv("data/orders.csv", index=False)
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data/orders.csv", "--discover", "--json", "r.json"])
    assert r.exit_code == 1, r.output
    assert "Code discovery: scanned" in r.output and "heuristic" in r.output
    md = Path("datapulse-report.md").read_text()
    assert "detected by text search; heuristic, not verified" in md
    doc = json.loads(Path("r.json").read_text())
    issues = {(i["check"], tuple(i["columns"])): i for i in doc["issues"]}
    nulls = {x["file"] for x in issues[("null_rate", ("customer_id",))]["code_references"]}
    assert {"web/src/dashboard.ts", "dotnet/Orders.cs"} <= nulls and "analytics/report.py" not in nulls
    missing = {x["file"] for x in issues[("schema.missing_column", ("country",))]["code_references"]}
    assert {"web/src/legacy.js", "sql/revenue.sql"} <= missing  # found via the BASELINE's columns
    assert runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data/orders.csv"]).exit_code == 1


def test_discovery_is_off_by_default(repo, runner):
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", "data/orders.csv"])
    make_df().drop(columns=["country"]).to_csv("data/orders.csv", index=False)
    runner.invoke(app, ["check", "data/orders.csv", "--json", "r.json"])
    doc = json.loads(Path("r.json").read_text())
    assert all(i["code_references"] == [] for i in doc["issues"])


def test_discovery_config_is_validated():
    assert parse_config({"discovery": {"enabled": True, "scope": "all", "exclude": ["gen"]}}).discovery.scope == "all"
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="discovery.scope"):
        parse_config({"discovery": {"scope": "everything"}})


def test_bundled_polyglot_example_works_end_to_end(tmp_path, monkeypatch, runner):
    ex = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples"
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    cfg = str(ex / "datapulse.yml")
    runner.invoke(app, ["baseline", str(ex / "data/orders.csv"), "-c", cfg])
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["check", str(ex / "data/orders_drifted.csv"), "-c", cfg, "--discover",
                            "--scan-root", str(ex / "polyglot")])

    md = Path("datapulse-report.md").read_text()

        assert needle in md

"""The web API (server/app.py) uses the same engine and report format as the CLI."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("fastapi")
pytest.importorskip("httpx")
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402

from conftest import make_df  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from server import app as server  # noqa: E402

ADMIN = {"name": "Ada Admin", "email": "ada@example.com", "password": "correct horse"}

CONFIG = """\
version: 1
checks:
  duplicates:
    columns: [order_id]
consumers:
  churn_model:
    type: model
    columns: [customer_id, amount]
"""


@pytest.fixture
def client(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATAPULSE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "home"))
    with TestClient(server.app) as c:
        assert c.post("/api/auth/setup", json=ADMIN).status_code == 200  # signed in as the first (admin) user
        yield c


@pytest.fixture
def project(client):
    created = client.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Orders"}).json()
    assert client.put(f"/api/projects/{created['id']}/config", json={"config_yaml": CONFIG}).status_code == 200
    return created


def _csv(df) -> bytes:
    return df.to_csv(index=False).encode()


def test_upload_baseline_then_check_uses_cli_report_format(client, project):
    pid = project["id"]
    df = make_df()
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("orders.csv", _csv(df), "text/csv")})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["baseline"]["baseline_version"] == 1
    assert r.json()["baseline"]["rows"] == len(df)

    bad = df.copy()
    bad.loc[:49, "customer_id"] = None
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/runs", files={"file": ("orders.csv", _csv(bad), "text/csv")})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    doc = r.json()
    assert doc["status"] == "failed" and doc["schema_version"] == 1
    issue = next(i for i in doc["issues"] if i["check"] == "null_rate")
    assert issue["severity"] == "critical" and issue["columns"] == ["customer_id"]
    assert [c["name"] for c in issue["consumers"]] == ["churn_model"]
    assert doc["markdown"].startswith("<!-- datapulse-report -->")

    detail = client.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}").json()
    assert detail["runs"][0]["id"] == doc["run_id"]
    assert detail["consumers"][0]["name"] == "churn_model"
    assert "categories" not in json.dumps(detail["baseline"])  # summary only, no raw category values
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}/runs/{doc['run_id']}").json()["status"] == "failed"


def test_rebaselining_bumps_version(client, project):
    pid = project["id"]
    for expected in (1, 2):
        r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(make_df()), "text/csv")})
        assert r.json()["baseline"]["baseline_version"] == expected


def test_ingest_json_rows_requires_project_key(client, project):
    pid = project["id"]
    rows = json.loads(make_df(50).to_json(orient="records"))
    body = {"dataset_name": "orders", "role": "baseline", "rows": rows}
    assert client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/ingest", json=body).status_code == 401
    assert client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/ingest", json=body, headers={"X-DataPulse-Key": "dp_wrong"}).status_code == 401
    headers = {"X-DataPulse-Key": project["api_key"]}
    assert client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/ingest", json=body, headers=headers).status_code == 200
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/ingest", headers=headers,
                    json={"dataset_name": "orders", "rows": rows + [rows[0]]})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert any(i["check"] == "duplicates" for i in r.json()["issues"])

    assert "fail_on" in r.json()["detail"]


def test_bad_uploads_are_rejected_and_not_kept(client, project, tmp_path):
    pid = project["id"]
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("notes.txt", b"hi", "text/plain")})
    assert r.status_code == 415
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/runs", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(make_df()), "text/csv")})
    assert r.status_code == 409  # no baseline yet
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("o.json", b"{not json", "application/json")})
    assert r.status_code == 400
    assert "uploads" not in r.json()["detail"]  # storage paths are not leaked
    assert list((tmp_path / "home" / "uploads").iterdir()) == []

def test_delete_project_removes_its_data_but_not_shared_files(client, project, tmp_path):
    pid = project["id"]
    client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(make_df()), "text/csv")})
    client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/runs", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(make_df()), "text/csv")})
    seeded = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()  # reads examples/, which must survive
    assert client.delete(f"/api/projects/{pid}").json() == {"deleted": True}
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}").status_code == 404
    assert client.delete(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}").status_code == 200
    assert list((tmp_path / "home" / "uploads").iterdir()) == []
    assert not (tmp_path / "home" / "samples" / seeded["id"]).exists()  # its sample databases too
    assert (ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders.csv").exists()
    assert client.delete(f"/api/projects/{pid}").status_code == 404
    assert client.get("/api/projects").json() == []


def test_runs_in_the_same_second_are_listed_newest_first(client, project):
    pid = project["id"]
    df = make_df()
    client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/baseline", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(df), "text/csv")})
    ids = [client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/runs", files={"file": ("o.csv", _csv(df), "text/csv")}).json()["run_id"]
           for _ in range(3)]
    assert [r["id"] for r in client.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}").json()["runs"]] == ids[::-1]


def test_ml_drift_runs_automatically_and_triage_is_saved_with_the_run(client, monkeypatch):
    from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text, tool_calls
    seeded = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()
    drifted = (ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders_drifted.csv").read_bytes()
    doc = client.post(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs", files={"file": ("d.csv", drifted, "text/csv")}).json()
    ml = next(i for i in doc["issues"] if i["check"] == "ml_drift")
    assert ml["measured"]["drift_auc"] > 0.9 and ml["columns"][0] == "amount"
    url = f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}/triage"

    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert client.get("/api/health").json()["ai"] is False
    r = client.post(url)
    assert r.status_code == 503 and "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in r.json()["detail"]

    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    triage = {"headline": "h", "verdict": "investigate", "verdict_reason": "r", "root_causes": [],
              "actions": [], "notify": [], "message": "m"}
    fake = FakeClaude([tool_calls(("get_run_history", {})), text(json.dumps(triage))])
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.agent.get_client", lambda cfg: fake)
    assert client.post(url).json()["verdict"] == "investigate"
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}").json()["triage"]["headline"] == "h"
    assert "[]" in fake.calls[1]["messages"][-1]["content"][0]["content"]  # history: no earlier runs


def test_draft_rules_endpoint_validates_the_draft(client, project, monkeypatch):
    from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    draft = {"config_yaml": CONFIG + "fail_on: warning\n", "summary": "Fail on warnings", "not_supported": []}
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.ai.get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([text(json.dumps(draft))]))
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{project['id']}/rules/draft", json={"request": "fail on warnings", "config_yaml": CONFIG})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["config_yaml"].endswith("fail_on: warning\n")
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{project['id']}").json()["fail_on"] == "critical"  # nothing saved

def test_everything_but_health_signin_and_ingest_needs_a_session(client, project):
    client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    assert client.get("/api/projects").status_code == 401
    assert client.post("/api/demo/seed").status_code == 401
    assert client.get("/api/health").status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/auth/status").json() == {"setup_needed": False, "user": None}
    rows = json.loads(make_df(60).to_json(orient="records"))
    r = client.post(f"/api/projects/{project['id']}/ingest", headers={"X-DataPulse-Key": project["api_key"]},
                    json={"dataset_name": "orders", "role": "baseline", "rows": rows})
    assert r.status_code == 200  # machine-to-machine ingest keeps using the project key


def test_setup_runs_once_and_login_is_checked(client, tmp_path):
    assert client.post("/api/auth/setup", json={**ADMIN, "email": "eve@example.com"}).status_code == 409
    client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    assert client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": ADMIN["email"], "password": "wrong"}).status_code == 401
    assert client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": "nobody@example.com", "password": "x"}).status_code == 401
    r = client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": "ADA@example.com ", "password": ADMIN["password"]})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["role"] == "admin"
    assert client.get("/api/auth/status").json()["user"]["name"] == "Ada Admin"
    import sqlite3
    db = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "home" / "datapulse.sqlite3")
    stored = db.execute("SELECT password_hash FROM users").fetchone()[0]
    assert ADMIN["password"] not in stored and stored.startswith("scrypt$")


def test_admin_manages_the_team_and_members_cannot(client):
    member = {"name": "Mo Member", "email": "mo@example.com", "password": "member-pass"}
    created = client.post("/api/users", json=member).json()
    assert created["role"] == "member"
    assert client.post("/api/users", json=member).status_code == 409  # duplicate email
    assert client.post("/api/users", json={**member, "email": "x@y.z", "password": "short"}).status_code == 422
    client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    assert client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": member["email"], "password": member["password"]}).status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/users").status_code == 403
    assert client.post("/api/users", json={**member, "email": "z@y.z"}).status_code == 403
    assert client.get("/api/projects").status_code == 200  # but can use the dashboard
    assert client.put("/api/auth/password", json={"current_password": "nope", "new_password": "new-password"}).status_code == 403
    assert client.put("/api/auth/password", json={"current_password": "member-pass", "new_password": "new-password"}).status_code == 200
    client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": ADMIN["email"], "password": ADMIN["password"]})
    me = client.get("/api/auth/status").json()["user"]
    assert client.delete(f"/api/users/{me['id']}").status_code == 409
    assert client.delete(f"/api/users/{created['id']}").status_code == 200
    assert client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"email": member["email"], "password": "new-password"}).status_code == 401


def test_checks_record_who_ran_them_and_review_status_is_shared(client):
    seeded = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()
    drifted = (ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders_drifted.csv").read_bytes()
    doc = client.post(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs", files={"file": ("d.csv", drifted, "text/csv")}).json()
    run = client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}").json()["runs"][0]
    assert run["created_by"] == "Ada Admin" and run["review"]["status"] == "open"
    url = f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}/review"
    assert client.put(url, json={"status": "acknowledged"}).json()["by"] == "Ada Admin"
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}").json()["runs"][0]["review"]["status"] == "acknowledged"
    assert client.put(url, json={"status": "done"}).status_code == 422
    assert client.put(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/nope/review", json={"status": "resolved"}).status_code == 404


def test_older_databases_are_upgraded_in_place(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import sqlite3
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATAPULSE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "old"))
    (tmp_path / "old").mkdir()
    db = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "old" / "datapulse.sqlite3")
    db.execute("CREATE TABLE runs (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, project_id TEXT, dataset_id TEXT, status TEXT, "
               "issue_count INTEGER, report_json TEXT, created_at TEXT)")
    db.execute("INSERT INTO runs VALUES ('r1', 'p', 'd', 'failed', 1, '{}', '2026-01-01')")
    db.commit(); db.close()
    with TestClient(server.app):
        pass
    db = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "old" / "datapulse.sqlite3")
    assert db.execute("SELECT review_status, created_by FROM runs").fetchone() == ("open", None)


def test_repair_agent_endpoint_download_and_recheck(client, monkeypatch):
    from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text, tool_calls
    seeded = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()
    drifted = (ROOT / "examples" / "data" / "orders_drifted.csv").read_bytes()
    doc = client.post(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs", files={"file": ("orders_drifted.csv", drifted, "text/csv")}).json()
    assert doc["charts"]["amount"]["hist"]["current"][-1] == 1.0  # chart data is saved with every check
    base = f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}"
    assert client.get(f"{base}/fixed").status_code == 404  # nothing repaired yet

    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert client.post(f"{base}/fix").status_code == 503

    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    summary = {"summary": "s", "not_fixed": [], "warnings": []}
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.fix.get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([
        tool_calls(("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01, "reason": "cents"}),
                   ("convert_to_number", {"column": "quantity", "reason": "units"}),
                   ("remove_duplicates", {"columns": ["order_id"], "reason": "dupes"})),
        text(json.dumps(summary))]))
    fix = client.post(f"{base}/fix").json()
    assert fix["by"] == "Ada Admin" and fix["file_name"] == "orders_drifted-fixed.csv"
    assert fix["after"]["total"] < fix["before"]["total"] == 9
    assert client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}").json()["runs"][0]["report"]["fix"]["dataset_id"] == fix["dataset_id"]

    r = client.get(f"{base}/fixed")
    assert r.status_code == 200 and "orders_drifted-fixed.csv" in r.headers["content-disposition"]
    assert len(r.text.strip().splitlines()) == 371  # header + 370 rows (10 duplicates removed)

    recheck = client.post(f"{base}/fixed/check").json()
    assert recheck["data_file"] == "orders_drifted-fixed.csv" and len(recheck["issues"]) == fix["after"]["total"]
    runs = client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}").json()["runs"]
    assert [r["report"]["data_file"] for r in runs[:2]] == ["orders_drifted-fixed.csv", "orders_drifted.csv"]

"""Data-source plug-ins and the unify engine."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from datapulse import sources
from datapulse.baseline import build_baseline
from datapulse.config import load_config
from datapulse.io import load_dataframe
from datapulse.sources.link import download_url, read_bytes
from datapulse.sources.mongodb import MongoDB
from datapulse.sources.sql import select_sql
from datapulse.unify import SOURCE_COLUMN, apply_recipe, combine, name_matches, propose_mapping, validate_recipe
from conftest import shop_shape
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text, tool_calls

EX = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples"
GOOD = load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders.csv")


# ---------- the plug-in contract ----------

def test_catalog_lists_every_plugin_with_its_form():
    kinds = {c["kind"]: c for c in sources.catalog()}
    visible = [c["kind"] for c in sources.catalog() if not c["hidden"]]
    assert visible == ["database", "file", "google_drive", "link", "rest", "web_page"]
    assert kinds["file"]["has_sample"] and kinds["link"]["has_sample"] and kinds["rest"]["has_sample"]
    assert kinds["web_page"]["has_sample"] and not kinds["database"]["has_sample"]
    assert {"postgres", "mysql", "sqlserver", "sql", "sqlite", "mongodb", "link"} <= set(kinds)
    assert [c["group"] for c in sources.catalog()][0] == "Databases"
    url = next(f for f in kinds["postgres"]["fields"] if f["name"] == "url")
    assert url["secret"] and url["placeholder"].startswith("postgresql://")


def test_settings_validation_keeps_saved_secrets_and_never_shows_them():
    saved = sources.clean_settings("postgres", {"url": "postgresql://u:pw@h/db", "query": "orders"})
    edited = sources.clean_settings("postgres", {"url": "", "query": "sales"}, previous=saved)
    assert edited == {"url": "postgresql://u:pw@h/db", "query": "sales"}
    assert sources.public_settings("postgres", edited) == {"url": "", "query": "sales", "saved_secrets": ["url"]}
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="Connection URL is required"):
        sources.clean_settings("postgres", {"query": "orders"})
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="Unknown setting"):
        sources.clean_settings("postgres", {"url": "x", "query": "y", "host": "z"})


def test_connection_errors_never_contain_the_password():
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError) as err:
        sources.try_connection("postgres", {"url": "postgresql://ada:Sup3r-Secret@127.0.0.1:1/db", "query": "t"})
    assert "Sup3r-Secret" not in str(err.value) and str(err.value).startswith("PostgreSQL:")
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="start with postgresql://"):
        sources.try_connection("postgres", {"url": "mysql://a:b@h/db", "query": "t"})


def test_queries_are_select_only():
    quote = lambda s: f'"{s}"'  # noqa: E731
    assert select_sql("public.orders;", quote) == 'SELECT * FROM "public"."orders"'
    assert select_sql("  SELECT * FROM t WHERE x = 1 ", quote) == "SELECT * FROM t WHERE x = 1"
    for bad in ["DELETE FROM t", "SELECT 1; DROP TABLE t", "/* hi */ UPDATE t SET x = 1", "orders o; drop"]:
        with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError):
            select_sql(bad, quote)


def test_sqlite_source_reads_a_table_and_stays_in_its_folder(sqlite_source, tmp_path):
    assert "8 column(s)" in sources.try_connection("sqlite", sqlite_source)
    df = sources.fetch_source("sqlite", {**sqlite_source, "query": "SELECT * FROM store_orders WHERE Country = 'US'"})
    assert len(df) == (GOOD.country == "US").sum() and "amount_cents" in df.columns
    outside = tmp_path.parent / "elsewhere.db"
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="No SQLite file"):
        sources.fetch_source("sqlite", {"path": str(outside), "query": "t"})


def test_row_limit(sqlite_source, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATAPULSE_MAX_SOURCE_ROWS", "100")
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="more than 100 rows"):
        sources.fetch_source("sqlite", sqlite_source, "Shop")


def test_mongodb_documents_are_flattened(monkeypatch):
    mongomock = pytest.importorskip("mongomock")
    client = mongomock.MongoClient()
    client.shop.orders.insert_many([
        {"order_id": 1, "customer": {"id": "C1", "address": {"country": "US"}}, "items": [1, 2], "status": "paid"},
        {"order_id": 2, "customer": {"id": "C2", "address": {"country": "DE"}}, "items": [], "status": "test"},
    ])
    monkeypatch.setattr(MongoDB, "client", lambda self, settings: client)
    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "close", lambda: None)
    settings = {"url": "mongodb://x", "database": "shop", "collection": "orders", "filter": '{"status": {"$ne": "test"}}'}
    assert "about 2 documents" in sources.try_connection("mongodb", settings)
    df = sources.fetch_source("mongodb", settings)
    assert len(df) == 1 and df.loc[0, "customer.address.country"] == "US"
    assert isinstance(df.loc[0, "_id"], str) and df.loc[0, "items"] == "[1, 2]"
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="no collection 'nope'"):
        sources.try_connection("mongodb", {**settings, "collection": "nope"})


def test_link_source_handles_sheets_and_refuses_internal_addresses():
    sheet = "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/AbC-123/edit#gid=42"
    assert download_url(sheet) == "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/AbC-123/export?format=csv&gid=42"
    doc = "https://docs.google.com/document/d/DOCID99/edit"
    assert download_url(doc) == "https://docs.google.com/document/d/DOCID99/export?format=docx"
    assert len(read_bytes(b"a,b\n1,2\n", "https://x/data", "text/csv")) == 1
    assert read_bytes(b'[{"a": 1}]', "https://x/d.json", "")["a"].tolist() == [1]
    for url in ["https://127.0.0.1/x.csv", "https://169.254.169.254/latest", "http://example.com/x.csv"]:
        with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError):
            sources.fetch_source("link", {"url": url})


@pytest.mark.skipif(not os.getenv("DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"), reason="needs DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL")
def test_real_postgres():
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    url = os.environ["DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"]
    shop_shape(GOOD).to_sql("dp_test_orders", create_engine(url.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+psycopg://")),
                            if_exists="replace", index=False)
    assert len(sources.fetch_source("postgres", {"url": url, "query": "dp_test_orders"})) == len(GOOD)
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError, match="read-only"):
        sources.fetch_source("postgres", {"url": url, "query": "WITH d AS (DELETE FROM dp_test_orders RETURNING *) SELECT * FROM d"})


# ---------- unify ----------

@pytest.fixture
def standard():
    cfg = load_config(EX / "datapulse.yml")
    return cfg, build_baseline(GOOD, EX / "data/orders.csv", 1, cfg.max_categories)


def test_names_are_matched_without_ai():
    ops = name_matches(["OrderID", "Customer ID", "amount_cents", "status"], ["order_id", "customer_id", "amount", "status"])
    assert [(o["args"]["column"], o["args"]["new_name"]) for o in ops] == [("OrderID", "order_id"), ("Customer ID", "customer_id")]


def test_recipes_replay_and_report_steps_that_no_longer_apply(standard):
    _, baseline = standard
    recipe = validate_recipe([{"operation": "rename_column", "args": {"column": "amount_cents", "new_name": "amount"}},
                              {"operation": "scale_column", "args": {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01}}])
    df, effects, problems = apply_recipe(shop_shape(GOOD), baseline["profile"], recipe)
    assert abs(df["amount"].mean() - GOOD["amount"].mean()) < 0.01 and not problems and len(effects) == 2
    _, _, problems = apply_recipe(GOOD, baseline["profile"], recipe)  # the source changed shape
    assert problems[0].startswith("Step 1 (rename column) no longer applies")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_recipe([{"operation": "exec", "args": {}}])


def test_ai_mapping_uses_statistics_only(standard):
    cfg, baseline = standard
    shop = shop_shape(GOOD)
    shop.loc[:3, "STATUS"] = "ZZ-SECRET"
    summary = {"summary": "Mapped the shop's orders.", "not_fixed": [], "warnings": []}
    fake = FakeClaude([
        tool_calls(("get_column_stats", {"column": "amount_cents"}), ("get_column_stats", {"column": "amount"})),
        tool_calls(("rename_column", {"column": "amount_cents", "new_name": "amount", "reason": "same range x100"})),
        tool_calls(("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01, "reason": "cents"}),
                   ("standardize_categories", {"column": "status", "reason": "capitals"}),
                   ("drop_column", {"column": "warehouse", "reason": "not in the standard"}),
                   ("fill_missing", {"column": "status", "strategy": "most_common", "reason": "not allowed here"})),
        text(json.dumps(summary)),
    ])
    proposal = propose_mapping(shop, baseline, cfg, client=fake)
    names = [o["operation"] for o in proposal["operations"]]
    assert proposal["automatic_steps"] == 6 and names[:6] == ["rename_column"] * 6
    assert names[6:] == ["rename_column", "scale_column", "standardize_categories", "drop_column"]
    assert proposal["after"]["total"] < proposal["before"]["total"]
    assert '"amount_cents"' in fake.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"]  # both column lists are in the brief
    errors = [r for r in fake.calls[3]["messages"][-1]["content"] if r["is_error"]]
    assert "not available" in errors[0]["content"]  # repair-only tools are refused while mapping
    assert "ZZ-SECRET" not in fake.sent() and "C0102" not in fake.sent()


def test_compare_shows_each_standard_column_with_its_source_and_rule_results(standard):
    from datapulse.compare import compare_mapping

    cfg, baseline = standard
    shop = shop_shape(GOOD)
    recipe = name_matches(list(shop.columns), list(GOOD.columns)) + [
        {"operation": "rename_column", "args": {"column": "amount_cents", "new_name": "amount"}},
        {"operation": "scale_column", "args": {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01}},
        {"operation": "drop_column", "args": {"column": "warehouse"}},
        {"operation": "rename_column", "args": {"column": "gone", "new_name": "x"}},
    ]
    out = compare_mapping(shop, baseline, cfg, recipe)
    columns = {c["name"]: c for c in out["columns"]}
    assert list(columns) == list(GOOD.columns)
    assert columns["order_id"]["source"] == "OrderID" and columns["order_id"]["status"] == "renamed"
    assert columns["amount"]["status"] == "converted"
    assert [s["operation"] for s in columns["amount"]["steps"]] == ["rename_column", "scale_column"]
    assert columns["status"]["status"] == "flagged" and columns["status"]["issues"][0]["severity"] == "warning"
    assert columns["status"]["after"]["samples"] == shop["STATUS"].head(3).tolist()
    assert out["dropped"] == [{"name": "warehouse", "effect": "Removed the column 'warehouse'."}]
    assert out["problems"][0].startswith("Step 10 (rename column) no longer applies")
    assert out["after"]["total"] < out["before"]["total"]

    cfg.report.show_values = False
    hidden = compare_mapping(shop, baseline, cfg, [])
    assert all(c["status"] == "missing" for c in hidden["columns"]) and len(hidden["extra"]) == len(shop.columns)
    assert not any(e["after"]["samples"] for e in hidden["extra"]) and not hidden["show_values"]


def test_combine_tags_rows_with_their_source():
    out = combine({"A": GOOD.head(2), "B": GOOD.head(3).drop(columns=["country"])}, list(GOOD.columns))
    assert out[SOURCE_COLUMN].tolist() == ["A", "A", "B", "B", "B"] and out["country"].isna().sum() == 3


# ---------- one "Database" source for every database ----------

def test_database_type_is_detected_from_the_url():
    from datapulse.sources.database import detect, type_label
    cases = {"postgresql://u:p@db.supabase.co:5432/postgres": "postgres", "postgres://u:p@h/d": "postgres",
             "mysql+pymysql://u:p@h/d": "mysql", "mariadb://h/d": "mysql", "mssql://u:p@h/d": "sqlserver",
             "mongodb+srv://u:p@cluster.mongodb.net/": "mongodb", "snowflake://u:p@acct/db": "sql"}
    assert {url: detect(url) for url in cases} == cases
    assert type_label("mongodb://x") == "MongoDB" and type_label("snowflake://x") == "SQL database"
    for bad in ["just text", "sqlite:///etc/passwd"]:
        with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError):
            detect(bad)


def test_database_source_reads_mongodb_with_database_dot_collection(monkeypatch):
    mongomock = pytest.importorskip("mongomock")
    client = mongomock.MongoClient()
    client.shop.orders.insert_many([{"order_id": 1, "customer": {"country": "US"}}, {"order_id": 2, "customer": {"country": "DE"}}])
    monkeypatch.setattr(MongoDB, "client", lambda self, settings: client)
    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "close", lambda: None)
    settings = {"url": "mongodb://user:pw@host/", "query": "shop.orders"}
    assert sources.try_connection("database", settings).startswith("MongoDB: Connected")
    assert sources.fetch_source("database", settings)["customer.country"].tolist() == ["US", "DE"]
    assert sources.type_of("database", settings) == "MongoDB"


def test_database_errors_name_the_detected_type_and_hide_the_password():
    with pytest.raises(sources.SourceError) as err:
        sources.try_connection("database", {"url": "postgresql://ada:Sup3r-Secret@127.0.0.1:1/db", "query": "t"})

@pytest.mark.skipif(not os.getenv("DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"), reason="needs DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL")
def test_database_source_on_real_postgres():
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    url = os.environ["DATAPULSE_TEST_POSTGRES_URL"]
    shop_shape(GOOD).to_sql("dp_test_db_source", create_engine(url.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+psycopg://")),
                            if_exists="replace", index=False)
    assert sources.try_connection("database", {"url": url, "query": "dp_test_db_source"}).startswith("PostgreSQL: Connected")
    assert len(sources.fetch_source("database", {"url": url, "query": "dp_test_db_source"})) == len(GOOD)

"""The data-sources API (connect, map, pull, unify) and the merged "Investigate with AI" endpoint."""
import json
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("fastapi")
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402

from server import app as server  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.io import load_dataframe  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.unify import SOURCE_COLUMN  # noqa: E402

EX = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples"
GOOD = load_dataframe(EX / "data/orders.csv")


@pytest.fixture

def test_orders_pdf_passes_the_sample_rules(client):
    """A PDF of the orders table is the same data, so the sample project's rules accept it."""
    from datapulse.io import load_dataframe
    from datapulse.sources.pdfdoc import table_pdf

    orders = load_dataframe(EX / "data" / "orders.csv")
    project = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()
    pid = project["id"]
    uploaded = client.post(
        f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources/files",
        files={"file": ("orders.pdf", table_pdf(orders), "application/pdf")},
    )
    assert uploaded.status_code == 200, uploaded.text
    source = client.post(
        f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources",
        json={"name": "Orders PDF", "kind": "file", "settings": {"file": uploaded.json()["file"]}},
    ).json()
    checked = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources/{source['id']}/pull", json={"role": "candidate"})
    assert checked.status_code == 200, checked.text
    body = checked.json()
    assert body["status"] == "passed", [(i["severity"], i["title"]) for i in body["issues"]]
    assert body["rows"]["current"] == len(orders)
