def test_sources_flow_connect_map_pull_unify(client, sqlite_source, tmp_path):
    pid = client.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Sales"}).json()["id"]
    base = f"/api/projects/{pid}"
    standard_csv = tmp_path / "standard.db"
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(standard_csv)) as db, db:
        GOOD.to_sql("orders", db, index=False)
    standard = client.post(f"{base}/sources", json={"name": "Main DB", "kind": "sqlite",
                                                    "settings": {"path": str(standard_csv), "query": "orders"}}).json()
    shop = client.post(f"{base}/sources", json={"name": "Shop", "kind": "sqlite", "settings": sqlite_source}).json()
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/test", json={"kind": "sqlite", "settings": sqlite_source}).json()["ok"]
    bad = client.post(f"{base}/sources/test", json={"kind": "sqlite", "settings": {**sqlite_source, "query": "nope"}}).json()
    assert not bad["ok"] and "no such table" in bad["message"]

    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/map").status_code == 409  # no standard yet
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/{standard['id']}/pull", json={"role": "baseline"}).status_code == 200
    proposal = client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/map").json()  # AI is off: name matches only
    assert proposal["automatic_steps"] == 6 and "AI is off" in proposal["summary"]
    listed = {s["name"]: s for s in client.get(f"{base}/sources").json()}
    assert listed["Main DB"]["status"] == "standard" and listed["Shop"]["status"] == "needs_mapping"
    assert listed["Shop"]["proposal"]["automatic_steps"] == 6

    recipe = proposal["operations"] + [
        {"operation": "rename_column", "args": {"column": "amount_cents", "new_name": "amount"}, "reason": "r"},
        {"operation": "scale_column", "args": {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01}, "reason": "r"},
        {"operation": "standardize_categories", "args": {"column": "status"}, "reason": "r"},
        {"operation": "drop_column", "args": {"column": "warehouse"}, "reason": "r"}]

    names_only = {c["name"]: c for c in client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/compare", json={}).json()["columns"]}
    assert names_only["amount"]["status"] == "missing" and names_only["order_id"]["source"] == "OrderID"
    compared = client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/compare", json={"operations": recipe}).json()
    columns = {c["name"]: c for c in compared["columns"]}
    assert columns["amount"]["source"] == "amount_cents" and columns["amount"]["status"] == "converted"
    assert columns["amount"]["after"]["mean"] == pytest.approx(columns["amount"]["standard"]["mean"], rel=1e-3)
    assert compared["dropped"][0]["name"] == "warehouse" and compared["after"]["total"] < compared["before"]["total"]
    bad = client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/compare", json={"operations": [{"operation": "exec", "args": {}}]})
    assert bad.status_code == 422
    saved = client.put(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/recipe", json={"operations": recipe}).json()
    assert saved["status"] == "mapped" and saved["proposal"] is None

    pulled = client.post(f"{base}/sources/{shop['id']}/pull", json={}).json()
    assert pulled["status"] == "passed" and pulled["source"]["steps"][0] == "Renamed 'OrderID' to 'order_id'."

    unified = client.post(f"{base}/unify").json()
    assert unified["data_file"] == "Unified data (2 sources)"
    assert [s["rows"] for s in unified["unified"]["sources"]] == [len(GOOD), len(GOOD)]
    assert not any(SOURCE_COLUMN in i["columns"] for i in unified["issues"])  # the tag isn't checked
    assert unified["status"] == "passed" and not unified["issues"]  # 2x the rows is expected for a combined table
    data = client.get(f"{base}/runs/{unified['run_id']}/data")
    assert data.text.splitlines()[0].startswith(f"{SOURCE_COLUMN},order_id") and len(data.text.splitlines()) == 1 + 2 * len(GOOD)

    assert client.delete(base).status_code == 200
    assert client.get(f"{base}/sources").status_code == 404


def test_secrets_stay_on_the_server(client):
    pid = client.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Prod data"}).json()["id"]
    form = {"name": "Prod", "kind": "postgres", "settings": {"url": "postgresql://u:Hunter2-pw@db/x", "query": "t"}}
    created = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources", json=form).json()
    edited = client.put(f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources/{created['id']}",
                        json={**form, "settings": {"url": "", "query": "other"}}).json()
    assert edited["settings"] == {"url": "", "query": "other", "saved_secrets": ["url"]}
    assert "Hunter2" not in json.dumps(client.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources").json())
    tried = client.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/sources/test",
                        json={"kind": "postgres", "settings": {"url": "", "query": "t"}, "source_id": created["id"]}).json()
    assert not tried["ok"] and "Hunter2" not in tried["message"]  # used the saved URL, didn't echo it


def test_investigate_runs_triage_and_repair_together(client, monkeypatch):
    seeded = client.post("/api/demo/seed").json()
    drifted = (EX / "data/orders_drifted.csv").read_bytes()
    doc = client.post(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs", files={"file": ("d.csv", drifted, "text/csv")}).json()
    url = f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}/investigate"
    assert client.post(url).status_code == 503  # AI off

    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "k")
    triage = {"headline": "Amounts are in cents", "verdict": "block", "verdict_reason": "r", "root_causes": [],
              "actions": [], "notify": [], "message": "m"}
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.agent.get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([text(json.dumps(triage))]))
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.fix.get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([text("not json")]))
    partial = client.post(url).json()  # the repair agent fails; the diagnosis is still saved
    assert partial["triage"]["headline"] == "Amounts are in cents" and partial["fix"] is None
    assert "not valid JSON" in partial["errors"]["fix"]

    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.agent.get_client", lambda cfg: pytest.fail("triage already done"))
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.fix.get_client", lambda cfg: FakeClaude([
        tool_calls(("scale_column", {"column": "amount", "factor": 0.01, "reason": "cents"})),
        text(json.dumps({"summary": "s", "not_fixed": [], "warnings": []}))]))
    done = client.post(url).json()
    assert done["errors"] == {} and done["fix"]["by"] == "Ada" and done["fix"]["after"]["total"] < 9
    saved = client.get(f"/api/projects/{seeded['id']}/runs/{doc['run_id']}").json()
    assert saved["triage"]["headline"] == "Amounts are in cents" and saved["fix"]["file_name"] == "d-fixed.csv"


def test_file_sources_upload_replace_and_clean_up(client, tmp_path):
    pid = client.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Files"}).json()["id"]
    base = f"/api/projects/{pid}"
    files = tmp_path / "home" / "source_files"
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/files", files={"file": ("x.exe", b"MZ", "application/octet-stream")}).status_code == 415
    first = client.post(f"{base}/sources/files", files={"file": ("../March orders.csv", GOOD.to_csv(index=False).encode(), "text/csv")}).json()
    assert first["name"] == "March orders.csv" and (files / first["file"]).is_file()
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/test", json={"kind": "file", "settings": {"file": first["file"]}}).json()["ok"]
    assert not client.post(f"{base}/sources/test", json={"kind": "file", "settings": {"file": "../../datapulse.sqlite3"}}).json()["ok"]

    source = client.post(f"{base}/sources", json={"name": "March", "kind": "file", "settings": {"file": first["file"]}}).json()
    assert source["type"] == "File"
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/{source['id']}/pull", json={"role": "baseline"}).status_code == 200

    second = client.post(f"{base}/sources/files", files={"file": ("april.csv", GOOD.head(450).to_csv(index=False).encode(), "text/csv")}).json()
    client.put(f"{base}/sources/{source['id']}", json={"name": "March", "kind": "file", "settings": {"file": second["file"]}})
    assert not (files / first["file"]).exists()  # the replaced version is removed
    assert client.post(f"{base}/sources/{source['id']}/pull", json={}).json()["rows"]["current"] == 450

    assert client.delete(base).status_code == 200
    assert not (files / second["file"]).exists()

"""Excel and documents through the File, Link and Google Drive sources, and the REST source's secret headers."""
import io

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("httpx")
pytest.importorskip("openpyxl")
import httpx  # noqa: E402

import datapulse.sources.http as http_mod  # noqa: E402
from datapulse import sources  # noqa: E402
from datapulse.sources.link import read_bytes  # noqa: E402
from conftest import make_df  # noqa: E402


@pytest.fixture
def mock_http(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", raising=False)  # documents fall back to one record of text each

    def install(handler):
        monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_TRANSPORT", httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        monkeypatch.setattr(http_mod, "_check_host", lambda host: None)
    return install


def test_rest_source_flattens_nested_records_and_keeps_headers_secret(mock_http):
    seen = {}

    def handler(request):
        seen["auth"] = request.headers.get("authorization")
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": {"items": [
            {"id": 1, "customer": {"country": "US"}, "total": 12.5},
            {"id": 2, "customer": {"country": "DE"}, "total": 8}]}})
    mock_http(handler)
    settings = {"url": "https://api.example.com/orders", "records_path": "data.items",
                "headers": '{"Authorization": "Bearer sk_live_Secret123"}'}
    df = sources.fetch_source("rest", settings)
    assert df["customer.country"].tolist() == ["US", "DE"] and seen["auth"] == "Bearer sk_live_Secret123"
    assert sources.public_settings("rest", settings)["headers"] == ""


def test_google_drive_documents_are_read_too(mock_http):
    mock_http(lambda request: httpx.Response(200, content=b"# Invoice 7\n\nTotal: $40\n", headers={
        "content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="invoice.md"', "content-type": "text/markdown"}))
    df = sources.fetch_source("google_drive", {"url": "https://drive.google.com/file/d/AbC123/view?usp=sharing"})
    assert len(df) == 1 and "Invoice 7" in df.loc[0, "title"]


def test_excel_links_are_read():
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    make_df(15).to_excel(buffer, index=False)
    assert len(read_bytes(buffer.getvalue(), "https://example.com/orders.xlsx", "")) == 15

"""The AI data unifier, map path: specs, coercion, and the mapping agent."""
import json

import pytest

from conftest import make_df
from datapulse.config import Config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.unifier import StoredSpec, UnifySpec, apply_spec, coerce, spec_diff, to_json_schema, unify_files
from datapulse.unifier.agent import MAX_VALUE_CHARS, SAMPLE_RECORDS, propose_spec
from datapulse.unifier.heuristic import default_spec, snake_case
from datapulse.unifier.spec import Field, get_path
from fake_claude import FakeClaude, text

SECRET = "ZZ-ROW-VALUE-SECRET"

MAPPING = {
    "description": "Customer orders",
    "fields": [
        {"name": "order_id", "from": "Order #", "type": "integer", "transform": None, "format": None,
         "separator": None, "description": "Order number"},
        {"name": "amount_usd", "from": "Total", "type": "number", "transform": "parse_number", "format": None,
         "separator": None, "description": "Order total in dollars"},
        {"name": "ordered_on", "from": "Date", "type": "date", "transform": "parse_date", "format": "%m/%d/%Y",
         "separator": None, "description": "Order date"},
        {"name": "customer_email", "from": "customer.email", "type": "string", "transform": "lowercase_trim",
         "format": None, "separator": None, "description": "Buyer email"},
    ],
}
RAW = [{"Order #": "1001", "Total": "$1,200.50", "Date": "03/07/2026", "customer": {"email": " Ada@X.com "}},
       {"Order #": "1002", "Total": "(15.00)", "Date": "03/08/2026", "customer": {"email": "bob@x.com"}},
       {"Order #": "1003", "Total": "5 units", "Date": "not a date", "customer": {"email": None}}]


# ---------- spec and coercion ----------

def test_map_spec_applies_types_and_transforms_and_counts_failures():
    spec = UnifySpec.from_dict({**MAPPING, "mode": "map"})
    rows, errors = apply_spec(RAW, spec)
    assert rows[0] == {"order_id": 1001, "amount_usd": 1200.5, "ordered_on": "2026-03-07", "customer_email": "ada@x.com"}
    assert rows[1]["amount_usd"] == -15.0  # accounting negative
    assert rows[2]["amount_usd"] is None and rows[2]["ordered_on"] is None  # never silently "fixed"
    assert errors == {"amount_usd": 1, "ordered_on": 1}
    assert rows[2]["customer_email"] is None  # missing values are null, not errors


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw,field,expected", [
    ("12.5", Field("n", "integer"), (None, False)),
    ("12", Field("n", "integer"), (12, True)),
    ("Yes", Field("b", "boolean"), (True, True)),
    ("maybe", Field("b", "boolean"), (None, False)),
    ("a; b ;c", Field("tags", "array", transform="split", separator=";"), (["a", "b", "c"], True)),
    ('{"k": 1}', Field("o", "object"), ({"k": 1}, True)),
    ("2026-09-01T10:00:00Z", Field("t", "datetime"), ("2026-09-01 10:00:00+00:00", True)),
    ("2026-09-01 10:00:00", Field("t", "datetime"), ("2026-09-01 10:00:00", True)),
    (3.0, Field("s", "string"), ("3", True)),
    ({"a": 1}, Field("s", "string"), ('{"a": 1}', True)),
    ("", Field("n", "number"), (None, True)),
])
def test_coerce(raw, field, expected):
    assert coerce(raw, field) == expected


def test_paths_resolve_exact_keys_nested_objects_and_list_items():
    rec = {"a.b": 1, "a": {"b": 2, "items": [{"sku": "X"}]}}
    assert get_path(rec, "a.b") == 1  # a CSV header containing a dot wins
    assert get_path(rec, "a.items.0.sku") == "X"
    assert get_path(rec, "a.missing") is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad,needle", [
    ({"fields": [{"name": "Bad Name", "type": "string"}]}, "snake_case"),
    ({"fields": [{"name": "x", "type": "decimal"}]}, "unknown type"),
    ({"fields": [{"name": "x", "type": "string", "transform": "eval"}]}, "unknown transform"),
    ({"fields": [{"name": "x", "type": "string"}, {"name": "x", "type": "integer"}]}, "twice"),
    ({"fields": [{"name": "_document", "type": "string"}]}, "snake_case"),
    ({"fields": [{"name": "tags", "type": "array", "transform": "split"}]}, "separator"),
    ({"mode": "extract", "fields": [{"name": "x", "type": "string"}]}, "record_mode"),
    ({"fields": []}, "at least one"),
])
def test_validation_rejects_bad_specs(bad, needle):
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match=needle):
        UnifySpec.from_dict(bad)


def test_heuristic_spec_names_types_and_identifiers():
    recs = [{"Order ID": "007", "Amount": "1.5", "When": "2026-01-01 00:00:00", "Paid": "true",
             "Customer": {"firstName": "Ada"}, "count": 3}]
    spec = default_spec(recs)
    types = {f.name: f.type for f in spec.fields}
    assert types == {"order_id": "string", "amount": "number", "when": "datetime", "paid": "boolean",
                     "customer_first_name": "string", "count": "integer"}  # "007" keeps its leading zeros
    assert snake_case("2nd Place") == "f_2nd_place"


def test_json_schema_and_diff():
    old = UnifySpec.from_dict({**MAPPING, "mode": "map"})
    new_fields = [dict(f) for f in MAPPING["fields"][:3]] + [{"name": "coupon", "type": "string", "from": "Coupon"}]
    new_fields[1]["from"] = "Grand Total"
    new = UnifySpec.from_dict({"mode": "map", "fields": new_fields})
    diff = spec_diff(old, new)
    assert diff["added"] == ["coupon"] and diff["removed"] == ["customer_email"]
    assert diff["remapped"] == [{"name": "amount_usd", "from_before": "Total", "from_now": "Grand Total"}]
    schema = to_json_schema(old)
    assert schema["properties"]["ordered_on"] == {"type": ["string", "null"], "format": "date",
                                                  "description": "Order date"}
    assert spec_diff(old, old) is None


# ---------- the mapping agent ----------

def test_propose_spec_sends_a_bounded_sample_and_self_corrects():
    records = [{"Order #": str(i), "Total": f"${i}.00", "Notes": SECRET + "x" * 500} for i in range(400)]
    bad = dict(MAPPING, fields=[{**MAPPING["fields"][0], "type": "money"}])
    good = {"description": "Orders", "fields": MAPPING["fields"][:2]}
    fake = FakeClaude([text(json.dumps(bad)), text(json.dumps(good))])
    previous = UnifySpec.from_dict({"mode": "map", "fields": [{"name": "order_id", "type": "integer", "from": "id"}]})
    spec = propose_spec(records, previous, Config(), client=fake)
    assert [f.name for f in spec.fields] == ["order_id", "amount_usd"] and spec.mode == "map"
    sent = json.loads(fake.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"])
    assert len(sent["sample_records"]) == SAMPLE_RECORDS and sent["total_records"] == 400
    assert all(len(r["Notes"]) <= MAX_VALUE_CHARS + 1 for r in sent["sample_records"])
    assert sent["previous_mapping"]["fields"][0]["from"] == "id"
    assert "unknown type" in fake.calls[1]["messages"][-1]["content"]  # the validation error went back
    assert fake.calls[0]["output_config"]["format"]["schema"]["required"] == ["description", "fields"]


def test_propose_spec_gives_up_after_repeated_invalid_output():
    bad = text(json.dumps({"description": "x", "fields": []}))
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="valid mapping"):
        propose_spec(RAW, None, Config(), client=FakeClaude([bad, bad]))


# ---------- pipeline ----------

def _csv(tmp_path, df, name="orders.csv"):
    p = tmp_path / name
    df.to_csv(p, index=False)
    return p


def test_pipeline_reuses_the_spec_until_the_shape_changes(tmp_path):
    df = make_df(30)
    first = unify_files([(_csv(tmp_path, df), "orders.csv")], None, Config(), use_ai=False)
    assert first.origin == "heuristic" and not first.reused and len(first.rows) == 30
    prev = StoredSpec(first.spec, first.origin, first.fingerprint)
    again = unify_files([(_csv(tmp_path, make_df(30, seed=3)), "orders.csv")], prev, Config(), use_ai=False)
    assert again.reused and again.changed is None
    shifted = unify_files([(_csv(tmp_path, df.assign(coupon="X")), "orders.csv")], prev, Config(), use_ai=False)
    assert not shifted.reused and shifted.changed["added"] == ["coupon"]


def test_pipeline_rejects_mixed_uploads(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "notes.txt").write_text("hi")
    with pytest.raises(DataPulseError, match="separately"):
        unify_files([(_csv(tmp_path, make_df(5)), "o.csv"), (tmp_path / "notes.txt", "notes.txt")], None, Config())


def test_pipeline_falls_back_when_the_ai_is_unavailable(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    def broken(*a, **k):
        raise DataPulseError("Could not reach the AI service")
    monkeypatch.setattr("datapulse.unifier.pipeline.propose_spec", broken)
    out = unify_files([(_csv(tmp_path, make_df(10)), "o.csv")], None, Config(), use_ai=True)
    assert out.origin == "heuristic" and "AI mapping unavailable" in out.stats["note"]

    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://api:8000/api/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    }

    location /docs {
        proxy_pass http://api:8000/docs;
    }

    location /openapi.json {
        proxy_pass http://api:8000/openapi.json;
    }

    location / {
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }
}

FILE [53/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\web\src\api.ts

export const API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || ''
// Where external tools should send requests (the Vite dev server proxies /api, but curl can't use it).
export const API_ORIGIN = API || (window.location.port === '5173' ? 'http://localhost:8000' : window.location.origin)

export type Severity = 'critical' | 'warning' | 'info'
export const SEVERITIES: Severity[] = ['critical', 'warning', 'info']

export type Issue = {
  check: string; severity: Severity; title: string; columns: string[]; measured_summary: string
  measured: Record<string, unknown>; examples: string[]; suggested_explanation: string; ai_explanation?: string | null
  consumers: Array<{ name: string; type: string; owner: string; columns: string[]; dataset_wide: boolean }>
}
export type Report = {
  status: 'passed' | 'failed'; issues: Issue[]; rows: { baseline: number; current: number }
  passed_checks: string[]; affected_consumers: string[]; schema_changed: boolean; data_file: string
  ai_note?: string | null; markdown?: string; triage?: Triage | null; counts?: Record<Severity, number>
  charts?: Record<string, ColumnChart>; fix?: Fix | null
  source?: { id: string; name: string; kind: string; type?: string; rows_pulled: number; mapped: boolean; steps: string[]; problems: string[] }
  unified?: { sources: Array<{ name: string; kind: string; type?: string; rows: number; error: string | null; problems: string[] }> }
  delivered?: Delivery[]
}
export type ColumnChart = {
  null_rate: [number, number | null]
  hist?: { edges: number[]; baseline: number[]; current: number[] }
}
type Counts = { total: number; critical: number; warning: number; info: number }
export type Fix = {
  summary: string; not_fixed: Array<{ issue: string; why: string }>; warnings: string[]
  operations: Array<{ operation: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; reason: string; effect: string }>
  before: Counts; after: Counts & { remaining: string[] }; rows: { before: number; after: number }
  file_name: string; by?: string; at?: string
}
export type Investigation = { triage: Triage | null; fix: Fix | null; errors: Partial<Record<'triage' | 'fix', string>> }
export type SourceField = { name: string; label: string; placeholder: string; secret: boolean; required: boolean; multiline: boolean; help: string; input: 'text' | 'file' }
export type SourceKind = { kind: string; label: string; group: string; description: string; fields: SourceField[]; available: boolean; missing_packages: string[]; hidden: boolean; has_sample: boolean }
export type Step = { operation: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; reason: string; effect?: string }
export type Mapping = Omit<Fix, 'file_name' | 'operations'> & { operations: Step[]; automatic_steps: number; source_columns: string[]; model: string | null; at?: string }
export type Source = {
  id: string; name: string; kind: string; type: string; settings: Record<string, string> & { saved_secrets?: string[] }
  status: 'standard' | 'mapped' | 'needs_mapping'; recipe: Step[]; proposal: Mapping | null
  last_pulled_at: string | null; last_run_id: string | null; created_by: string | null; created_at: string
}
export type Delivery = { id: string; name: string; type: string; table: string; ok: boolean; held: boolean; message: string }
export type Destination = {
  id: string; name: string; type: string; table: string; mode: 'replace' | 'append'; saved_secrets: string[]
  last_sent_at: string | null; last_result: string | null; created_by: string | null; created_at: string
}
export type DestinationForm = { name: string; url: string; table: string; mode: 'replace' | 'append' }
export type SourceForm = { name: string; kind: string; settings: Record<string, string> }
export type ColumnSide = { kind: string; null_rate: number | null; mean: number | null; min: number | null; max: number | null; top: string[]; samples?: string[] }
export type ColumnIssue = { check: string; severity: Severity; title: string; detail: string }
export type ComparedColumn = {
  name: string; source: string | null; status: 'same' | 'renamed' | 'converted' | 'flagged' | 'missing'
  steps: Array<{ operation: string; effect: string }>; issues: ColumnIssue[]
  standard: ColumnSide; before: ColumnSide | null; after: ColumnSide | null
}
export type Comparison = {
  columns: ComparedColumn[]; extra: Array<{ name: string; as: string; after: ColumnSide }>
  dropped: Array<{ name: string; effect: string }>; table_steps: string[]; problems: string[]
  rows: { before: number; after: number; standard: number }; before: Counts; after: Counts
  table_issues: ColumnIssue[]; show_values: boolean
}
export type Triage = {
  headline: string; verdict: 'block' | 'investigate' | 'safe_to_ship'; verdict_reason: string
  root_causes: Array<{ hypothesis: string; confidence: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high'; evidence: string }>
  actions: Array<{ step: string; owner: string }>; notify: Array<{ who: string; why: string }>; message: string
  steps: Array<{ tool: string; input: Record<string, unknown> }>; model: string; by?: string; at?: string
}
export type RulesDraft = { config_yaml: string; summary: string; not_supported: string[] }
export type ReviewStatus = 'open' | 'acknowledged' | 'resolved'
export type Review = { status: ReviewStatus; by?: string | null; at?: string | null }
export type Run = {
  id: string; status: string; issue_count: number; created_at: string; report: Report
  created_by?: string | null; review: Review
}
export type User = { id: string; name: string; email: string; role: 'admin' | 'member' }
export type Project = { id: string; name: string; has_baseline: boolean; run_count: number; latest_status?: string | null }
export type ColumnStats = { kind: string; dtype: string; null_rate: number | null; unique_count: number | null }
export type Baseline = { baseline_version: number; rows: number; source?: string; created_at?: string; columns: Record<string, ColumnStats> }
export type ProjectDetail = {
  id: string; name: string; has_baseline: boolean; config_yaml: string; fail_on: string; baseline: Baseline | null
  consumers: Array<{ name: string; type: string; owner: string; columns: string[] }>
  runs: Run[]
}
export type Created = { id: string; api_key: string }

async function request<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const init: RequestInit = { method }
  if (body instanceof FormData) init.body = body
  else if (body !== undefined) Object.assign(init, { body: JSON.stringify(body), headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, { ...init, credentials: 'include' })
  // A session that expired or was revoked sends everyone back to the sign-in screen.
  if (response.status === 401 && !path.startsWith('/api/auth/')) window.dispatchEvent(new Event('datapulse:signed-out'))
  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail = await response.json().then(b => b.detail, () => null)
    // FastAPI validation errors arrive as a list of {msg} objects
    throw new Error(typeof detail === 'string' ? detail
      : Array.isArray(detail) ? detail.map((d: { msg: string }) => d.msg).join('; ')
      : `Request failed (${response.status})`)
  }
  return response.json()
}

export const api = {
  authStatus: () => request<{ setup_needed: boolean; user: User | null }>('GET', '/api/auth/status'),
  setup: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => request<User>('POST', '/api/auth/setup', { name, email, password }),
  login: (email: string, password: string) => request<User>('POST', '/api/auth/login', { email, password }),
  logout: () => request('POST', '/api/auth/logout'),
  changePassword: (current_password: string, new_password: string) =>
    request('PUT', '/api/auth/password', { current_password, new_password }),
  users: () => request<User[]>('GET', '/api/users'),
  addUser: (u: { name: string; email: string; password: string; role: User['role'] }) => request<User>('POST', '/api/users', u),
  removeUser: (id: string) => request('DELETE', `/api/users/${id}`),
  investigate: (id: string, run: string) => request<Investigation>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/investigate`),
  runDataUrl: (id: string, run: string) => `${API}/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/data`,
  sourceCatalog: () => request<SourceKind[]>('GET', '/api/sources/catalog'),
  sourceSample: (id: string, kind: string) =>
    request<{ name: string; settings: Record<string, string> }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/sample`, { kind }),
  sources: (id: string) => request<Source[]>('GET', `/api/projects/${id}/sources`),
  testSource: (id: string, kind: string, settings: Record<string, string>, source_id?: string) =>
    request<{ ok: boolean; message: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/test`, { kind, settings, source_id }),
  uploadSourceFile(id: string, file: File) {
    const form = new FormData()
    form.append('file', file)
    return request<{ file: string; name: string; note: string | null }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/files`, form)
  },
  addSource: (id: string, form: SourceForm) => request<Source>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources`, form),
  editSource: (id: string, sid: string, form: SourceForm) => request<Source>('PUT', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}`, form),
  removeSource: (id: string, sid: string) => request('DELETE', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}`),
  mapSource: (id: string, sid: string) => request<Mapping>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}/map`),
  compareSource: (id: string, sid: string, operations?: Step[]) =>
    request<Comparison>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}/compare`, { operations: operations ?? null }),
  discardMapping: (id: string, sid: string) => request<Source>('DELETE', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}/proposal`),
  saveRecipe: (id: string, sid: string, operations: Step[]) => request<Source>('PUT', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}/recipe`, { operations }),
  pullSource: (id: string, sid: string, role: 'baseline' | 'candidate') =>
    request<Report & { run_id?: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/sources/${sid}/pull`, { role }),
  destinations: (id: string) => request<Destination[]>('GET', `/api/projects/${id}/destinations`),
  testDestination: (id: string, form: Omit<DestinationForm, 'name'>, destination_id?: string) =>
    request<{ ok: boolean; message: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/destinations/test`, { ...form, destination_id }),
  addDestination: (id: string, form: DestinationForm) => request<Destination>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/destinations`, form),
  editDestination: (id: string, did: string, form: DestinationForm) => request<Destination>('PUT', `/api/projects/${id}/destinations/${did}`, form),
  removeDestination: (id: string, did: string) => request('DELETE', `/api/projects/${id}/destinations/${did}`),
  sendRun: (id: string, run: string) => request<{ delivered: Delivery[] }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/send`),
  unify: (id: string) => request<Report & { run_id: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/unify`),
  checkFixed: (id: string, run: string) => request<Report & { run_id: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/fixed/check`),
  fixedUrl: (id: string, run: string) => `${API}/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/fixed`,
  review: (id: string, run: string, status: ReviewStatus) => request<Review>('PUT', `/api/projects/${id}/runs/${run}/review`, { status }),
  health: () => request<{ version: string; ai: boolean }>('GET', '/api/health'),
  projects: () => request<Project[]>('GET', '/api/projects'),
  project: (id: string) => request<ProjectDetail>('GET', `/api/projects/${id}`),
  create: (name: string) => request<Created>('POST', '/api/projects', { name }),
  seed: () => request<Created>('POST', '/api/demo/seed'),
  remove: (id: string) => request('DELETE', `/api/projects/${id}`),
  saveRules: (id: string, config_yaml: string) => request('PUT', `/api/projects/${id}/config`, { config_yaml }),
  draftRules: (id: string, req: string, config_yaml: string) =>
    request<RulesDraft>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/rules/draft`, { request: req, config_yaml }),
  suggestions: (id: string) => request<{ yaml: string }>('GET', `/api/projects/${id}/suggestions`),
  upload(id: string, kind: 'baseline' | 'runs', file: File, useAi = false) {
    const form = new FormData()
    form.append('file', file)
    if (kind === 'runs') form.append('use_ai', String(useAi))
    return request<Report & { run_id?: string }>('POST', `/api/projects/${id}/${kind}`, form)
  },
}

export const plural = (n: number, word: string) => `${n.toLocaleString()} ${word}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`

export function fmtDate(value?: string) {
  return value ? new Date(value).toLocaleString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }) : '—'
}

export function timeAgo(value?: string) {
  if (!value) return '—'
  const minutes = (Date.now() - new Date(value).getTime()) / 60000
  if (minutes < 1) return 'just now'
  if (minutes < 60) return `${Math.round(minutes)}m ago`
  if (minutes < 1440) return `${Math.round(minutes / 60)}h ago`
  return minutes < 10080 ? `${Math.round(minutes / 1440)}d ago` : fmtDate(value)
}

/** Overall state of a run, used for colour and wording everywhere. */
export type Tone = 'failed' | 'issues' | 'clean'
export const runTone = (run: Pick<Run, 'status' | 'issue_count'>): Tone =>
  run.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : run.issue_count ? 'issues' : 'clean'
export const TONE_LABEL: Record<Tone, string> = { failed: 'Failed', issues: 'Passed with warnings', clean: 'All clear' }

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, API_ORIGIN, plural, type Created, type Project, type User } from './api'
import { AccountPage, SignIn } from './auth'
import { Icon } from './icons'
import { ProjectView, TABS, type Tab } from './project'
import { Modal, Spinner, ToastProvider, useAction, useNotify, useTheme } from './ui'

export type Route = { id?: string; tab: Tab; run?: string; account?: boolean }

// #/p/<project>/<tab>/<run> — the URL is the source of truth, so links and the back button work.
function parseHash(): Route {
  if (window.location.hash.startsWith('#/account')) return { tab: 'checks', account: true }
  const [, id, tab, run] = window.location.hash.match(/^#\/p\/([\w-]+)(?:\/(\w+))?(?:\/([\w-]+))?/) || []
  return { id, tab: TABS.includes(tab as Tab) ? tab as Tab : 'sources', run }
}

function useRoute() {
  const [route, setRoute] = useState(parseHash)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onChange = () => setRoute(parseHash())
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', onChange)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', onChange)
  }, [])
  const go = useCallback((next: Route, replace = false) => {
    const hash = next.id ? `#/p/${next.id}/${next.tab}${next.run ? `/${next.run}` : ''}` : '#/'
    if (replace) { window.history.replaceState(null, '', hash); setRoute(parseHash()) } else window.location.hash = hash
  }, [])
  return [route, go] as const
}

export default function App() {
  return <ToastProvider><Gate /></ToastProvider>
}

/** Nothing but the sign-in screen until someone is signed in. */
function Gate() {
  const [auth, setAuth] = useState<{ setupNeeded: boolean; user: User | null } | 'offline' | null>(null)
  const check = useCallback(() => api.authStatus().then(s => setAuth({ setupNeeded: s.setup_needed, user: s.user }),
    () => setAuth('offline')), [])
  useEffect(() => {
    check()
    const signedOut = () => setAuth(a => a && a !== 'offline' ? { ...a, user: null } : a)
    window.addEventListener('datapulse:signed-out', signedOut)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('datapulse:signed-out', signedOut)
  }, [check])

  if (auth === null) return <div className="loading center"><span className="spinner" /> Loading…</div>
  if (auth === 'offline') return <div className="main"><Offline retry={check} /></div>
  if (!auth.user) return <SignIn setupNeeded={auth.setupNeeded} onSignedIn={user => setAuth({ setupNeeded: false, user })} />
  const signOut = () => api.logout().finally(() => setAuth({ setupNeeded: false, user: null }))
  return <Shell user={auth.user} signOut={signOut} />
}

function Shell({ user, signOut }: { user: User; signOut: () => void }) {
  const { theme, toggle } = useTheme()
  const [route, go] = useRoute()
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[] | null>(null)
  const [offline, setOffline] = useState(false)
  const [ai, setAi] = useState(false)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  const [newKey, setNewKey] = useState<{ id: string; key: string } | null>(null)

  const refresh = useCallback(() => api.projects().then(list => { setProjects(list); setOffline(false) }, () => setOffline(true)), [])
  useEffect(() => { refresh(); api.health().then(h => setAi(h.ai), () => {}) }, [refresh])

  // Land on the first project when the URL doesn't name one that exists.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!route.account && projects?.length && !projects.some(p => p.id === route.id)) go({ id: projects[0].id, tab: 'sources' }, true)
  }, [projects, route.id, route.account, go])

  const opened = useCallback((created: Created) => {
    setNewKey({ id: created.id, key: created.api_key })
    setCreating(false)
    refresh().then(() => go({ id: created.id, tab: 'sources' }))
  }, [refresh, go])

  const active = projects?.find(p => p.id === route.id)

  return <div className="shell">
    <aside className="sidebar">
      <div className="brand"><span className="logo"><Icon name="activity" size={18} /></span>DataPulse</div>
      <button className="button primary full" onClick={() => setCreating(true)} disabled={offline}><Icon name="plus" size={15} /> New project</button>
      <nav className="project-list" aria-label="Projects">
        {projects?.map(p => <a key={p.id} href={`#/p/${p.id}/sources`} className={`project-link ${p.id === route.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
          aria-current={p.id === route.id ? 'page' : undefined}>
          <i className={`dot ${p.latest_status || ''}`} title={p.latest_status ? `Last check ${p.latest_status}` : 'No checks yet'} />
          <span><b>{p.name}</b><small>{p.has_baseline ? plural(p.run_count, 'check') : 'Needs a standard'}</small></span>
        </a>)}
      </nav>
      <div className="sidebar-foot">
        <a href="#/account" className={`me ${route.account ? 'active' : ''}`} title="Account & team">
          <span className="avatar">{user.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</span>
          <span><b>{user.name}</b><small>{user.role === 'admin' ? 'Admin · manage team' : 'Account'}</small></span>
        </a>
        <div className="foot-actions">
          <a href={`${API_ORIGIN}/docs`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><Icon name="code" size={14} /> API docs</a>
          <button className="icon-btn" onClick={toggle} aria-label={`Switch to ${theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'} mode`}>
            <Icon name={theme === 'dark' ? 'sun' : 'moon'} />
          </button>
          <button className="button ghost small-btn" onClick={signOut}>Sign out</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>

    <main className="main">
      {route.account ? <AccountPage user={user} />
        : offline ? <Offline retry={refresh} />
        : projects === null ? <div className="loading"><span className="spinner" /> Loading…</div>
        : !projects.length ? <Welcome onCreated={opened} />
        : active && <ProjectView key={active.id} route={{ ...route, id: active.id }} go={go} onChanged={refresh} ai={ai}
            apiKey={newKey?.id === active.id ? newKey.key : undefined} dismissKey={() => setNewKey(null)} />}
    </main>

    {creating && <Modal title="New project" onClose={() => setCreating(false)}>
      <p className="muted">A project unifies one kind of data, such as orders or patients, from all the places it lives.</p>
      <CreateProject onCreated={opened} autoFocus />
      <div className="or">or</div>
      <SampleButton onCreated={opened} />
    </Modal>}
  </div>
}

function Offline({ retry }: { retry: () => void }) {
  return <div className="center-card">
    <span className="empty-icon bad"><Icon name="plug" size={22} /></span>
    <h1>Can't reach the DataPulse API</h1>
    <p>Start it from the project folder, then try again:</p>
    <pre className="inline-code">docker compose up</pre>
    <button className="button primary" onClick={retry}>Try again</button>
  </div>
}

function Welcome({ onCreated }: { onCreated: (c: Created) => void }) {
  return <div className="center-card wide">
    <h1>All your data, in one format, checked</h1>
    <p>The same data lives in many places, each in its own shape: a PostgreSQL database, a MySQL store system,
      MongoDB, a Google Sheet. DataPulse connects to all of them, converts each one to a single standard format,
      and combines them into one clean table, checked on every pull.</p>
    <ol className="how">
      <li><b>Connect your sources</b><span>Databases, spreadsheets and file links. Passwords stay on the server; access is read-only.</span></li>
      <li><b>AI maps each one to your standard</b><span>Renamed columns, cents vs. dollars, numbers stored as text. You approve once; every later pull converts automatically.</span></li>
      <li><b>Get one unified, checked table</b><span>Rules and an ML drift model catch problems. AI investigates them and repairs a copy.</span></li>
    </ol>
    <div className="welcome-actions">
      <CreateProject onCreated={onCreated} />
      <div className="or">or</div>
      <SampleButton onCreated={onCreated} />
    </div>
  </div>
}

/** A sample project with three connected databases holding the same orders in different formats. */
function SampleButton({ onCreated }: { onCreated: (c: Created) => void }) {
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const notify = useNotify()
  return <button className="button outline" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => run('seed', async () => {
    onCreated(await api.seed()); notify.ok('Sample project ready: 3 connected sources. Map the two that need it, then Unify.')
  })}>{busy ? <Spinner text="Loading…" /> : <><Icon name="sparkle" size={15} /> Try it with sample data</>}</button>
}

function CreateProject({ onCreated, autoFocus }: { onCreated: (c: Created) => void; autoFocus?: boolean }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const valid = name.trim().length >= 2
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => { e.preventDefault(); if (valid) run('create', async () => onCreated(await api.create(name.trim()))) }
  return <form className="create" onSubmit={submit}>
    <label htmlFor="project-name">Project name</label>
    <div className="row">
      <input id="project-name" autoFocus={autoFocus} value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Orders pipeline" maxLength={80} />
      <button className="button primary" disabled={!valid || !!busy}>{busy ? <Spinner text="Creating…" /> : 'Create'}</button>
    </div>
  </form>
}

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, type User } from './api'
import { Icon } from './icons'
import { Spinner, useAction, useNotify } from './ui'

/** First run (create the admin account) or sign in. */
export function SignIn({ setupNeeded, onSignedIn }: { setupNeeded: boolean; onSignedIn: (u: User) => void }) {
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', email: '', password: '' })
  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: e.target.value }))
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    run('auth', async () => onSignedIn(setupNeeded
      ? await api.setup(form.name.trim(), form.email, form.password)
      : await api.login(form.email, form.password)))
  }
  return <div className="auth-page">
    <form className="auth-card" onSubmit={submit}>
      <div className="brand"><span className="logo"><Icon name="activity" size={18} /></span>DataPulse</div>
      <h1>{setupNeeded ? 'Create the admin account' : 'Sign in'}</h1>
      {setupNeeded && <p className="muted">You're the first person here. You'll add your teammates next.</p>}
      {setupNeeded && <label>Name<input value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} autoFocus required maxLength={80} /></label>}
      <label>Email<input type="email" value={form.email} onChange={set('email')} autoFocus={!setupNeeded} required autoComplete="username" /></label>
      <label>Password<input type="password" value={form.password} onChange={set('password')} required
        minLength={setupNeeded ? 8 : 1} autoComplete={setupNeeded ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'} />
        {setupNeeded && <small className="muted">At least 8 characters</small>}</label>
      <button className="button primary full" disabled={!!busy}>{busy ? <Spinner text="One moment…" /> : setupNeeded ? 'Create account' : 'Sign in'}</button>
      {!setupNeeded && <p className="muted small">No account? Ask your DataPulse admin to add you.</p>}
    </form>
  </div>
}

/** Change your password; admins also manage who has access. */
export function AccountPage({ user }: { user: User }) {
  return <div className="account">
    <h1>Account & team</h1>
    <p className="muted">Signed in as <b>{user.name}</b> ({user.email}) · {user.role}</p>
    {user.role === 'admin' && <Team me={user} />}
    <ChangePassword />
  </div>
}

function Team({ me }: { me: User }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([])
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'member' as User['role'] })
  const load = () => api.users().then(setUsers, notify.fail)
  useEffect(() => { load() }, []) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const add = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    run('add', async () => {
      await api.addUser(form)
      notify.ok(`Added ${form.name}. Send them the address of this dashboard and their temporary password.`)
      setForm({ name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'member' })
      await load()
    })
  }
  const remove = (u: User) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Remove ${u.name}? They will be signed out and lose access.`)) return
    run('remove', async () => { await api.removeUser(u.id); notify.ok(`Removed ${u.name}`); await load() })
  }

  return <section className="card">
    <h2>Team</h2>
    <p className="muted">Everyone here sees the same projects, checks and AI triage.</p>
    <ul className="people">{users.map(u => <li key={u.id}>
      <span className="avatar">{u.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</span>
      <span><b>{u.name}{u.id === me.id && ' (you)'}</b><small>{u.email}</small></span>
      <em>{u.role}</em>
      {u.id !== me.id && <button className="icon-btn" aria-label={`Remove ${u.name}`} onClick={() => remove(u)}><Icon name="trash" size={15} /></button>}
    </li>)}</ul>
    <form className="add-person" onSubmit={add}>
      <h3>Add a teammate</h3>
      <div className="grid">
        <input placeholder="Name" value={form.name} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} required maxLength={80} />
        <input placeholder="Email" type="email" value={form.email} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} required />
        <input placeholder="Temporary password (8+ characters)" value={form.password} minLength={8}
          onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} required />
        <select value={form.role} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, role: e.target.value as User['role'] })} aria-label="Role">
          <option value="member">Member</option><option value="admin">Admin</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy}>{busy === 'add' ? <Spinner text="Adding…" /> : 'Add teammate'}</button>
    </form>
  </section>
}

function ChangePassword() {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState('')
  const [next, setNext] = useState('')
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    run('pw', async () => { await api.changePassword(current, next); setCurrent(''); setNext(''); notify.ok('Password changed') })
  }
  return <form className="card change-password" onSubmit={submit}>
    <h2>Change your password</h2>
    <input type="password" placeholder="Current password" value={current} onChange={e => setCurrent(e.target.value)} required autoComplete="current-password" />
    <input type="password" placeholder="New password (8+ characters)" value={next} onChange={e => setNext(e.target.value)} minLength={8} required autoComplete="new-password" />
    <button className="button outline" disabled={!!busy}>{busy ? <Spinner text="Saving…" /> : 'Change password'}</button>
  </form>
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import type { ColumnChart } from './api'

const pct = (p: number) => `${(p * 100).toFixed(p > 0 && p < 0.1 ? 1 : 0)}%`
const num = (v: number) => Math.abs(v) >= 1000 ? v.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })
  : v.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: Math.abs(v) < 10 ? 2 : 1 })

/** Bucket i of a histogram whose edges are the baseline's deciles: "< e0", "e0–e1", …, "> eN". */
function bucketLabel(edges: number[], i: number) {
  if (i === 0) return `under ${num(edges[0])}`
  if (i === edges.length) return `over ${num(edges[edges.length - 1])}`
  return `${num(edges[i - 1])} – ${num(edges[i])}`
}

function Legend() {
  return <div className="chart-legend">
    <span><i className="sw base" />Baseline</span><span><i className="sw new" />New file</span>
  </div>
}

/** Share of values in each of the baseline's buckets, baseline vs. the new file, on one scale. */
export function BeforeAfter({ column, chart }: { column: string; chart: NonNullable<ColumnChart['hist']> }) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const n = chart.baseline.length
  const W = 560, H = 170, top = 14, bottom = 30, plot = H - top - bottom
  const max = Math.max(...chart.baseline, ...chart.current, 0.01)
  const slot = W / n, gap = 2, bar = Math.max(3, Math.min(22, (slot - 10) / 2))
  const y = (p: number) => top + plot - (p / max) * plot
  const path = (x: number, p: number) => {
    if (p <= 0) return ''
    const h = Math.max(2, (p / max) * plot), r = Math.min(4, bar / 2, h)
    const yt = top + plot - h
    return `M${x},${top + plot} V${yt + r} Q${x},${yt} ${x + r},${yt} H${x + bar - r} Q${x + bar},${yt} ${x + bar},${yt + r} V${top + plot} Z`
  }
  const peak = chart.current.indexOf(Math.max(...chart.current))
  return <figure className="chart">
    <Legend />
    <div className="chart-plot" onMouseLeave={() => setHover(null)}>
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`} role="img"
        aria-label={`${column}: share of values per range, baseline vs new file. Largest share in the new file: ${bucketLabel(chart.edges, peak)} (${pct(chart.current[peak])}).`}>
        <line className="grid" x1={0} x2={W} y1={y(max)} y2={y(max)} />
        <text className="tick" x={0} y={y(max) - 4}>{pct(max)}</text>
        <line className="axis" x1={0} x2={W} y1={top + plot} y2={top + plot} />
        {chart.baseline.map((b, i) => {
          const x0 = i * slot + (slot - 2 * bar - gap) / 2
          return <g key={i} className={hover !== null && hover !== i ? 'dim' : ''} onMouseEnter={() => setHover(i)}>
            <rect className="hit" x={i * slot} y={0} width={slot} height={H} />
            <path className="bar base" d={path(x0, b)} />
            <path className="bar new" d={path(x0 + bar + gap, chart.current[i])} />
          </g>
        })}
        <text className="tick" x={2} y={H - 8}>{bucketLabel(chart.edges, 0)}</text>
        <text className="tick" x={W - 2} y={H - 8} textAnchor="end">{bucketLabel(chart.edges, n - 1)}</text>
        {chart.current[peak] > 0.3 && <text className="direct" x={peak * slot + slot / 2} y={y(chart.current[peak]) - 5}
          textAnchor="middle">{pct(chart.current[peak])}</text>}
      </svg>
      {hover !== null && <div className="chart-tip" style={{ left: `${((hover + 0.5) / n) * 100}%` }}>
        <b>{bucketLabel(chart.edges, hover)}</b>
        <span><i className="sw base" />Baseline {pct(chart.baseline[hover])}</span>
        <span><i className="sw new" />New file {pct(chart.current[hover])}</span>
      </div>}
    </div>
    <figcaption>Share of <code>{column}</code> values in each range. The ranges are the baseline's deciles, so a normal file has about 10% in each.</figcaption>
  </figure>
}

/** Empty-value rate, baseline vs. the new file. */
export function NullRates({ column, rates }: { column: string; rates: [number, number | null] }) {
  const rows: Array<[string, string, number | null]> = [['Baseline', 'base', rates[0]], ['New file', 'new', rates[1]]]
  const max = Math.max(rates[0], rates[1] ?? 0, 0.05)
  return <figure className="chart" aria-label={`Empty values in ${column}: baseline ${pct(rates[0])}, new file ${rates[1] === null ? 'column missing' : pct(rates[1])}`}>
    <div className="hbars">
      {rows.map(([label, cls, v]) => <div key={label} className="hbar">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span className="track"><i className={cls} style={{ width: `${((v ?? 0) / max) * 100}%` }} /></span>
        <b>{v === null ? 'missing' : pct(v)}</b>
      </div>)}
    </div>
    <figcaption>Share of empty values in <code>{column}</code>.</figcaption>
  </figure>
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { api, plural, runTone, timeAgo, type ColumnSide, type ComparedColumn, type Comparison, type ProjectDetail,
  type Run, type Severity, type Source, type Step, type Tone } from './api'
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons'
import { Spinner } from './ui'

const pct = (p: number | null | undefined) => p == null ? '—' : `${(p * 100).toFixed(p > 0 && p < 0.1 ? 1 : 0)}%`
const num = (v: number | null | undefined) => v == null ? '—' : Math.abs(v) >= 1000
  ? v.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) : v.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })
const countsOf = (run: Run): Record<Severity, number> => run.report.counts ?? {
  critical: run.report.issues.filter(i => i.severity === 'critical').length,
  warning: run.report.issues.filter(i => i.severity === 'warning').length,
  info: run.report.issues.filter(i => i.severity === 'info').length,
}
const RULES: Record<string, string> = {
  schema: 'columns & types', row_count: 'row count', null_rate: 'empty values', categorical: 'allowed values',
  duplicates: 'duplicates', distribution: 'value ranges', ml_drift: 'learned drift',
}
const ruleName = (check: string) => RULES[check] ?? check.replace(/_/g, ' ')

/** The most recent check of one source, from the project's recent runs. */
export const lastCheckOf = (detail: ProjectDetail, source: Source) => detail.runs.find(r => r.report.source?.id === source.id)

/* ---------- health cards ---------- */

export function HealthCards({ detail, sources }: { detail: ProjectDetail; sources: Source[] }) {
  const unified = detail.runs.find(r => r.report.unified)
  const latest = detail.runs[0]
  const recent = detail.runs.slice(0, 10)
  const passing = recent.filter(r => r.status === 'passed').length
  const todo = sources.filter(s => s.status === 'needs_mapping').length
  const failing = sources.filter(s => { const run = lastCheckOf(detail, s); return run && run.status === 'failed' }).length
  const counts = latest ? countsOf(latest) : null
  const cards: Array<{ label: string; value: string; note: string; tone: Tone | 'none'; icon: IconName }> = [
    { label: 'Sources', value: String(sources.length), icon: 'plug',
      tone: !sources.length ? 'none' : todo || failing ? 'issues' : 'clean',
      note: !sources.length ? 'None connected yet' : [`${sources.length - todo} mapped`, todo && `${todo} need mapping`, failing && `${failing} failing`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') },
    { label: 'Unified table', value: unified ? unified.report.rows.current.toLocaleString() : '—', icon: 'database',
      tone: unified ? runTone(unified) : 'none',
      note: unified ? `rows · ${timeAgo(unified.created_at)} · ${unified.issue_count ? plural(unified.issue_count, 'issue') : 'all clear'}` : 'Not unified yet' },
    { label: 'Checks passing', value: recent.length ? `${passing}/${recent.length}` : '—', icon: 'activity',
      tone: !recent.length ? 'none' : passing === recent.length ? 'clean' : passing >= recent.length / 2 ? 'issues' : 'failed',
      note: recent.length ? `of the last ${plural(recent.length, 'check')}` : 'No checks yet' },
    { label: 'Latest issues', value: counts ? String(counts.critical + counts.warning + counts.info) : '—', icon: 'flag',
      tone: !latest ? 'none' : runTone(latest),
      note: counts ? `${counts.critical} critical · ${counts.warning} warning${latest ? ` · ${timeAgo(latest.created_at)}` : ''}` : 'No checks yet' },
  ]
  return <div className="health">{cards.map(c => <div key={c.label} className={`card health-card ${c.tone}`}>
    <span className="health-icon"><Icon name={c.icon} size={16} /></span>
    <small>{c.label}</small>
    <b>{c.value}</b>
    <span className="muted">{c.note}</span>
  </div>)}</div>
}

/* ---------- sources → standard → unified flow ---------- */

const LANE = 64, GAP = 10

export function FlowDiagram({ detail, sources, icon, openRun, unify, unifying, canUnify }:
  { detail: ProjectDetail; sources: Source[]; icon: (kind: string) => IconName; openRun: (run?: string) => void
    unify: () => void; unifying: boolean; canUnify: boolean }) {
  const standard = sources.find(s => s.status === 'standard')
  const unified = detail.runs.find(r => r.report.unified)
  const height = Math.max(LANE, sources.length * (LANE + GAP) - GAP)
  const W = 120
  const lane = (s: Source) => {
    const run = lastCheckOf(detail, s)
    const state = s.status === 'needs_mapping' ? 'todo' : run?.status === 'failed' ? 'bad' : s.status === 'standard' ? 'std' : 'ok'
    const label = s.status === 'standard' ? 'Standard' : s.status === 'needs_mapping' ? 'Needs mapping'
      : s.recipe.length ? plural(s.recipe.length, 'step') : 'Same format'
    return { run, state, label }
  }
  return <section className="card flow" aria-label="How your sources become one table">
    <div className="card-head"><h3>Sources → unified table</h3>
      <small className="muted">Each source is converted to the standard, then combined and checked.</small></div>
    {!sources.length ? <p className="muted">Add a source to see it here.</p> : <div className="flow-grid">
      <ol className="flow-lanes" style={{ gap: GAP }}>{sources.map(s => {
        const { run, state, label } = lane(s)
        return <li key={s.id} style={{ height: LANE }}>
          <a className={`flow-node ${state}`} href={`#source-${s.id}`}
            onClick={e => { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById(`source-${s.id}`)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }) }}>
            <span className="source-icon"><Icon name={icon(s.kind)} size={16} /></span>
            <span className="flow-text"><b>{s.name}</b>
              <small>{s.type}{run ? ` · ${run.report.rows.current.toLocaleString()} rows` : ''}</small></span>
            <span className={`flow-chip ${state}`}>{label}</span>
            {run && <span className={`dot ${run.status}`} title={`Last check ${run.status}: ${plural(run.issue_count, 'issue')}`} />}
          </a>
        </li>
      })}</ol>
      <svg className="flow-links" viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${height}`} preserveAspectRatio="none" style={{ height }} aria-hidden="true">
        {sources.map((s, i) => {
          const y = i * (LANE + GAP) + LANE / 2, mid = height / 2
          return <path key={s.id} className={lane(s).state} d={`M0,${y} C${W / 2},${y} ${W / 2},${mid} ${W},${mid}`} />
        })}
      </svg>
      <div className={`flow-target ${unified ? runTone(unified) : ''}`} style={{ minHeight: Math.min(height, 220) }}>
        <small className="step">Standard format</small>
        <b>{standard ? standard.name : 'Not chosen yet'}</b>
        <span className="muted">{detail.baseline ? `${plural(Object.keys(detail.baseline.columns).length, 'column')} · v${detail.baseline.baseline_version}` : 'Use one source as the standard'}</span>
        <hr />
        <small className="step">Unified table</small>
        {unified ? <>
          <b>{unified.report.rows.current.toLocaleString()} rows</b>
          <span className={`flow-status ${runTone(unified)}`}>{unified.issue_count ? plural(unified.issue_count, 'issue') : 'All checks passed'} · {timeAgo(unified.created_at)}</span>
          <div className="actions">
            <button className="button ghost small-btn" onClick={() => openRun(unified.id)}>Report</button>
            <a className="button ghost small-btn" href={api.runDataUrl(detail.id, unified.id)}><Icon name="download" size={13} /> Data</a>
          </div>
        </> : <span className="muted">Not unified yet</span>}
        {detail.has_baseline && <button className="button primary small-btn" disabled={!canUnify || unifying} onClick={unify}>
          {unifying ? <Spinner text="Unifying…" /> : unified ? 'Unify again' : 'Unify now'}</button>}
      </div>
    </div>}
  </section>
}

/* ---------- their column → our column ---------- */

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<ComparedColumn['status'], string> = {
  same: 'Same', renamed: 'Renamed', converted: 'Converted', flagged: 'Flagged by a rule', missing: 'Missing',
}
const STEP_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  rename_column: 'rename', scale_column: 'scale', convert_to_number: 'to number', standardize_categories: 'fix spelling',
  fill_missing: 'fill empty',
}

/** Kinds that are compatible for the schema check (whole-number floats count as integers). */
const sameKind = (a?: string, b?: string) => a === b || (a === 'float' && b === 'integer')

/** Values differ from the standard though no rule flags them, e.g. ids offset by 50,000. */
function looksDifferent(std: ColumnSide, after: ColumnSide | null) {
  if (!after || std.mean == null || after.mean == null || std.min == null || std.max == null || after.min == null || after.max == null) return false
  const overlap = Math.min(std.max, after.max) - Math.max(std.min, after.min)
  const span = Math.max(std.max - std.min, 1e-9)
  return overlap < 0.25 * span || Math.abs(after.mean - std.mean) > 0.5 * Math.max(Math.abs(std.mean), span)
}

function RangeBar({ std, after }: { std: ColumnSide; after: ColumnSide }) {
  if (std.min == null || std.max == null || after.min == null || after.max == null) return null
  const lo = Math.min(std.min, after.min), hi = Math.max(std.max, after.max), span = hi - lo || 1
  const at = (v: number) => `${((v - lo) / span) * 100}%`
  const width = (a: number, b: number) => `${Math.max(1.5, ((b - a) / span) * 100)}%`
  return <div className="range" title={`Standard ${num(std.min)} – ${num(std.max)}, mapped ${num(after.min)} – ${num(after.max)}`}>
    <span className="range-row"><i className="base" style={{ left: at(std.min), width: width(std.min, std.max) }} />
      {std.mean != null && <em style={{ left: at(std.mean) }} />}</span>
    <span className="range-row"><i className="new" style={{ left: at(after.min), width: width(after.min, after.max) }} />
      {after.mean != null && <em style={{ left: at(after.mean) }} />}</span>
    <small><span>{num(lo)}</span><span>{num(hi)}</span></small>
  </div>
}

function Samples({ values, compare }: { values?: string[]; compare?: string[] }) {
  if (!values?.length) return null
  return <span className="samples">{values.map((v, i) => compare && compare[i] !== undefined && compare[i] !== v
    ? <mark key={i}>{v}</mark> : <span key={i}>{v}</span>)}</span>
}

function CompareRow({ c }: { c: ComparedColumn }) {
  const different = c.status !== 'missing' && looksDifferent(c.standard, c.after)
  const typeChanged = c.after && !sameKind(c.standard.kind, c.after.kind)
  const nullUp = c.after && c.after.null_rate != null && c.standard.null_rate != null && c.after.null_rate > c.standard.null_rate + 0.001
  const tone = c.status === 'flagged' ? (c.issues.some(i => i.severity === 'critical') ? 'critical' : 'warning') : c.status
  return <div className={`cmp-row ${tone}`}>
    <div className="cmp-cell theirs">
      {c.source ? <><code>{c.source}</code><small className="muted">{c.before?.kind}</small>
        <Samples values={c.before?.samples} /></> : <em className="bad">No column from this source</em>}
    </div>
    <div className="cmp-arrow">
      <span className={`pill cmp-status ${tone}`}>{STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</span>
      {c.steps.length > 0 && <span className="cmp-steps">{c.steps.map((s, i) =>
        <span key={i} className={`step-chip ${s.operation}`} title={s.effect}>{STEP_LABEL[s.operation] || s.operation.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>)}</span>}
      <Icon name="arrow" size={18} />
    </div>
    <div className="cmp-cell ours">
      <code>{c.name}</code>
      <small className={typeChanged ? 'hl' : 'muted'}>{c.after ? c.after.kind : c.standard.kind}{typeChanged && ` (standard: ${c.standard.kind})`}</small>
      <Samples values={c.after?.samples} compare={c.steps.some(s => s.operation !== 'rename_column') ? c.before?.samples : undefined} />
    </div>
    <div className="cmp-cell vs">
      {c.after && c.after.min != null && c.standard.min != null ? <RangeBar std={c.standard} after={c.after} />
        : c.standard.top.length ? <span className="tops">{(c.after?.top.length ? c.after.top : c.standard.top).map(v =>
          <span key={v} className={c.after && !c.standard.top.includes(v) ? 'chip hl' : 'chip'}>{v}</span>)}</span> : null}
      <small className={nullUp ? 'hl' : 'muted'}>Empty: {pct(c.standard.null_rate)} → {c.after ? pct(c.after.null_rate) : 'missing'}</small>
      {different && <small className="hl"><Icon name="alert" size={12} /> Values look different from the standard; no rule flags this</small>}
    </div>
    {c.issues.length > 0 && <ul className="cmp-issues">{c.issues.map((i, n) =>
      <li key={n} className={i.severity} title={i.detail}><span className={`pill ${i.severity}`}>{i.severity}</span> {i.title}
        <small className="muted">rule: {ruleName(i.check)}</small></li>)}</ul>}
  </div>
}

export function MappingCompare({ projectId, source, operations }: { projectId: string; source: Source; operations?: Step[] }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Comparison | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [changesOnly, setChangesOnly] = useState(false)
  const key = JSON.stringify(operations ?? null)
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      api.compareSource(projectId, source.id, operations)
        .then(d => { if (live) { setData(d); setError(null) } }, e => { if (live) setError((e as Error).message) })
    }, 250)
    return () => { live = false; window.clearTimeout(timer) }
  }, [projectId, source.id, key]) // `key` stands in for `operations`, which is a new array on every render
  const rows = useMemo(() => data ? data.columns.filter(c => !changesOnly || c.status !== 'same') : [], [data, changesOnly])

  if (error) return <p className="test-result bad"><Icon name="alert" size={14} /> {error}</p>
  if (!data) return <div className="loading"><span className="spinner" /> Comparing columns…</div>
  const counts = data.columns.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, c) => ({ ...acc, [c.status]: (acc[c.status] || 0) + 1 }), {})
  return <section className="compare" aria-label={`${source.name} columns compared with the standard`}>
    <div className="compare-head">
      <div className="fix-score">
        <div><small>Issues vs the standard</small><b>{data.before.total} <span>→</span> {data.after.total}</b></div>
        <div><small>Rows</small><b>{data.rows.before.toLocaleString()} <span>→</span> {data.rows.after.toLocaleString()}</b></div>
      </div>
      <div className="cmp-legend">{(['same', 'renamed', 'converted', 'flagged', 'missing'] as const).filter(s => counts[s]).map(s =>
        <span key={s} className={`pill cmp-status ${s === 'flagged' ? 'warning' : s}`}>{counts[s]} {STATUS_LABEL[s].toLowerCase()}</span>)}</div>
      <label className="toggle"><input type="checkbox" checked={changesOnly} onChange={e => setChangesOnly(e.target.checked)} /> Only changes</label>
    </div>
    <div className="cmp-grid">
      <div className="cmp-row head"><span>Their column ({source.name})</span><span /><span>Our column (standard)</span><span>Compared with the standard</span></div>
      {rows.map(c => <CompareRow key={c.name} c={c} />)}
      {data.extra.map(e => <div key={e.name} className="cmp-row extra">
        <div className="cmp-cell theirs"><code>{e.name}</code><small className="muted">{e.after.kind}</small><Samples values={e.after.samples} /></div>
        <div className="cmp-arrow"><span className="pill cmp-status warning">Not in the standard</span><Icon name="arrow" size={18} /></div>
        <div className="cmp-cell ours"><em className="muted">Flagged as a new column. Map or drop it.</em></div>
        <div className="cmp-cell vs" />
      </div>)}
      {data.dropped.map(d => <div key={d.name} className="cmp-row dropped">
        <div className="cmp-cell theirs"><code>{d.name}</code></div>
        <div className="cmp-arrow"><span className="pill cmp-status">Dropped</span></div>
        <div className="cmp-cell ours"><em className="muted">{d.effect}</em></div>
        <div className="cmp-cell vs" />
      </div>)}
    </div>
    {data.table_steps.map(s => <p key={s} className="note"><Icon name="info" size={13} /> {s}</p>)}
    {data.table_issues.map((i, n) => <p key={n} className={`note ${i.severity === 'info' ? '' : 'warn'}`}><Icon name="alert" size={13} /> {i.title}: {i.detail} <small className="muted">(rule: {ruleName(i.check)})</small></p>)}
    {data.problems.map(p => <p key={p} className="note warn"><Icon name="alert" size={13} /> {p}</p>)}
    {!data.show_values && <p className="muted small"><Icon name="shield" size={13} /> Sample values are hidden by this project's rules (report.show_values).</p>}
  </section>
}

// Small stroke icon set (24px grid, inherits currentColor) so the UI doesn't depend on font glyphs.
const PATHS: Record<string, string> = {
  plus: 'M12 5v14M5 12h14',

export type IconName = keyof typeof PATHS

export function Icon({ name, size = 16, className }: { name: IconName; size?: number; className?: string }) {
  return <svg className={`icon ${className || ''}`} width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
    strokeWidth={1.8} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d={PATHS[name]} /></svg>
}
