import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { api, API_ORIGIN, fmtDate, plural, runTone, timeAgo, SEVERITIES, TONE_LABEL,
  type Issue, type ProjectDetail, type Run, type Severity, type Triage, type RulesDraft, type ReviewStatus, type ColumnChart } from './api'
import type { Route } from './App'
import { BeforeAfter, NullRates } from './charts'
import { SourceNote, SourcesTab, UnifiedNote } from './sources'
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons'
import { CodeBlock, copyText, Dropzone, Empty, Spinner, useAction, useNotify } from './ui'

export const TABS = ['sources', 'checks', 'baseline', 'rules', 'api'] as const
export type Tab = typeof TABS[number]
const TAB_LABEL: Record<Tab, string> = { sources: 'Sources', checks: 'Results', baseline: 'Standard', rules: 'Rules', api: 'Send data' }
const SEVERITY_ICON: Record<Severity, IconName> = { critical: 'alert', warning: 'flag', info: 'info' }
const FAIL_ON: Record<string, string> = {
  critical: 'Fails on critical issues', warning: 'Fails on warnings or worse', info: 'Fails on any issue', never: 'Never fails',
}

type Props = { route: Route & { id: string }; go: (r: Route) => void; onChanged: () => Promise<unknown>; apiKey?: string; dismissKey: () => void; ai: boolean }

export function ProjectView({ route, go, onChanged, apiKey, dismissKey, ai }: Props) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<ProjectDetail | null>(null)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('') // rules editor text survives tab switches
  const reload = useCallback(() => api.project(route.id).then(d => { setDetail(d); return d }), [route.id])
  useEffect(() => { reload().then(d => setDraft(d.config_yaml), notify.fail) }, [reload, notify])

  const dirty = !!detail && draft !== detail.config_yaml
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) return
    const warn = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => e.preventDefault()
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', warn)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', warn)
  }, [dirty])

  if (!detail) return <div className="loading"><span className="spinner" /> Loading project…</div>

  const latest = detail.runs[0]
  const changed = () => reload().then(onChanged)
  const remove = async () => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${detail.name}" with its baseline and ${plural(detail.runs.length, 'check')}? This can't be undone.`)) return
    try { await api.remove(detail.id); await onChanged(); go({ tab: 'sources' }); notify.ok(`Deleted ${detail.name}`) } catch (e) { notify.fail(e) }
  }

  return <div className="project">
    <header className="project-head">
      <div>
        <h1>{detail.name}</h1>
        <p className="status-line">
          {!detail.has_baseline ? <><i className="dot" /> Waiting for a standard: pick a source on the Sources tab</>
            : latest ? <><i className={`dot ${latest.status}`} /> {TONE_LABEL[runTone(latest)]} · last checked {timeAgo(latest.created_at)}</>
            : <><i className="dot" /> Ready for its first check</>}
          <span className="sep">·</span>{FAIL_ON[detail.fail_on]}
        </p>
      </div>
      <button className="button ghost danger" onClick={remove}><Icon name="trash" size={15} /> Delete</button>
    </header>

    {apiKey && <div className="banner">
      <Icon name="shield" size={18} />
      <div><b>Copy this project's API key now. It won't be shown again.</b><code>{apiKey}</code></div>
      <button className="button outline" onClick={() => copyText(apiKey, notify, 'API key copied')}><Icon name="copy" size={14} /> Copy</button>
      <button className="icon-btn" aria-label="Dismiss" onClick={dismissKey}><Icon name="x" /></button>
    </div>}

    <nav className="tabs" role="tablist">
      {TABS.map(t => <a key={t} role="tab" aria-selected={route.tab === t} href={`#/p/${detail.id}/${t}`} className={route.tab === t ? 'active' : ''}>
        {TAB_LABEL[t]}{t === 'checks' && detail.runs.length > 0 && <span className="count">{detail.runs.length}</span>}
        {t === 'rules' && dirty && <span className="unsaved" title="Unsaved changes" />}
      </a>)}
    </nav>

    {route.tab === 'checks' && <ChecksTab detail={detail} runId={route.run} select={run => go({ ...route, run })} changed={changed} ai={ai} />}
    {route.tab === 'sources' && <SourcesTab detail={detail} changed={changed} ai={ai} openRun={run => go({ id: detail.id, tab: 'checks', run })} />}
    {route.tab === 'baseline' && <BaselineTab detail={detail} changed={changed} />}
    {route.tab === 'rules' && <RulesTab detail={detail} draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} dirty={dirty} changed={changed} ai={ai} />}
    {route.tab === 'api' && <ApiTab detail={detail} apiKey={apiKey} />}
  </div>
}

/* ---------- checks ---------- */

/** Results: every check (uploads, pulls, unifies) with its report and the AI investigation. Data comes in on Sources. */
function ChecksTab({ detail, runId, select, changed, ai }: { detail: ProjectDetail; runId?: string; select: (id?: string) => void; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; ai: boolean }) {
  const viewed = detail.runs.find(r => r.id === runId) || detail.runs[0]
  if (!viewed) return <Empty icon="activity" title="No results yet">
    <span>{detail.has_baseline ? 'Pull a source, unify your sources, or quick-check a file. Every result appears here.'
      : 'Start on Sources: connect your data and pick the standard format.'}</span>
    <a className="button primary" href={`#/p/${detail.id}/sources`}>Go to Sources</a>
  </Empty>
  return <div className="checks-layout">
    <RunHistory runs={detail.runs} selected={viewed.id} select={select} />
    <RunReport key={viewed.id} run={viewed} projectId={detail.id} ai={ai} changed={changed} select={select} />
  </div>
}

function RunHistory({ runs, selected, select }: { runs: Run[]; selected: string; select: (id: string) => void }) {
  const max = Math.max(1, ...runs.map(r => r.issue_count))
  return <aside className="card history">
    <h3>History</h3>
    <div className="bars" aria-hidden="true">
      {[...runs].reverse().map(r => <i key={r.id} className={`${runTone(r)} ${r.id === selected ? 'on' : ''}`}
        style={{ height: `${12 + 88 * r.issue_count / max}%` }} title={`${fmtDate(r.created_at)}: ${plural(r.issue_count, 'issue')}`}
        onClick={() => select(r.id)} />)}
    </div>
    <ul>
      {runs.map(r => <li key={r.id}><button className={r.id === selected ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => select(r.id)}>
        <ToneIcon tone={runTone(r)} />
        <span><b>{r.report.data_file || 'Upload'}</b><small>{fmtDate(r.created_at)}{r.issue_count > 0 && r.review.status !== 'open' && ` · ${REVIEW[r.review.status]}`}</small></span>
        <em className={runTone(r)}>{r.issue_count || '✓'}</em>
      </button></li>)}
    </ul>
  </aside>
}

function RunReport({ run, projectId, ai, changed, select }: { run: Run; projectId: string; ai: boolean; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; select: (id?: string) => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Severity | null>(null)
  const [expandedMap, setExpandedMap] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})
  const { report } = run
  const tone = runTone(run)
  const counts = SEVERITIES.map(s => [s, report.issues.filter(i => i.severity === s).length] as const).filter(([, n]) => n)
  const shown = report.issues.filter(i => !filter || i.severity === filter)

  const issueKey = (issue: Issue, idx: number) => {
    const rawIdx = report.issues.indexOf(issue)
    return `${issue.check}-${issue.columns.join(',')}-${rawIdx >= 0 ? rawIdx : idx}`
  }

  const allExpanded = shown.length > 0 && shown.every((issue, i) => !!expandedMap[issueKey(issue, i)])
  const toggleAll = () => {
    const next = !allExpanded
    const updated: Record<string, boolean> = { ...expandedMap }
    shown.forEach((issue, i) => {
      updated[issueKey(issue, i)] = next
    })
    setExpandedMap(updated)
  }

  return <section className="card report">
    <div className={`verdict ${tone}`}>
      <ToneIcon tone={tone} big />
      <div>
        <h2>{tone === 'failed' ? 'This data would break things' : tone === 'issues' ? 'Passed, with warnings' : 'Everything looks normal'}</h2>
        <p>{report.data_file} · {fmtDate(run.created_at)} · {report.rows.current.toLocaleString()} rows{run.created_by && ` · checked by ${run.created_by}`}</p>
      </div>
      {report.markdown && <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => copyText(report.markdown!, notify, 'Markdown report copied')}>
        <Icon name="copy" size={14} /> Copy report</button>}
    </div>

    {report.issues.length > 0 && <ReviewBar run={run} projectId={projectId} changed={changed} />}
    {report.source && <SourceNote projectId={projectId} run={run} />}
    {report.unified && <UnifiedNote projectId={projectId} run={run} changed={changed} />}
    {report.issues.length > 0 && <InvestigateSection run={run} projectId={projectId} ai={ai} changed={changed} select={select} />}

    {report.issues.length > 0 && <div className="issues-toolbar">
      {counts.length > 1 ? <div className="filters">
        <button className={!filter ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setFilter(null)}>All {report.issues.length}</button>
        {counts.map(([s, n]) => <button key={s} className={`${s} ${filter === s ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setFilter(s)}>
          <Icon name={SEVERITY_ICON[s]} size={13} /> {n} {s}</button>)}
      </div> : <span />}
      {shown.length > 0 && <button className="button ghost small-btn issue-toggle-all" onClick={toggleAll}>
        <Icon name="chevronDown" size={13} className={`issue-chevron ${allExpanded ? 'open' : ''}`} />
        {allExpanded ? 'Collapse all' : 'Expand all'}
      </button>}
    </div>}

    {shown.map((issue, i) => {
      const key = issueKey(issue, i)
      return <IssueCard
        key={key}
        issue={issue}
        charts={report.charts}
        open={!!expandedMap[key]}
        onToggle={() => setExpandedMap(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }))}
      />
    })}
    {report.ai_note && <p className="note"><Icon name="sparkle" size={13} /> {report.ai_note}</p>}
    {report.passed_checks.length > 0 && <p className="passed"><Icon name="check" size={14} /> Passed: {report.passed_checks.map(c => c.replace('_', ' ')).join(', ')}</p>}
  </section>
}

function IssueCard({ issue, charts, open, onToggle }: { issue: Issue; charts?: Record<string, ColumnChart>; open?: boolean; onToggle?: () => void }) {
  const [localOpen, setLocalOpen] = useState(false)
  const isExpanded = open !== undefined ? open : localOpen
  const toggle = onToggle || (() => setLocalOpen(o => !o))
  const measured = Object.entries(issue.measured || {})

  return <article className={`issue ${issue.severity}${isExpanded ? ' expanded' : ' collapsed'}`}>
    <header
      className="issue-head"
      onClick={toggle}
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      aria-expanded={isExpanded}
      aria-label={`${isExpanded ? 'Collapse' : 'Expand'} ${issue.title} message`}
      onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); toggle() } }}
    >
      <div className="issue-title-group">
        <span className={`pill ${issue.severity}`}><Icon name={SEVERITY_ICON[issue.severity]} size={12} /> {issue.severity}</span>
        <h3>{issue.title}</h3>
      </div>
      <div className="issue-toggle-btn" aria-hidden="true">
        <span className="issue-toggle-text">{isExpanded ? 'Hide message' : 'View message'}</span>
        <Icon name="chevronDown" size={14} className={`issue-chevron ${isExpanded ? 'open' : ''}`} />
      </div>
    </header>

    {isExpanded && <div className="issue-body">
      <p className="issue-summary">{issue.measured_summary}</p>
      {issue.check === 'ml_drift' && <DriftBars issue={issue} />}
      <IssueChart issue={issue} charts={charts} />
      {issue.suggested_explanation && <p className="cause"><b>Possible cause</b> {issue.suggested_explanation}</p>}
      {issue.ai_explanation && <p className="cause ai"><b><Icon name="sparkle" size={12} /> AI suggestion</b> {issue.ai_explanation}</p>}
      {issue.consumers.length > 0 && <p className="affects"><b>May affect</b>{issue.consumers.map(c =>
        <span key={c.name} className="chip" title={c.dataset_wide ? 'Reads this dataset' : `Uses ${c.columns.join(', ')}`}>{c.name}</span>)}</p>}
      {(measured.length > 0 || issue.examples.length > 0) && <details>
        <summary>Details</summary>
        <dl>{measured.map(([k, v]) => <div key={k}><dt>{k.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</dt><dd>{fmtValue(v)}</dd></div>)}</dl>
        {issue.examples.length > 0 && <p className="examples">Examples: {issue.examples.join(' · ')}</p>}
        <small className="muted">Check: <code>{issue.check}</code></small>
      </details>}
    </div>}
  </article>
}

/* ---------- shared issue status ---------- */

const REVIEW: Record<ReviewStatus, string> = { open: 'Open', acknowledged: 'Acknowledged', resolved: 'Resolved' }

function ReviewBar({ run, projectId, changed }: { run: Run; projectId: string; changed: () => Promise<unknown> }) {
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const { status, by, at } = run.review
  return <div className="review">
    <div className="segmented" role="group" aria-label="Issue status">
      {(Object.keys(REVIEW) as ReviewStatus[]).map(s => <button key={s} className={`${s} ${status === s ? 'on' : ''}`} disabled={!!busy}
        aria-pressed={status === s} onClick={() => status !== s && act(s, async () => { await api.review(projectId, run.id, s); await changed() })}>
        {REVIEW[s]}</button>)}
    </div>
    <small className="muted">{by ? `${REVIEW[status]} by ${by} · ${timeAgo(at || undefined)}` : 'Nobody has picked this up yet'}</small>
  </div>
}

/** The before/after picture for issues where one exists: value ranges for shifts, empty rates for nulls. */
function IssueChart({ issue, charts }: { issue: Issue; charts?: Record<string, ColumnChart> }) {
  const column = issue.columns[0]
  const chart = column ? charts?.[column] : undefined
  if (!chart) return null
  if ((issue.check === 'distribution' || issue.check === 'ml_drift') && chart.hist) return <BeforeAfter column={column} chart={chart.hist} />
  if (issue.check === 'null_rate') return <NullRates column={column} rates={chart.null_rate} />
  return null
}

/* ---------- Investigate with AI: diagnosis + repair in one pass ---------- */

const VERDICT: Record<Triage['verdict'], { label: string; tone: string; icon: IconName }> = {
  block: { label: 'Block this batch', tone: 'failed', icon: 'alert' },
  investigate: { label: 'Investigate before shipping', tone: 'issues', icon: 'flag' },
  safe_to_ship: { label: 'Safe to ship', tone: 'clean', icon: 'check' },
}
const PART: Record<string, string> = { triage: 'The diagnosis', fix: 'The repair' }

function InvestigateSection({ run, projectId, ai, changed, select }:
  { run: Run; projectId: string; ai: boolean; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; select: (id?: string) => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const { triage, fix } = run.report
  const investigate = () => act('investigate', async () => {
    const result = await api.investigate(projectId, run.id)
    await changed()
    Object.entries(result.errors).forEach(([part, message]) => notify.fail(new Error(`${PART[part]} didn't finish: ${message}`)))
  })
  const recheck = () => act('recheck', async () => {
    const report = await api.checkFixed(projectId, run.id)
    await changed(); select(report.run_id)
    notify.ok(`Fixed file checked: ${plural(report.issues.length, 'issue')} left`)
  })
  const button = <button className="button primary" disabled={!ai || !!busy} onClick={investigate}>
    {busy === 'investigate' ? <Spinner text="Investigating…" /> : <><Icon name="sparkle" size={14} /> {triage && !fix ? 'Repair a copy with AI' : fix && !triage ? 'Diagnose with AI' : 'Investigate with AI'}</>}
  </button>

  if (!triage && !fix) return <div className="triage-cta">
    <span className="triage-icon"><Icon name="sparkle" size={18} /></span>
    <div>
      <b>{busy ? 'Two AI agents are on it…' : 'Investigate with AI'}</b>
      <small>{busy ? 'One finds the cause while the other repairs a copy. About a minute.'
        : ai ? 'Finds what went wrong and who it affects, and repairs a copy of the file. Your original is never changed.'
        : 'AI is off. Set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY on the server to turn it on.'}</small>
    </div>
    {button}
  </div>

  const v = triage && VERDICT[triage.verdict]
  return <section className="triage">
    <header>
      {v ? <span className={`pill verdict ${v.tone}`}><Icon name={v.icon} size={12} /> {v.label}</span>
        : <span className="pill verdict clean"><Icon name="wrench" size={12} /> Repaired copy</span>}
      <small className="muted"><Icon name="sparkle" size={12} /> AI investigation{(triage?.by || fix?.by) && ` · requested by ${triage?.by || fix?.by}`}</small>
    </header>
    {triage && <h3>{triage.headline}</h3>}

    {fix && <div className="fix-summary">
      <b>AI fixed {plural(fix.operations.length, 'problem')}</b>
      <div className="fix-score">
        <div><small>Issues</small><b>{fix.before.total} <span>→</span> {fix.after.total}</b></div>
        <div><small>Critical</small><b>{fix.before.critical} <span>→</span> {fix.after.critical}</b></div>
        <div><small>Rows</small><b>{fix.rows.before.toLocaleString()} <span>→</span> {fix.rows.after.toLocaleString()}</b></div>
      </div>
    </div>}
    <div className="actions">
      {fix && <a className="button primary" href={api.fixedUrl(projectId, run.id)} download={fix.file_name}><Icon name="download" size={14} /> Download fixed file</a>}
      {fix && <button className="button outline" disabled={!!busy} onClick={recheck}>{busy === 'recheck' ? <Spinner text="Checking…" /> : 'Check the fixed file'}</button>}
      {triage && <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => copyText(triage.message, notify, 'Message copied')}><Icon name="copy" size={14} /> Copy message to team</button>}
    </div>

    <div className="folds">
      {triage && triage.root_causes.length > 0 && <Fold title="What went wrong" count={triage.root_causes.length}>
        <p className="muted">{triage.verdict_reason}</p>
        <ol className="causes">{triage.root_causes.map((c, i) => <li key={i}><span className={`conf ${c.confidence}`}>{c.confidence}</span>
          <div><b>{c.hypothesis}</b><small>{c.evidence}</small></div></li>)}</ol>
      </Fold>}
      {fix && fix.operations.length > 0 && <Fold title="What the AI fixed" count={fix.operations.length}>
        <p className="muted">{fix.summary}</p>
        <ol className="causes">{fix.operations.map((op, i) => <li key={i}><span className="conf done">fixed</span>
          <div><b>{op.effect}</b><small>{op.reason}</small></div></li>)}</ol>
        {fix.warnings.map((w, i) => <p key={i} className="note"><Icon name="info" size={13} /> {w}</p>)}
      </Fold>}
      {fix && fix.not_fixed.length > 0 && <Fold title="Left for a person" count={fix.not_fixed.length}>
        <ul className="notify">{fix.not_fixed.map((n, i) => <li key={i}><b>{n.issue}</b> {n.why}</li>)}</ul>
      </Fold>}
      {triage && triage.actions.length > 0 && <Fold title="Next steps" count={triage.actions.length}>
        <ul className="checklist">{triage.actions.map((a, i) => <li key={i}><label><input type="checkbox" /> <span>{a.step}</span> <em>{a.owner}</em></label></li>)}</ul>
      </Fold>}
      {triage && triage.notify.length > 0 && <Fold title="Who to tell" count={triage.notify.length}>
        <ul className="notify">{triage.notify.map((n, i) => <li key={i}><b>{n.who}</b> {n.why}</li>)}</ul>
      </Fold>}
      {triage && <Fold title="Message to send"><CodeBlock code={triage.message} label="Copy message" /></Fold>}
      {triage && triage.steps.length > 0 && <Fold title="How the agent investigated" count={triage.steps.length}>
        <ol className="agent-steps">{triage.steps.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{describeStep(s)}</li>)}</ol>
      </Fold>}
    </div>
    {(!triage || !fix) && <div className="actions"><small className="muted">{triage ? 'No repaired copy yet.' : 'No diagnosis yet.'}</small>{button}</div>}
  </section>
}

/** A collapsed section of the AI panel: title and count, opened on click. */
function Fold({ title, count, children }: { title: string; count?: number; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <details className="fold">
    <summary><span>{title}</span>{count !== undefined && <span className="count">{count}</span>}</summary>
    <div className="fold-body">{children}</div>
  </details>
}

/** One tool call by the triage agent, in words. */
function describeStep(step: Triage['steps'][number]) {
  const input = step.input || {}
  const column = typeof input.column === 'string' ? input.column : null
  switch (step.tool) {
    case 'list_issues': return 'Listed the issues the checks found'
    case 'get_column_stats': return column ? <>Compared column <code>{column}</code> with the baseline</> : 'Compared a column with the baseline'
    case 'get_run_history': return 'Looked at earlier checks of this data'
    case 'get_consumers': return 'Looked up who uses this data'
    default: return <><code>{step.tool}</code>{Object.values(input).length > 0 && ` ${Object.values(input).map(String).join(', ')}`}</>
  }
}

/** The drift model's column drivers, parsed from "amount (90%), quantity (9%)". */
function DriftBars({ issue }: { issue: Issue }) {
  const drivers = [...String(issue.measured.top_drivers || '').matchAll(/([^,]+?) \((\d+)%\)/g)].map(m => [m[1].trim(), Number(m[2])] as const)
  const auc = Number(issue.measured.drift_auc)
  return <div className="drift">
    <div className="gauge" title="0.5 = the model can't tell the files apart, 1.0 = it always can">
      <small>Drift score</small><b>{auc.toFixed(2)}</b>
      <span className="track"><i style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, (auc - 0.5) / 0.5) * 100}%` }} /></span>
      <small className="muted">0.5 same · 1.0 different</small>
    </div>
    <div className="drivers"><small>What gave it away</small>
      {drivers.map(([col, pct]) => <div key={col} className="driver"><code>{col}</code><span className="track"><i style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} /></span><b>{pct}%</b></div>)}
    </div>
  </div>
}

const fmtValue = (v: unknown) => v === null || v === undefined ? 'n/a' : typeof v === 'boolean' ? (v ? 'yes' : 'no')
  : typeof v === 'number' ? v.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 4 }) : String(v)

function ToneIcon({ tone, big }: { tone: ReturnType<typeof runTone>; big?: boolean }) {
  return <span className={`tone ${tone} ${big ? 'big' : ''}`}><Icon name={tone === 'failed' ? 'alert' : tone === 'issues' ? 'flag' : 'check'} size={big ? 20 : 13} /></span>
}

/* ---------- baseline ---------- */

function BaselineTab({ detail, changed }: { detail: ProjectDetail; changed: () => Promise<unknown> }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const b = detail.baseline
  if (!b) return <Empty icon="database" title="No standard yet"><span>The standard is the format every source is converted to.</span><a className="button primary" href={`#/p/${detail.id}/sources`}>Pick a source</a></Empty>
  const columns = Object.entries(b.columns).filter(([name]) => name.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()))

  const replace = (file: File) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Replace baseline v${b.baseline_version} with ${file.name}? Future checks will compare against the new file.`)) return
    run('replace', async () => { await api.upload(detail.id, 'baseline', file); await changed(); notify.ok('Baseline replaced') })
  }

  return <>
    <div className="card facts">
      <div><small>Version</small><b>v{b.baseline_version}</b></div>
      <div><small>Rows</small><b>{b.rows.toLocaleString()}</b></div>
      <div><small>Columns</small><b>{Object.keys(b.columns).length}</b></div>
      <div><small>From file</small><b title={b.source}>{b.source || '—'}</b></div>
      <div><small>Saved</small><b>{fmtDate(b.created_at)}</b></div>
    </div>
    <div className="card">
      <div className="card-head"><h2>Columns</h2>
        {Object.keys(b.columns).length > 8 && <input className="search" placeholder="Filter columns…" value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} aria-label="Filter columns" />}</div>
      <div className="table-wrap"><table>
        <thead><tr><th>Column</th><th>Type</th><th>Empty values</th><th className="num">Distinct values</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>{columns.map(([name, s]) => <tr key={name}>
          <td><code>{name}</code></td>
          <td>{s.kind} <small className="muted">{s.dtype}</small></td>
          <td><span className="meter"><i style={{ width: `${(s.null_rate ?? 0) * 100}%` }} /></span> {((s.null_rate ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(1)}%</td>
          <td className="num">{s.unique_count?.toLocaleString() ?? '—'}</td>
        </tr>)}</tbody>
      </table>{!columns.length && <p className="muted pad">No columns match "{query}".</p>}</div>
    </div>
    <div className="card">
      <h2>Replace the standard with a file</h2>
      <p className="muted">Do this after an intentional change to the data. The version number goes up and older checks keep their results.</p>
      <Dropzone title="Drop the new known-good file" hint="You'll be asked to confirm" busy={busy && 'Profiling…'} onFile={replace} />
    </div>
  </>
}

/* ---------- rules ---------- */

const CHEATSHEET: Array<[string, string]> = [
  ['fail_on', 'Which severity fails a check: critical, warning, info or never'],
  ['schema', 'Missing columns, new columns, type changes'],
  ['row_count', 'Row count change beyond max_change_pct, or below min_rows'],
  ['null_rate', 'Empty values rising by more than max_increase (0.05 = 5 points)'],
  ['categorical', 'Text values never seen in the baseline'],
  ['duplicates', 'Repeated key columns or whole rows'],
  ['distribution', 'Numbers shifting (PSI and mean shift)'],
  ['ml_drift', 'A model learns to tell new rows from baseline rows; max_auc is the limit (0.5 = same)'],
  ['consumers', 'Dashboards, models or services and the columns they read'],
]

function RulesTab({ detail, draft, setDraft, dirty, changed, ai }:
  { detail: ProjectDetail; draft: string; setDraft: (s: string) => void; dirty: boolean; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; ai: boolean }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [suggested, setSuggested] = useState('')
  const [ask, setAsk] = useState('')
  const [proposal, setProposal] = useState<RulesDraft | null>(null)
  const save = () => run('save', async () => { await api.saveRules(detail.id, draft); await changed(); notify.ok('Rules saved') })

  const drafting = () => run('draft', async () => setProposal(await api.draftRules(detail.id, ask, draft)))

  return <div className="rules-layout">
    <div className="card">
      <form className="ask" onSubmit={e => { e.preventDefault(); if (ask.trim().length > 2) drafting() }}>
        <label htmlFor="ask"><Icon name="sparkle" size={14} /> Describe a change in plain English</label>
        <div className="row">
          <input id="ask" value={ask} onChange={e => setAsk(e.target.value)} disabled={!ai}
            placeholder={ai ? 'e.g. order_id must be unique, and alert if amount shifts a lot' : 'Set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY on the server to use this'} />
          <button className="button primary" disabled={!ai || ask.trim().length < 3 || !!busy}>{busy === 'draft' ? <Spinner text="Drafting…" /> : 'Draft'}</button>
        </div>
      </form>
      {proposal && <div className="proposal">
        <b>{proposal.summary}</b>
        {proposal.not_supported.map(n => <p key={n} className="muted"><Icon name="info" size={13} /> Not possible as a rule: {n}</p>)}
        <details><summary>Preview the full file</summary><CodeBlock code={proposal.config_yaml} /></details>
        <div className="actions">
          <button className="button primary" onClick={() => { setDraft(proposal.config_yaml); setProposal(null); setAsk('') }}>Put in editor</button>
          <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => setProposal(null)}>Discard</button>
        </div>
      </div>}
      <div className="card-head">
        <h2>datapulse.yml</h2>
        <span className="muted">{dirty ? 'Unsaved changes' : 'Saved'}</span>
      </div>
      <textarea className="editor" value={draft} onChange={e => setDraft(e.target.value)} spellCheck={false} aria-label="Rules (YAML)"
        onKeyDown={e => { if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') { e.preventDefault(); if (dirty) save() } }} />
      <div className="actions">
        <button className="button primary" onClick={save} disabled={!dirty || !!busy}>{busy === 'save' ? <Spinner text="Saving…" /> : 'Save rules'}</button>
        <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => setDraft(detail.config_yaml)} disabled={!dirty}>Discard changes</button>
        <span className="muted hint">Ctrl+S to save</span>
      </div>
      {suggested && <><h3>Suggested from your baseline</h3><p className="muted">Nothing was applied. Copy the parts you want into the editor.</p><CodeBlock code={suggested} /></>}
    </div>
    <aside>
      <div className="card">
        <h3>Who uses this data</h3>
        {detail.consumers.length ? <ul className="consumers">{detail.consumers.map(c => <li key={c.name}>
          <b>{c.name}</b><small>{c.type}{c.owner && ` · ${c.owner}`}</small><span>{c.columns.join(', ')}</span>
        </li>)}</ul> : <p className="muted">None yet. Add a <code>consumers:</code> section so each issue shows what it may break.</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="card">
        <h3>Quick reference</h3>
        <dl className="cheats">{CHEATSHEET.map(([k, v]) => <div key={k}><dt><code>{k}</code></dt><dd>{v}</dd></div>)}</dl>
        {detail.has_baseline && <button className="button outline full" disabled={!!busy}
          onClick={() => run('suggest', async () => setSuggested((await api.suggestions(detail.id)).yaml))}>
          {busy === 'suggest' ? <Spinner text="Thinking…" /> : <><Icon name="sparkle" size={14} /> Suggest rules from baseline</>}</button>}
      </div>
    </aside>
  </div>
}

/* ---------- send data from other services ---------- */

function ApiTab({ detail, apiKey }: { detail: ProjectDetail; apiKey?: string }) {
  const url = `${API_ORIGIN}/api/projects/${detail.id}/ingest`
  const curl = `curl -X POST ${url} \\
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  -H 'X-DataPulse-Key: ${apiKey || 'YOUR_PROJECT_API_KEY'}' \\
  -d '{"dataset_name": "orders", "role": "candidate",
       "rows": [{"order_id": 1, "amount": 22.5, "status": "paid"}]}'`
  return <div className="card prose">
    <h2>Send rows from any service</h2>
    <p>Any language that can make an HTTP request can run a check. The response is the same JSON report the CLI writes with <code>--json</code>.</p>
    <CodeBlock code={curl} label="Copy command" />
    <ul>
      <li><code>"role": "baseline"</code> replaces the baseline; <code>"candidate"</code> (the default) runs a check.</li>
      <li>Up to 50,000 rows per request. For bigger files, use Quick check on the Sources tab, or the CLI / GitHub Action.</li>
      <li>The API key was shown once, when the project was created. Only its hash is stored.</li>
    </ul>
    <a className="link" href={`${API_ORIGIN}/docs`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Interactive API docs <Icon name="external" size={13} /></a>
  </div>
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, plural, runTone, timeAgo, type Destination, type DestinationForm, type Mapping, type ProjectDetail, type Run, type Source, type SourceKind, type Step } from './api'
import { FlowDiagram, HealthCards, lastCheckOf, MappingCompare } from './flow'
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons'
import { Dropzone, Empty, Modal, Spinner, useAction, useNotify } from './ui'

const STATUS: Record<Source['status'], { label: string; tone: string }> = {
  standard: { label: 'Standard', tone: 'clean' },
  mapped: { label: 'Mapped', tone: 'clean' },
  needs_mapping: { label: 'Needs mapping', tone: 'issues' },
}

let catalogCache: Promise<SourceKind[]> | null = null
function useCatalog() {
  const [kinds, setKinds] = useState<SourceKind[]>([])
  useEffect(() => {
    catalogCache ??= api.sourceCatalog().catch(e => { catalogCache = null; throw e })
    catalogCache.then(setKinds, () => {})
  }, [])
  return kinds
}
const labelOf = (kinds: SourceKind[], kind: string) => kinds.find(k => k.kind === kind)?.label || kind
const KIND_ICON: Record<string, IconName> = {
  database: 'database', file: 'upload', link: 'external', rest: 'code', google_drive: 'external', web_page: 'activity',
}

const FILE_TYPES = '.csv,.json,.jsonl,.ndjson,.parquet,.pq,.xlsx,.xls,.pdf,.docx,.txt,.md,.markdown,.eml,.html,.htm,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.gif'
const NAME_HINT: Record<string, string> = {
  database: 'Store database', file: 'March export', link: 'Sales sheet', rest: 'Shop API', google_drive: 'Shared orders file', web_page: 'Supplier page',
}

/** Mirrors the server's detection, to show which database a pasted URL points at. */
const DETECTED: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
  [/^mongodb(\+srv)?:/i, 'MongoDB'], [/^(postgres(ql)?|redshift|timescaledb)(\+\w+)?:/i, 'PostgreSQL'],
  [/^(mysql|mariadb)(\+\w+)?:/i, 'MySQL / MariaDB'], [/^(mssql|sqlserver)(\+\w+)?:/i, 'Microsoft SQL Server'],
  [/^snowflake:/i, 'Snowflake'], [/^bigquery:/i, 'BigQuery'], [/^oracle(\+\w+)?:/i, 'Oracle'], [/^databricks:/i, 'Databricks'],
]
const detected = (url: string) => url.includes('://') ? DETECTED.find(([re]) => re.test(url.trim()))?.[1] || 'SQL database (driver must be installed on the server)' : null

/* ---------- the Sources tab ---------- */

export function SourcesTab({ detail, changed, ai, openRun }:
  { detail: ProjectDetail; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; ai: boolean; openRun: (run?: string) => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const kinds = useCatalog()
  const [list, setList] = useState<Source[] | null>(null)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Source | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const reload = useCallback(() => api.sources(detail.id).then(setList), [detail.id])
  useEffect(() => { reload().catch(notify.fail) }, [reload, notify])
  const refresh = () => Promise.all([reload(), changed()])

  if (!list) return <div className="loading"><span className="spinner" /> Loading sources…</div>
  const ready = list.filter(s => s.status !== 'needs_mapping')
  const unify = () => act('unify', async () => {
    const report = await api.unify(detail.id)
    await refresh(); openRun(report.run_id)
    const sent = (report.delivered || []).filter(d => d.ok).length, held = (report.delivered || []).some(d => d.held)
    notify.ok(`Unified ${plural(report.rows.current, 'row')}: ${report.issues.length ? plural(report.issues.length, 'issue') + ' to review' : 'all clear'}`
      + (sent ? ` · sent to ${plural(sent, 'destination')}` : held ? ' · held back from your database' : ''))
  })

  const lastUnified = detail.runs.find(r => r.report.unified)
  const steps: Array<[string, boolean]> = [
    ['Connect your sources', list.length > 0],
    ['Pick the standard format', detail.has_baseline],
    ['Map the others to it', list.length > 1 && list.every(s => s.status !== 'needs_mapping')],
    ['Unify into one table', !!lastUnified],
  ]
  const next = steps.findIndex(([, done]) => !done)

  return <>
    <div className="card sources-intro">
      <div>
        <h2>Unify your data</h2>
        <ol className="progress">{steps.map(([label, done], i) =>
          <li key={label} className={done ? 'done' : i === next ? 'next' : ''}><span>{done ? <Icon name="check" size={12} /> : i + 1}</span>{label}</li>)}</ol>
      </div>
      <div className="actions">
        <button className={`button ${list.length ? 'outline' : 'primary'}`} onClick={() => setEditing('new')}><Icon name="plus" size={14} /> Add source</button>
        {detail.has_baseline && <button className="button primary" disabled={!ready.length || !!busy} onClick={unify}
          title={ready.length ? undefined : 'Map a source first'}>
          {busy === 'unify' ? <Spinner text="Unifying…" /> : <><Icon name="database" size={14} /> Unify {ready.length > 1 ? `all ${ready.length} sources` : 'sources'}</>}
        </button>}
      </div>
    </div>

    <HealthCards detail={detail} sources={list} />
    {list.length > 0 && <FlowDiagram detail={detail} sources={list} icon={k => KIND_ICON[k] || 'database'} openRun={openRun}
      unify={unify} unifying={busy === 'unify'} canUnify={ready.length > 0} />}

    {list.length === 0 ? <Empty icon="plug" title="No sources yet">
      <span>Any database, a file, a link, a REST API, Google Drive or a web page. Use test data to try one without your own credentials.</span>
    </Empty> : <div className="source-list">
      {list.map(s => <SourceCard key={s.id} source={s} kinds={kinds} detail={detail} ai={ai}
        refresh={refresh} edit={() => setEditing(s)} openRun={openRun} />)}
    </div>}

    {detail.has_baseline && <QuickCheck detail={detail} changed={changed} openRun={openRun} />}
    {detail.has_baseline && <Destinations projectId={detail.id} />}

    {editing && <SourceDialog projectId={detail.id} kinds={kinds} source={editing === 'new' ? null : editing}
      close={() => setEditing(null)} saved={() => { setEditing(null); reload() }} />}
  </>
}

function SourceCard({ source: s, kinds, detail, ai, refresh, edit, openRun }:
  { source: Source; kinds: SourceKind[]; detail: ProjectDetail; ai: boolean; refresh: () => Promise<unknown>; edit: () => void; openRun: (run?: string) => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const [comparing, setComparing] = useState(false)
  const status = STATUS[s.status]
  const lastRun = lastCheckOf(detail, s)
  const pull = (role: 'baseline' | 'candidate') => act(role, async () => {
    if (role === 'baseline' && detail.has_baseline && !window.confirm(`Make "${s.name}" the new standard? Other sources' recipes may need re-mapping.`)) return
    const report = await api.pullSource(detail.id, s.id, role)
    await refresh()
    if (role === 'baseline') notify.ok(`${s.name} is now the standard format`)
    else { openRun(report.run_id); notify.ok(report.status === 'failed' ? `${s.name}: ${plural(report.issues.length, 'issue')} to review` : `${s.name} passed`) }
  })
  const map = () => act('map', async () => { await api.mapSource(detail.id, s.id); await refresh() })
  const remove = () => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Remove the source "${s.name}"? Past checks are kept.`)) return
    act('remove', async () => { await api.removeSource(detail.id, s.id); await refresh() })
  }

  return <article className="card source" id={`source-${s.id}`}>
    <header>
      <span className="source-icon"><Icon name={KIND_ICON[s.kind] || 'database'} size={18} /></span>
      <div>
        <h3>{s.name}</h3>
        <small className="muted">{s.type || labelOf(kinds, s.kind)}{s.last_pulled_at ? ` · pulled ${timeAgo(s.last_pulled_at)}` : ' · never pulled'}
          {lastRun && <> · last check <span className={`flow-status ${runTone(lastRun)}`}>{lastRun.issue_count ? plural(lastRun.issue_count, 'issue') : 'passed'}</span></>}</small>
      </div>
      <span className={`pill verdict ${status.tone}`}>{status.label}{s.status === 'mapped' && s.recipe.length > 0 && ` · ${plural(s.recipe.length, 'step')}`}</span>
    </header>

    {s.proposal && <MappingReview proposal={s.proposal} projectId={detail.id} source={s} refresh={refresh} />}
    {!s.proposal && comparing && <MappingCompare projectId={detail.id} source={s} operations={s.recipe} />}

    <div className="actions">
      {s.proposal ? null : !detail.has_baseline ? <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => pull('baseline')}>
        {busy === 'baseline' ? <Spinner text="Learning the format…" /> : 'Use as the standard'}</button>
        : s.status === 'needs_mapping' ? <button className="button primary ai" disabled={!!busy} onClick={map}>
          {busy === 'map' ? <Spinner text="Mapping…" /> : <><Icon name="sparkle" size={14} /> Map to the standard</>}</button>
        : <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => pull('candidate')}>
          {busy === 'candidate' ? <Spinner text="Pulling…" /> : 'Pull & check'}</button>}
      {s.last_run_id && <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => openRun(s.last_run_id!)}>Last check</button>}
      {s.status === 'mapped' && <button className="button ghost" disabled={!!busy} onClick={map}>{busy === 'map' ? <Spinner text="Mapping…" /> : 'Re-map'}</button>}
      {!s.proposal && detail.has_baseline && s.status !== 'standard' && <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => setComparing(c => !c)}>
        {comparing ? 'Hide columns' : 'Compare columns'}</button>}
      {detail.has_baseline && s.status !== 'standard' && <button className="button ghost" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => pull('baseline')}>Make standard</button>}
      <span className="spacer" />
      <button className="button ghost" onClick={edit}>Edit</button>
      <button className="button ghost danger" disabled={!!busy} onClick={remove} aria-label={`Remove ${s.name}`}><Icon name="trash" size={14} /></button>
    </div>
    {s.status === 'needs_mapping' && !s.proposal && !ai && detail.has_baseline &&
      <small className="muted">AI is off: only columns with matching names will be mapped.</small>}
  </article>
}

/* ---------- reviewing a proposed mapping ---------- */

const describe = (op: Step) => op.effect || `${op.operation.replace(/_/g, ' ')} ${Object.values(op.args).join(' → ')}`

function MappingReview({ proposal: p, projectId, source, refresh }: { proposal: Mapping; projectId: string; source: Source; refresh: () => Promise<unknown> }) {
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [keep, setKeep] = useState(() => p.operations.map(() => true))
  const chosen = p.operations.filter((_, i) => keep[i])
  const approve = () => act('approve', async () => {
    await api.saveRecipe(projectId, source.id, chosen.map(({ effect: _effect, ...step }) => step))
    await refresh()
    notify.ok(`${source.name} is mapped. Every pull now converts it automatically.`)
  })
  const discard = () => act('discard', async () => { await api.discardMapping(projectId, source.id); await refresh() })

  return <section className="triage mapping">
    <header>
      <span className="pill verdict issues"><Icon name="sparkle" size={12} /> Proposed mapping</span>
      <small className="muted">{p.model ? 'AI + name matching' : 'Name matching'}</small>
    </header>
    <div className="fix-score">
      <div><small>Issues against the standard</small><b>{p.before.total} <span>→</span> {p.after.total}</b></div>
      <div><small>Rows</small><b>{p.rows.before.toLocaleString()} <span>→</span> {p.rows.after.toLocaleString()}</b></div>
    </div>
    <p>{p.summary}</p>
    <h4>Their column → our column · updates as you choose steps</h4>
    <MappingCompare projectId={projectId} source={source} operations={chosen.map(({ effect: _effect, ...step }) => step)} />
    <h4>Steps · uncheck any you don't want</h4>
    <ul className="recipe">{p.operations.map((op, i) => <li key={i}>
      <label><input type="checkbox" checked={keep[i]} onChange={e => setKeep(k => k.map((v, j) => j === i ? e.target.checked : v))} />
        <span><b>{describe(op)}</b><small>{i < p.automatic_steps ? 'Matched by name' : op.reason}</small></span></label>
    </li>)}</ul>
    {p.not_fixed.length > 0 && <><h4>Not mapped</h4>
      <ul className="notify">{p.not_fixed.map((n, i) => <li key={i}><b>{n.issue}</b> {n.why}</li>)}</ul></>}
    {p.after.remaining.length > 0 && <p className="note"><Icon name="info" size={13} /> Still flagged after mapping: {p.after.remaining.join('; ')}</p>}
    <div className="actions">
      <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy} onClick={approve}>{busy === 'approve' ? <Spinner text="Saving…" /> : `Approve ${plural(chosen.length, 'step')}`}</button>
      <button className="button ghost" disabled={!!busy} onClick={discard}>Discard</button>
    </div>
  </section>
}

/* ---------- add / edit a source ---------- */

function SourceDialog({ projectId, kinds, source, close, saved }:
  { projectId: string; kinds: SourceKind[]; source: Source | null; close: () => void; saved: () => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const [kind, setKind] = useState(source?.kind || '')
  const [name, setName] = useState(source?.name || '')
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
    const { saved_secrets: _saved, ...rest } = source?.settings || {}
    return rest as Record<string, string>
  })
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; message: string } | null>(null)
  const plugin = kinds.find(k => k.kind === kind)
  const savedSecrets = source?.settings.saved_secrets || []

  const settings = () => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(values).filter(([k]) => plugin?.fields.some(f => f.name === k)))
  const test = () => act('test', async () => setResult(await api.testSource(projectId, kind, settings(), source?.id)))
  const fill = () => act('sample', async () => {
    const sample = await api.sourceSample(projectId, kind)
    setName(current => current.trim() ? current : sample.name)
    setValues(current => ({ ...current, ...sample.settings }))
    setResult(null)
  })
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    act('save', async () => {
      const form = { name: name.trim() || plugin!.label, kind, settings: settings() }
      if (source) await api.editSource(projectId, source.id, form)
      else await api.addSource(projectId, form)
      notify.ok(`${form.name} saved`)
      saved()
    })
  }

  const upload = (field: string, file: File) => act('upload', async () => {
    const stored = await api.uploadSourceFile(projectId, file)
    setValues(v => ({ ...v, [field]: stored.file }))
    setResult(stored.note ? { ok: true, message: stored.note } : null)
    if (!name.trim()) setName(stored.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''))
  })
  const choices = kinds.filter(k => !k.hidden)

  return <Modal title={source ? `Edit ${source.name}` : 'Add a data source'} onClose={close}>
    {!plugin ? <div className="kind-picker">
      <p className="muted">Where does this data live?</p>
      <div className="kinds big">{choices.map(k => <button key={k.kind} type="button" className="kind"
        disabled={!k.available} title={k.available ? undefined : `Needs ${k.missing_packages.join(', ')} on the server`}
        onClick={() => { setKind(k.kind); setValues({}); setResult(null) }}>
        <span className="source-icon"><Icon name={KIND_ICON[k.kind] || 'database'} size={20} /></span>
        <b>{k.label}</b><small>{k.available ? k.description : 'Not installed on this server'}</small>
      </button>)}</div>
    </div> : <form className="source-form" onSubmit={submit}>
      {!source && <button type="button" className="link back" onClick={() => setKind('')}>← {plugin.label}</button>}
      <label>Name<input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} maxLength={80}
        placeholder={`e.g. ${NAME_HINT[kind] || 'Sales data'}`} /></label>
      {plugin.fields.map(f => <label key={f.name}>{f.label}{!f.required && <small className="muted"> optional</small>}
        {f.input === 'file'
          ? <span className="file-pick">
            <input type="file" accept={FILE_TYPES} disabled={!!busy}
              onChange={e => { const file = e.target.files?.[0]; e.target.value = ''; if (file) upload(f.name, file) }} />
            <small className="muted">{busy === 'upload' ? 'Uploading… documents are read by the AI, which can take a minute' : values[f.name] ? `Uploaded: ${values[f.name].split('/').pop()}` : f.placeholder}</small>
          </span>
          : f.multiline
          ? <textarea rows={3} value={values[f.name] || ''} placeholder={f.placeholder} spellCheck={false}
            onChange={e => { setValues(v => ({ ...v, [f.name]: e.target.value })); setResult(null) }} />
          : <input type={f.secret ? 'password' : undefined} value={values[f.name] || ''} autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false}
            placeholder={f.secret && savedSecrets.includes(f.name) ? 'Saved. Leave empty to keep it' : f.placeholder}
            onChange={e => { setValues(v => ({ ...v, [f.name]: e.target.value })); setResult(null) }} />}
        {kind === 'database' && f.name === 'url' && values.url && detected(values.url)
          ? <small className="detected"><Icon name="check" size={12} /> {detected(values.url)}</small>
          : f.help && <small className="muted">{f.help}</small>}
      </label>)}
      {plugin.fields.some(f => f.secret) && <p className="muted small"><Icon name="shield" size={13} /> Passwords stay on the DataPulse server and are never shown again. A read-only database user is best.</p>}
      {result && <p className={`test-result ${result.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'}`}><Icon name={result.ok ? 'check' : 'alert'} size={14} /> {result.message}</p>}
      <div className="actions">
        {plugin.has_sample && <button type="button" className="button ghost" disabled={!!busy} onClick={fill}>
          {busy === 'sample' ? <Spinner text="Preparing…" /> : 'Use test data'}</button>}
        <button type="button" className="button outline" disabled={!!busy} onClick={test}>
          {busy === 'test' ? <Spinner text={kind === 'file' ? 'Reading…' : 'Connecting…'} /> : kind === 'file' ? 'Read the file' : 'Test connection'}</button>
        <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy}>{busy === 'save' ? <Spinner text="Saving…" /> : source ? 'Save changes' : 'Add source'}</button>
      </div>
    </form>}
  </Modal>
}

/* ---------- where a check's data came from ---------- */

export function SourceNote({ projectId, run }: { projectId: string; run: Run }) {
  const kinds = useCatalog()
  const s = run.report.source!
  return <div className="source-note">
    <Icon name="database" size={15} />
    <span>Pulled {s.rows_pulled.toLocaleString()} rows from <b>{s.name}</b> ({s.type || labelOf(kinds, s.kind)})
      {s.mapped ? (s.steps.length ? `, converted to the standard in ${plural(s.steps.length, 'step')}` : '') : '. Not mapped to the standard yet.'}</span>
    <a className="link" href={api.runDataUrl(projectId, run.id)}><Icon name="download" size={13} /> Data</a>
    {s.problems.map(p => <p key={p} className="note warn"><Icon name="alert" size={13} /> The source changed shape. {p}</p>)}
  </div>
}

export function UnifiedNote({ projectId, run, changed }: { projectId: string; run: Run; changed: () => Promise<unknown> }) {
  const kinds = useCatalog()
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const delivered = run.report.delivered || []
  const resend = () => act('send', async () => {
    const { delivered } = await api.sendRun(projectId, run.id)
    await changed()
    if (!delivered.length) notify.ok('Add a destination on the Sources tab first')
    else if (delivered.every(d => d.ok)) notify.ok(`Sent to ${plural(delivered.length, 'destination')}`)
    else notify.fail(new Error(delivered.find(d => !d.ok)!.message))
  })
  return <div className="source-note unified">
    <Icon name="database" size={15} />
    <span>Combined from {plural(run.report.unified!.sources.filter(s => !s.error).length, 'source')}, each converted to the standard.</span>
    <a className="button outline" href={api.runDataUrl(projectId, run.id)}><Icon name="download" size={14} /> Download unified table</a>
    <ul>{run.report.unified!.sources.map(s => <li key={s.name}>
      <b>{s.name}</b> <small className="muted">{s.type || labelOf(kinds, s.kind)}</small>
      {s.error ? <em className="bad">{s.error}</em> : <span>{s.rows.toLocaleString()} rows</span>}
      {s.problems.map(p => <em key={p} className="bad">{p}</em>)}
    </li>)}</ul>
    {delivered.length > 0 && <ul className="deliveries">{delivered.map(d => <li key={d.id} className={d.ok ? 'ok' : d.held ? 'held' : 'bad'}>
      <Icon name={d.ok ? 'check' : d.held ? 'shield' : 'alert'} size={13} /><b>{d.name}</b><span>{d.message}</span>
    </li>)}</ul>}
    {run.status === 'passed' && (!delivered.length || delivered.some(d => !d.ok)) &&
      <button className="button ghost" disabled={!!busy} onClick={resend}>{busy ? <Spinner text="Sending…" /> : <><Icon name="upload" size={14} /> Send to your database</>}</button>}
  </div>
}

/* ---------- quick check: one file against the standard, without adding it as a source ---------- */

function QuickCheck({ detail, changed, openRun }: { detail: ProjectDetail; changed: () => Promise<unknown>; openRun: (run?: string) => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, run] = useAction()
  const [useAi, setUseAi] = useState(false)
  const check = (file: File) => run('check', async () => {
    const report = await api.upload(detail.id, 'runs', file, useAi)
    await changed()
    openRun(report.run_id)
    notify.ok(report.status === 'failed' ? `Check failed: ${plural(report.issues.length, 'issue')} to review` : 'Check passed')
  })
  return <section className="card quick-check">
    <div className="card-head"><div>
      <h2>Quick check a file</h2>
      <p className="muted">Check one file against the standard without adding it as a source. The result opens in Results.</p>
    </div></div>
    <Dropzone title="Drop a file to check it once" hint={`Compared with standard v${detail.baseline?.baseline_version} · CSV, Excel, JSON or Parquet`}
      busy={busy && 'Checking your data…'} onFile={check} />
    <label className="toggle"><input type="checkbox" checked={useAi} onChange={e => setUseAi(e.target.checked)} />
      Add AI explanations to each issue</label>
  </section>
}

/* ---------- destinations: send unified data into the company's own database ---------- */

function Destinations({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [list, setList] = useState<Destination[] | null>(null)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Destination | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const reload = useCallback(() => api.destinations(projectId).then(setList), [projectId])
  useEffect(() => { reload().catch(notify.fail) }, [reload, notify])
  if (!list) return null
  const remove = (d: Destination) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Stop sending to "${d.name}"? Data already sent stays in your database.`)) return
    act(d.id, async () => { await api.removeDestination(projectId, d.id); await reload() })
  }
  return <section className="card destinations">
    <div className="card-head">
      <div><h2>Send unified data to</h2>
        <p className="muted">Every time you unify, the result is written into your own database, but only if it passes its checks.</p></div>
      <button className="button outline" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}><Icon name="plus" size={14} /> Add destination</button>
    </div>
    {list.length === 0 ? <p className="muted">No destination yet. Unified data can still be downloaded.</p>
      : <ul className="destination-list">{list.map(d => <li key={d.id}>
        <span className="source-icon"><Icon name="database" size={16} /></span>
        <div><b>{d.name}</b>
          <small className="muted">{d.type} · table <code>{d.table}</code> · {d.mode === 'replace' ? 'replaced each time' : 'new rows added each time'}</small>
          {d.last_result && <small className="muted">Last: {d.last_result} · {timeAgo(d.last_sent_at || undefined)}</small>}
        </div>
        <button className="button ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(d)}>Edit</button>
        <button className="button ghost danger" disabled={busy === d.id} onClick={() => remove(d)} aria-label={`Remove ${d.name}`}><Icon name="trash" size={14} /></button>
      </li>)}</ul>}
    {editing && <DestinationDialog projectId={projectId} destination={editing === 'new' ? null : editing}
      close={() => setEditing(null)} saved={() => { setEditing(null); reload() }} />}
  </section>
}

function DestinationDialog({ projectId, destination, close, saved }:
  { projectId: string; destination: Destination | null; close: () => void; saved: () => void }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  const [busy, act] = useAction()
  const [form, setForm] = useState<DestinationForm>({ name: destination?.name || '', url: '', table: destination?.table || 'unified_data', mode: destination?.mode || 'replace' })
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; message: string } | null>(null)
  const set = (k: keyof DestinationForm, v: string) => { setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v })); setResult(null) }
  const test = () => act('test', async () => setResult(await api.testDestination(projectId, form, destination?.id)))
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    act('save', async () => {
      const body = { ...form, name: form.name.trim() || 'My database' }
      if (destination) await api.editDestination(projectId, destination.id, body)
      else await api.addDestination(projectId, body)
      notify.ok(`${body.name} saved. Unified data will be sent there.`)
      saved()
    })
  }
  return <Modal title={destination ? `Edit ${destination.name}` : 'Send unified data to your database'} onClose={close}>
    <form className="source-form" onSubmit={submit}>
      <label>Name<input value={form.name} onChange={e => set('name', e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Company warehouse" maxLength={80} /></label>
      <label>Connection URL
        <input type="password" value={form.url} onChange={e => set('url', e.target.value)} autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false}
          placeholder={destination ? 'Saved. Leave empty to keep it' : 'postgresql://user:password@host:5432/database'} />
        {form.url && detected(form.url) ? <small className="detected"><Icon name="check" size={12} /> {detected(form.url)}</small>
          : <small className="muted">PostgreSQL, Supabase, MySQL, SQL Server, MongoDB… The user needs permission to write this table.</small>}
      </label>
      <label>Table<input value={form.table} onChange={e => set('table', e.target.value)} spellCheck={false} placeholder="unified_data" />
        <small className="muted">Created on the first send if it doesn't exist. Only this table is ever written. For MongoDB: a collection.</small></label>
      <div className="mode-pick" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Each time">
        {(['replace', 'append'] as const).map(m => <label key={m} className={form.mode === m ? 'on' : ''}>
          <input type="radio" name="mode" checked={form.mode === m} onChange={() => set('mode', m)} />
          <span><b>{m === 'replace' ? 'Replace' : 'Add rows'}</b><small>{m === 'replace' ? 'The table always holds the latest unified data' : 'Each unify adds its rows, keeping history'}</small></span>
        </label>)}
      </div>
      <p className="muted small"><Icon name="shield" size={13} /> The URL stays on the DataPulse server. Data that fails its checks is never sent.</p>
      {result && <p className={`test-result ${result.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'}`}><Icon name={result.ok ? 'check' : 'alert'} size={14} /> {result.message}</p>}
      <div className="actions">
        <button type="button" className="button outline" disabled={!!busy || (!form.url && !destination)} onClick={test}>{busy === 'test' ? <Spinner text="Connecting…" /> : 'Test connection'}</button>
        <button className="button primary" disabled={!!busy}>{busy === 'save' ? <Spinner text="Saving…" /> : destination ? 'Save changes' : 'Add destination'}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </Modal>
}

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons'

/* ---------- theme ---------- */

type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'

export function useTheme() {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(() => document.documentElement.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light')
  useEffect(() => { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme }, [theme])
  const toggle = () => {
    const next = theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'
    try { localStorage.setItem('datapulse-theme', next) } catch { /* per-viewer convenience only */ }
    setTheme(next)
  }
  return { theme, toggle }
}

/* ---------- toasts ---------- */

type Toast = { id: number; error: boolean; text: string }
type Notify = { ok: (text: string) => void; fail: (e: unknown) => void }
const ToastContext = createContext<Notify>({ ok: () => {}, fail: () => {} })
export const useNotify = () => useContext(ToastContext)

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([])
  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => setToasts(list => list.filter(t => t.id !== id)), [])
  const push = useCallback((error: boolean, text: string) => {
    const id = Date.now() + Math.random()
    setToasts(list => [...list.slice(-2), { id, error, text }])
    if (!error) window.setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), 4000) // errors stay until dismissed
  }, [dismiss])
  const [notify] = useState<Notify>(() => ({ ok: t => push(false, t), fail: e => push(true, (e as Error).message) }))
  return <ToastContext.Provider value={notify}>
    {children}
    <div className="toasts" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      {toasts.map(t => <div key={t.id} className={`toast ${t.error ? 'error' : ''}`}>
        <Icon name={t.error ? 'alert' : 'check'} size={16} />
        <span>{t.text}</span>
        <button className="icon-btn" aria-label="Dismiss" onClick={() => dismiss(t.id)}><Icon name="x" size={14} /></button>
      </div>)}
    </div>
  </ToastContext.Provider>
}

/** Run an async action with a busy flag; errors become toasts. */
export function useAction() {
  const { fail } = useNotify()
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const run = useCallback(async (key: string, action: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    setBusy(key)
    try { await action() } catch (e) { fail(e) } finally { setBusy(null) }
  }, [fail])
  return [busy, run] as const
}

export async function copyText(text: string, notify: Notify, what = 'Copied to clipboard') {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); notify.ok(what) } catch { notify.fail(new Error('Copy is unavailable here; select the text instead.')) }
}

/* ---------- building blocks ---------- */

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children }: { title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  const panel = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null
    panel.current?.focus()
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose()
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => { window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey); previous?.focus() }
  }, [onClose])
  return <div className="backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
    <div className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} tabIndex={-1} ref={panel} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <div className="modal-head"><h2>{title}</h2><button className="icon-btn" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}><Icon name="x" /></button></div>
      {children}
    </div>
  </div>
}

const ACCEPT = '.csv,.json,.jsonl,.ndjson,.parquet,.pq'

/** Drop or pick a file; the action starts straight away. */
export function Dropzone({ title, hint, busy, disabled, onFile }:
  { title: string; hint: string; busy?: string | false | null; disabled?: boolean; onFile: (file: File) => void }) {
  const [over, setOver] = useState(false)
  const off = disabled || !!busy
  return <label className={`dropzone ${over ? 'over' : ''} ${off ? 'off' : ''}`}
    onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); if (!off) setOver(true) }}
    onDragLeave={() => setOver(false)}
    onDrop={e => { e.preventDefault(); setOver(false); if (!off && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) onFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]) }}>
    <span className="dropzone-icon">{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : <Icon name="upload" size={20} />}</span>
    <span><b>{busy || (over ? 'Drop to start' : title)}</b><small>{busy ? 'This usually takes a few seconds' : hint}</small></span>
    <input type="file" accept={ACCEPT} disabled={off} onChange={e => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; e.target.value = ''; if (f) onFile(f) }} />
  </label>
}

export function Empty({ icon, title, children }: { icon: IconName; title: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="empty"><span className="empty-icon"><Icon name={icon} size={22} /></span><b>{title}</b>{children}</div>
}

export function CodeBlock({ code, label = 'Copy' }: { code: string; label?: string }) {
  const notify = useNotify()
  return <div className="code">
    <button className="copy" onClick={() => copyText(code, notify)}><Icon name="copy" size={13} /> {label}</button>
    <pre>{code}</pre>
  </div>
}

export const Spinner = ({ text }: { text: string }) => <><span className="spinner" /> {text}</>

"""AI triage agent: investigates a failed check the way an on-call data engineer would, then writes the
incident note - likely root cause, whether to block the release, fix steps, who to notify, a message to send.

Claude drives the investigation through read-only tools over aggregate data (issues, column statistics, run
history, downstream consumers). Tool results never contain raw rows, category values or example values.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional

from .ai import create, get_client, json_format, safe_measured, slim_profile, text_of
from .config import Config
from .errors import DataPulseError

SYSTEM = """You are the on-call data engineer for a team whose dashboards, models and services read this dataset. \
A data-quality check has just flagged a new batch. Investigate with the tools before concluding: start with \
list_issues, then look at the columns involved, the run history, and who depends on the data.

Then triage it:
- headline: one short sentence (under 25 words) a manager can read.
- verdict_reason: one sentence.
- verdict: "block" if shipping this batch would likely break a consumer or corrupt results, "investigate" if it \
needs a human look first, "safe_to_ship" if the findings are benign.
- root_causes: at most 3 ranked hypotheses tied to evidence you looked up, each one short sentence with one \
short sentence of evidence. Say how sure you are; never present a hypothesis as fact.
- actions: at most 4 concrete next steps of one sentence each, each with an owner taken from the consumer list or a role such as \
"data producer"; never invent people.
- notify: only consumers or owners that appear in the data, and in one sentence why each should hear about it.
- message: a Slack/email message under 70 words the engineer can send as-is.
Be specific and brief. You only ever see aggregates, never rows."""

SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
    "required": ["headline", "verdict", "verdict_reason", "root_causes", "actions", "notify", "message"],
    "properties": {
        "headline": {"type": "string"},
        "verdict": {"type": "string", "enum": ["block", "investigate", "safe_to_ship"]},
        "verdict_reason": {"type": "string"},
        "root_causes": {"type": "array", "items": {
            "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["hypothesis", "confidence", "evidence"],
            "properties": {"hypothesis": {"type": "string"}, "evidence": {"type": "string"},
                           "confidence": {"type": "string", "enum": ["low", "medium", "high"]}}}},
        "actions": {"type": "array", "items": {
            "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["step", "owner"],
            "properties": {"step": {"type": "string"}, "owner": {"type": "string"}}}},
        "notify": {"type": "array", "items": {
            "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["who", "why"],
            "properties": {"who": {"type": "string"}, "why": {"type": "string"}}}},
        "message": {"type": "string"},
    },
}


def _tool(name: str, description: str, properties: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    props = properties or {}
    return {"name": name, "description": description, "strict": True,
            "input_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": props, "required": list(props),
                             "additionalProperties": False}}


TOOLS = [
    _tool("list_issues", "Every issue the checks found: check name, severity, columns, measured numbers and "
                         "which consumers read those columns."),
    _tool("get_column_stats", "Baseline vs. current aggregate statistics for one column: type, null rate, "
                              "distinct values, numeric summary.",
          {"column": {"type": "string", "description": "Exact column name"}}),
    _tool("get_run_history", "Earlier checks of this dataset, newest first: when, pass/fail, which checks and "
                             "columns failed. Use it to tell a one-off from a recurring or worsening problem."),
    _tool("get_consumers", "Downstream dashboards, models and services, their owners and the columns they read."),
]


@dataclass
class TriageContext:
    """What the agent may look at. `report` is a JSON report (render_json); profiles are profile_dataframe output."""
    report: Dict[str, Any]
    baseline_profile: Dict[str, Any]
    current_profile: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    history: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    consumers: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)

    def list_issues(self) -> Any:
        return [{"check": i["check"], "severity": i["severity"], "title": i["title"], "columns": i["columns"],
                 "measured": safe_measured(i.get("measured", {})),
                 "consumers": [{"name": c["name"], "type": c["type"], "owner": c["owner"]} for c in i["consumers"]]}
                for i in self.report["issues"]]

    def get_column_stats(self, column: str) -> Any:
        side = lambda p: slim_profile(p)["columns"].get(column, "not present") if p else "not available"
        return {"column": column, "baseline": side(self.baseline_profile), "current": side(self.current_profile)}

    def get_run_history(self) -> Any:
        return self.history if self.history is not None else "No run history is available (single CLI run)."

    def get_consumers(self) -> Any:
        return self.consumers or "No consumers are configured for this dataset."


def run_triage(ctx: TriageContext, cfg: Config, client=None, max_turns: int = 10,
               on_step: Optional[Callable[[str, Dict[str, Any]], None]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Run the agent loop. Returns the triage plus `steps`, the tool calls it made (shown to the user)."""
    client = client or get_client(cfg)
    rows = ctx.report.get("rows", {})
    messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [{"role": "user", "content": (
        f"The check of {ctx.report.get('data_file', 'a new batch')} {ctx.report.get('status', 'ran')} with "
        f"{len(ctx.report['issues'])} issue(s); rows went from {rows.get('baseline')} (baseline) to "
        f"{rows.get('current')}. Fail threshold: {ctx.report.get('fail_on')}. Investigate and triage it.")}]
    steps: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(max_turns):
        response = create(client, cfg, system=SYSTEM, tools=TOOLS, messages=messages,
                          output_config={**json_format(SCHEMA), "effort": "medium"})
        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": response.content})
        if response.stop_reason == "pause_turn":
            continue
        calls = [b for b in response.content if b.type == "tool_use"]
        if not calls:
            try:
                triage = json.loads(text_of(response))
            except ValueError as e:
                raise DataPulseError(f"The AI triage was not valid JSON: {e}")
            return {**triage, "steps": steps, "model": cfg.ai.model}
        results = []
        for call in calls:
            steps.append({"tool": call.name, "input": call.input})
            if on_step:
                on_step(call.name, call.input)
            handler = getattr(ctx, call.name, None) if call.name in {t["name"] for t in TOOLS} else None
            if handler is None:
                results.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": call.id, "is_error": True,
                                "content": f"Unknown tool {call.name}"})
                continue
            results.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": call.id,
                            "content": json.dumps(handler(**call.input), default=str)})
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": results})
    raise DataPulseError(f"The AI triage did not finish within {max_turns} steps.")

FILE [66/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\ai.py
