"""Claude-powered features: issue explanations, rule suggestions, and plain-English rule drafting.

Privacy: only aggregate statistics, column names and detected-issue metrics are sent. Raw rows, category
values and example values never are (tests enforce this). The triage agent lives in `agent.py`.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from .config import Config, parse_config_text
from .errors import DataPulseError
from .models import CheckReport

# Models that accept server-side refusal fallbacks ("default" routes by refusal category).
FALLBACK_MODELS = {"claude-fable-5-1", "claude-opus-5-5", "claude-opus-5", "claude-sonnet-5-5"}

EXPLAIN_SYSTEM = (
    "You are a data engineer helping triage data-quality regressions. You receive aggregate statistics and "
    "detected issues about a dataset (never raw rows). For each issue give a short, hedged root-cause hypothesis "
    "and one concrete next step. These are hypotheses, not facts."
)
SUGGEST_SYSTEM = (
    "You are a data engineer. Given an aggregate profile of a dataset (never raw rows), suggest additional "
    "validation rules worth adding, each with a one-line reason. Be concise and say which suggestions are "
    "judgement calls. Plain text, at most 8 bullet points."
)
RULES_SYSTEM = """You edit DataPulse configuration files (datapulse.yml) from plain-English requests.

Return the complete updated file. Keep every existing setting and comment the request doesn't touch. Only use \
keys DataPulse supports - anything else fails validation:
- top level: version (1), baseline, fail_on (critical|warning|info|never), max_categories, checks, consumers, \
report, discovery, ai
- checks.schema: missing_columns, added_columns, type_changes (each a severity)
- checks.row_count: severity, max_change_pct (percent), min_rows
- checks.null_rate: severity, max_increase (0-1, absolute), columns (map column -> max increase 0-1)
- checks.categorical: severity, max_unexpected_rate (0-1), columns (list) - flags values not seen in the baseline
- checks.duplicates: severity, max_duplicate_rate (0-1), columns (key columns that must be unique)
- checks.distribution: severity, psi_threshold, max_mean_shift_std, columns (list)
- checks.ml_drift: severity, max_auc (0.5-1), max_rows, exclude (list)
- consumers: map name -> {type, owner, description, columns (list, required)}
Severities: critical | warning | info | off. Use only column names that exist in the dataset profile. If part of \
the request can't be expressed with these keys (e.g. an explicit allowed-values list or a value range), leave it \
out and list it under `not_supported` with the closest supported alternative."""

EXPLAIN_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["explanations"],
    "properties": {"explanations": {"type": "array", "items": {
        "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["index", "text"],
        "properties": {"index": {"type": "integer"}, "text": {"type": "string"}}}}},
}
RULES_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["config_yaml", "summary", "not_supported"],
    "properties": {"config_yaml": {"type": "string"}, "summary": {"type": "string"},
                   "not_supported": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}},
}


def get_api_key(cfg: Config) -> Optional[str]:
    # Keys pasted into a host's settings often pick up a trailing space or newline, which makes every request
    # fail as an illegal header ("Connection error"). Trim it.
    return (os.environ.get(cfg.ai.api_key_env) or "").strip() or None


def get_client(cfg: Config):
    """An Anthropic client, or a DataPulseError that says how to enable AI features."""
    key = get_api_key(cfg)
    if not key:
        raise DataPulseError(f"AI features need the ${cfg.ai.api_key_env} environment variable to be set.")
    import anthropic

    base_url = os.environ.get("DATAPULSE_AI_URL")  # e.g. a company proxy in front of the Anthropic API
    if base_url and base_url.rstrip("/").endswith("/v1/messages"):  # older docs gave the full endpoint
        base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")[: -len("/v1/messages")]
    # Organization-level keys must name a workspace on every request; workspace-scoped keys don't need this.
    workspace = os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE_ID", "").strip()
    return anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=key, base_url=base_url or None, timeout=120.0, max_retries=2,
                               default_headers={"anthropic-workspace-id": workspace} if workspace else None)


def create(client, cfg: Config, **params):
    """One Messages API call with refusal fallback, normalised errors and stop-reason checks."""
    import anthropic

    if cfg.ai.model in FALLBACK_MODELS:
        params.update(betas=["server-side-fallback-2026-07-01"], fallbacks="default")
    try:
        response = client.beta.messages.create(model=cfg.ai.model, max_tokens=16000, **params)
    except anthropic.AuthenticationError:
        raise DataPulseError(f"The API key in ${cfg.ai.api_key_env} was rejected.")
    except anthropic.RateLimitError:
        raise DataPulseError("The AI service is rate limiting requests; try again in a minute.")
    except anthropic.APIStatusError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"AI request failed with HTTP {e.status_code}: {e.message}")
    except anthropic.APIConnectionError as e:
        cause = f" ({e.__cause__})" if e.__cause__ else ""
        raise DataPulseError(f"Could not reach the AI service: {e}{cause}")
    if response.stop_reason == "refusal":
        raise DataPulseError("The AI declined this request.")
    if response.stop_reason == "max_tokens":
        raise DataPulseError("The AI response was cut off before it finished.")
    return response


def text_of(response) -> str:
    text = "".join(b.text for b in response.content if b.type == "text")
    if not text.strip():
        raise DataPulseError("AI response was empty")
    return text


def json_format(schema: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"format": {"type": "json_schema", "schema": schema}}


def slim_profile(profile: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """A profile without category values or histogram internals: safe to send."""
    cols = {}
    for name, info in profile["columns"].items():
        slim = {k: v for k, v in info.items() if k not in ("categories", "numeric")}
        if "categories" in info:
            counts = list(info["categories"].values())
            slim["category_count"] = len(counts)
            slim["largest_category_share"] = round(max(counts) / sum(counts), 3)
        if "numeric" in info:
            slim["numeric"] = {k: v for k, v in info["numeric"].items() if k not in ("bin_edges", "bin_props")}
        cols[name] = slim
    return {"rows": profile["rows"], "duplicate_rows": profile.get("duplicate_rows", 0), "columns": cols}


build_suggest_payload = slim_profile


def safe_measured(measured: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {k: v for k, v in measured.items() if isinstance(v, (int, float, bool, str)) or v is None}


def build_explain_payload(report: CheckReport) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "dataset": {"baseline_rows": report.baseline_rows, "current_rows": report.current_rows,
                    "current_columns": report.current_columns},
        "issues": [{"index": n, "check": i.check, "severity": i.severity, "columns": i.columns,
                    "measured": safe_measured(i.measured), "consumer_types": sorted({c["type"] for c in i.consumers})}
                   for n, i in enumerate(report.issues)],
    }


def explain_issues(report: CheckReport, cfg: Config) -> str:
    """Attach AI explanations to issues. Returns a status line for the user ('' if nothing to say)."""
    if not get_api_key(cfg):
        return f"AI explanations requested but ${cfg.ai.api_key_env} is not set; using deterministic explanations."
    if not report.issues:
        return ""
    try:
        response = create(get_client(cfg), cfg, system=EXPLAIN_SYSTEM, output_config=json_format(EXPLAIN_SCHEMA),
                          messages=[{"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(build_explain_payload(report))}])
        for item in json.loads(text_of(response))["explanations"]:
            if 0 <= int(item["index"]) < len(report.issues):
                report.issues[int(item["index"])].ai_explanation = str(item["text"]).strip()
    except (DataPulseError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
        return f"AI explanations unavailable ({e}); using deterministic explanations."
    return "AI explanations added (generated from aggregate statistics only; unverified)."


def suggest_with_ai(profile: Dict[str, Any], cfg: Config) -> str:
    response = create(get_client(cfg), cfg, system=SUGGEST_SYSTEM,
                      messages=[{"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(slim_profile(profile))}])
    return text_of(response).strip()


def draft_rules(request: str, config_yaml: str, profile: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], cfg: Config,
                client=None, attempts: int = 2) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Turn a plain-English request into a complete, validated datapulse.yml. Nothing is saved.

    If the draft fails DataPulse's own validation, the error goes back to the model for one more try.
    """
    client = client or get_client(cfg)
    context = {"request": request, "current_config_yaml": config_yaml,
               "dataset_profile": slim_profile(profile) if profile else "no baseline yet; column names unknown"}
    messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [{"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(context)}]
    error = ""
    for _ in range(attempts):
        response = create(client, cfg, system=RULES_SYSTEM, output_config=json_format(RULES_SCHEMA),
                          messages=messages)
        try:
            draft = json.loads(text_of(response))
            parse_config_text(draft["config_yaml"], "AI draft")
            return {"config_yaml": draft["config_yaml"], "summary": draft["summary"],
                    "not_supported": draft["not_supported"]}
        except (ValueError, KeyError, DataPulseError) as e:
            error = str(e)
            messages += [{"role": "assistant", "content": response.content},
                         {"role": "user", "content": f"That draft failed validation: {error}\nReturn a corrected draft."}]
    raise DataPulseError(f"The AI could not produce a valid configuration ({error}).")

"""Versioned baseline profiles stored as JSON."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict

import pandas as pd

from . import __version__
from .errors import DataPulseError
from .io import file_sha256
from .profile import profile_dataframe

SCHEMA_VERSION = 1


def build_baseline(df: pd.DataFrame, data_file, version: int, max_categories: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = Path(data_file)
    return {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "baseline_version": version,
        "datapulse_version": __version__,
        "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat(),
        "source": {"file": p.as_posix(), "format": p.suffix.lower().lstrip("."), "sha256": file_sha256(p)},
        "profile": profile_dataframe(df, max_categories),
    }


def parse_baseline(text: str, origin: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        data = json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Baseline {origin} is not valid JSON: {e}")
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or "schema_version" not in data or "profile" not in data:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Baseline {origin} is not a DataPulse baseline file.")
    if data["schema_version"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise DataPulseError(
            f"Baseline {origin} has schema_version {data['schema_version']}, expected {SCHEMA_VERSION}. "
            "Re-create it with `datapulse baseline`.")
    prof = data["profile"]
    if not isinstance(prof, dict) or "rows" not in prof or "columns" not in prof:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Baseline {origin} is missing its profile data.")
    data.setdefault("baseline_version", 1)
    return data


def load_baseline(path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = Path(path)
    if not p.exists():
        raise DataPulseError(f"Baseline not found: {p}. Create one with: datapulse baseline <data-file>")
    return parse_baseline(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), str(p))


def save_baseline(path, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def describe(data: Dict[str, Any], origin: str) -> str:
    src = data.get("source", {}).get("file", "?")
    return f"{origin} (v{data.get('baseline_version', 1)}, from {src}, created {data.get('created_at', '?')})"

"""The quality checks. Every issue carries measured facts and, separately, a
deterministic *suggested* explanation that is explicitly not verified."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .config import LEVEL, Config
from .models import CheckReport, Issue
from .profile import NUMERIC_KINDS, bin_proportions, column_kind, numeric_values, profile_dataframe


def _n(x) -> str:
    if x is None:
        return "n/a"
    x = float(x)
    if x.is_integer():
        return f"{int(x):,}"
    return f"{x:.4g}" if abs(x) < 0.1 else f"{x:,.2f}"


def _pct(x: float) -> str:
    return f"{x * 100:.1f}%"


def psi(expected, actual, eps: float = 1e-4) -> float:
    e = np.clip(np.asarray(expected, dtype=float), eps, None)
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(actual, dtype=float), eps, None)
    return float(np.sum((a - e) * np.log(a / e)))


def _type_hint(b: str, c: str) -> str:
    if b in NUMERIC_KINDS and c == "string":
        return ("Some rows probably contain non-numeric text (currency symbols, units, 'N/A') "
                "so the whole column is now read as text.")
    if b == "integer" and c == "float":
        return "Fractional values appeared in a column that used to hold whole numbers."
    if b == "float" and c == "integer":
        return "The column now only contains whole numbers; a rounding or casting step may have been added upstream."
    if b == "string" and c in NUMERIC_KINDS:
        return "The column now parses as numbers; an upstream cast or a changed export format is likely."
    if "datetime" in (b, c):
        return "The date/time format of the export may have changed."
    return "An upstream schema or export change is a likely cause."


def check_schema(base: Dict, cur: Dict, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    bcols, ccols = base["columns"], cur["columns"]
    s = cfg.schema
    out: List[Issue] = []
    added = [c for c in ccols if c not in bcols]
    if s.missing_columns != "off":
        for c in (c for c in bcols if c not in ccols):
            kind = bcols[c]["kind"]
            cands = [a for a in added if ccols[a]["kind"] == kind]
            hint = "The column may have been dropped or renamed upstream."
            if cands:
                hint += f" Added column(s) with the same type that could be a rename: {', '.join(cands)}."
            out.append(Issue("schema.missing_column", s.missing_columns, f"Column '{c}' is missing",
                             f"'{c}' was in the baseline ({kind}) but is not in the current file.",
                             [c], {"baseline_type": kind, "present_in_current": False}, explanation=hint))
    if s.added_columns != "off":
        for c in added:
            kind = ccols[c]["kind"]
            out.append(Issue("schema.added_column", s.added_columns, f"New column '{c}'",
                             f"'{c}' ({kind}) is in the current file but not in the baseline.",
                             [c], {"current_type": kind},
                             explanation="A new field was likely added upstream. Consumers that select explicit "
                                         "columns are unaffected; consumers that read every column may be."))
    if s.type_changes != "off":
        for c in bcols:
            if c in ccols and bcols[c]["kind"] != ccols[c]["kind"]:
                b, k = bcols[c], ccols[c]
                if b["kind"] == "float" and k["kind"] == "integer":
                    continue  # whole-number floats are compatible with the baseline type
                out.append(Issue("schema.type_change", s.type_changes, f"Type of '{c}' changed",
                                 f"'{c}' changed from {b['kind']} to {k['kind']}.", [c],
                                 {"baseline_type": b["kind"], "current_type": k["kind"],
                                  "baseline_dtype": b["dtype"], "current_dtype": k["dtype"]},
                                 explanation=_type_hint(b["kind"], k["kind"])))
    return out


def check_row_count(base: Dict, cur: Dict, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    rc = cfg.row_count
    b, c = base["rows"], cur["rows"]
    out: List[Issue] = []
    if b > 0:
        change = (c - b) / b * 100
        if abs(change) > rc.max_change_pct:
            expl = ("Rows were added: a re-loaded or appended batch, or duplicated history, is possible."
                    if change > 0 else
                    "Rows were lost: an upstream filter, a partial export, or a truncated load is possible.")
            out.append(Issue("row_count", rc.severity, "Row count changed beyond threshold",
                             f"Rows went from {_n(b)} to {_n(c)} ({change:+.1f}%; allowed ±{rc.max_change_pct:g}%).",
                             [], {"baseline_rows": b, "current_rows": c, "change_pct": round(change, 2),
                                  "max_change_pct": rc.max_change_pct}, explanation=expl))
    if rc.min_rows is not None and c < rc.min_rows:
        out.append(Issue("row_count.min_rows", rc.severity, "Row count below minimum",
                         f"The file has {_n(c)} rows; the configured minimum is {_n(rc.min_rows)}.",
                         [], {"current_rows": c, "min_rows": rc.min_rows},
                         explanation="The export may be partial or empty."))
    return out


def check_null_rate(base: Dict, cur: Dict, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    nr = cfg.null_rate
    out: List[Issue] = []
    for col, b in base["columns"].items():
        c = cur["columns"].get(col)
        if c is None:
            continue
        limit = nr.columns.get(col, nr.max_increase)
        inc = c["null_rate"] - b["null_rate"]
        if inc > limit + 1e-12:
            out.append(Issue("null_rate", nr.severity, f"Null rate rose in '{col}'",
                             f"Null rate in '{col}' rose from {_pct(b['null_rate'])} to {_pct(c['null_rate'])} "
                             f"(+{inc * 100:.1f} points; allowed +{limit * 100:.1f}).",
                             [col], {"baseline_null_rate": round(b["null_rate"], 4),
                                     "current_null_rate": round(c["null_rate"], 4),
                                     "increase": round(inc, 4), "allowed_increase": limit,
                                     "current_null_count": c["null_count"]},
                             explanation="A failed join, a parsing problem, or an upstream field that stopped "
                                         "being populated can produce a sudden jump in missing values."))
    return out


def check_categorical(base: Dict, df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    cc = cfg.categorical
    out: List[Issue] = []
    for col, b in base["columns"].items():
        if "categories" not in b or col not in df.columns:
            continue
        if cc.columns is not None and col not in cc.columns:
            continue
        s = df[col].dropna()
        if s.empty or column_kind(df[col]) != "string":
            continue
        s = s.astype(str)
        known = set(b["categories"])
        unexpected = s[~s.isin(known)]
        rate = len(unexpected) / len(s)
        if len(unexpected) == 0 or rate <= cc.max_unexpected_rate + 1e-12:
            continue
        counts = unexpected.value_counts()
        folded = {k.strip().lower() for k in known}
        variants = sum(1 for v in counts.index if v.strip().lower() in folded)
        expl = "A new category was probably introduced upstream."
        if variants:
            expl = (f"{variants} unexpected value(s) match a known category apart from case or whitespace, "
                    "which suggests inconsistent formatting upstream. ") + expl
        out.append(Issue("categorical", cc.severity, f"Unexpected values in '{col}'",
                         f"{_n(len(counts))} value(s) not seen in the baseline appear in {_n(len(unexpected))} rows "
                         f"({_pct(rate)} of non-null rows; allowed {_pct(cc.max_unexpected_rate)}).",
                         [col], {"unexpected_distinct_values": len(counts), "unexpected_rows": int(len(unexpected)),
                                 "unexpected_rate": round(rate, 4), "baseline_category_count": len(known),
                                 "case_or_whitespace_variants": variants},
                         examples=[f"{v} ({c} rows)" for v, c in counts.head(cfg.report.max_examples).items()]
                         if cfg.report.show_values else [], explanation=expl))
    return out


def check_duplicates(base: Dict, df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    dp = cfg.duplicates
    n = len(df)
    if n == 0:
        return []
    keys = dp.columns
    if keys:
        missing = [k for k in keys if k not in df.columns]
        if missing:
            return [Issue("duplicates.config", dp.severity, "Duplicate-check key column missing",
                          f"Configured key column(s) {missing} are not in the current file, so uniqueness "
                          "could not be verified.", missing, {"missing_key_columns": len(missing)},
                          explanation="The key column may have been renamed or dropped.")]
        mask = df.duplicated(subset=keys, keep="first")
        base_rate = 0.0
    else:
        mask = df.duplicated(keep="first")
        base_rate = base["duplicate_rows"] / base["rows"] if base["rows"] else 0.0
    dups = int(mask.sum())
    rate = dups / n
    if rate - base_rate <= dp.max_duplicate_rate + 1e-12 or dups == 0:
        return []
    examples: List[str] = []
    if keys and cfg.report.show_values:
        # map(str), not astype(str): newer pandas keeps empty values as NaN in astype(str), which join can't take
        vc = df.loc[mask, keys].apply(lambda col: col.map(str)).agg(" | ".join, axis=1).value_counts()
        examples = [f"{k} ({c + 1} rows)" for k, c in vc.head(cfg.report.max_examples).items()]
    what = f"key {keys}" if keys else "whole rows"
    return [Issue("duplicates", dp.severity, "Duplicate rows detected",
                  f"{_n(dups)} duplicate row(s) by {what} ({_pct(rate)} of rows; baseline {_pct(base_rate)}, "
                  f"allowed increase {_pct(dp.max_duplicate_rate)}).", list(keys),
                  {"duplicate_rows": dups, "duplicate_rate": round(rate, 4),
                   "baseline_duplicate_rate": round(base_rate, 4), "key_columns": ", ".join(keys) or "(all columns)"},
                  examples=examples,
                  explanation="A batch may have been loaded twice, or a join fanned out rows.")]


def _scale_hint(b_mean: float, c_mean: float) -> str:
    if not b_mean or not c_mean:
        return ""
    ratio = c_mean / b_mean
    for factor, label in ((100, "100x"), (1000, "1000x"), (0.01, "1/100"), (0.001, "1/1000")):
        if abs(ratio / factor - 1) < 0.1:
            return (f" The mean changed by roughly {label}, which is typical of a unit change "
                    "(for example dollars vs. cents).")
    return ""


def check_distribution(base: Dict, cur: Dict, df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    ds = cfg.distribution
    out: List[Issue] = []
    for col, b in base["columns"].items():
        bn = b.get("numeric")
        c = cur["columns"].get(col)
        if not bn or c is None or c["kind"] not in NUMERIC_KINDS:
            continue
        if ds.columns is not None and col not in ds.columns:
            continue
        if ds.columns is None and b["kind"] == "integer" and b["unique_count"] == base["rows"] > 1:
            continue  # identifier-like column (all values unique); name it in `columns` to check it anyway
        v = numeric_values(df[col])
        if v.empty:
            continue
        c_mean = float(v.mean())
        score = psi(bn["bin_props"], bin_proportions(v.to_numpy(), bn["bin_edges"]))
        if bn["std"] > 0:
            shift = abs(c_mean - bn["mean"]) / bn["std"]
            shifted = shift > ds.max_mean_shift_std
        else:
            shift = None
            shifted = abs(c_mean - bn["mean"]) > 1e-12
        if score <= ds.psi_threshold and not shifted:
            continue
        shift_txt = "n/a (baseline was constant)" if shift is None else f"{shift:.2f} std"
        out.append(Issue("distribution", ds.severity, f"Distribution shift in '{col}'",
                         f"Mean of '{col}' moved from {_n(bn['mean'])} to {_n(c_mean)} (shift {shift_txt}; "
                         f"allowed {ds.max_mean_shift_std:g}); PSI {score:.3f} (allowed {ds.psi_threshold:g}).",
                         [col], {"baseline_mean": round(bn["mean"], 4), "current_mean": round(c_mean, 4),
                                 "mean_shift_std": None if shift is None else round(shift, 3),
                                 "psi": round(score, 4), "baseline_median": round(bn["p50"], 4),
                                 "current_median": round(float(v.median()), 4),
                                 "current_min": round(float(v.min()), 4), "current_max": round(float(v.max()), 4)},
                         explanation="The population being exported may have changed (a new filter, segment or "
                                     "time window), or an upstream calculation changed." + _scale_hint(bn["mean"], c_mean)))
    return out


def check_ml_drift(reference: pd.DataFrame, df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: Config) -> List[Issue]:
    from .ml import drift_model, top_drivers
    md = cfg.ml_drift
    res = drift_model(reference, df, md.exclude, md.max_rows)
    if res["auc"] is None or res["auc"] <= md.max_auc:
        return []
    drivers = top_drivers(res["drivers"])
    named = ", ".join(f"{c} ({share:.0%})" for c, share in drivers)
    skipped = ", ".join(f"{c} ({why})" for c, why in res["skipped"].items())
    return [Issue("ml_drift", md.severity, "A model can tell the new data from the baseline",
                  f"A classifier trained to separate baseline rows from new rows scored AUC {res['auc']:.2f} "
                  f"(0.5 = indistinguishable; allowed {md.max_auc:g}). It relied most on: {named}.",
                  [c for c, _ in drivers],
                  {"drift_auc": res["auc"], "allowed_auc": md.max_auc, "top_drivers": named,
                   "rows_compared_per_file": res["rows_per_side"], "columns_not_modelled": skipped or "none"},
                  explanation="The rows no longer look like they come from the same population. Check the driver "
                              "columns first: a unit change, a new source system, a changed filter, or a shift in "
                              "how these columns relate to each other (which per-column checks cannot see).")]


def _consumers_for(issue: Issue, cfg: Config) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    cols = set(issue.columns)
    out = []
    for c in cfg.consumers:
        matched = sorted(cols & set(c.columns)) if cols else sorted(c.columns)
        if matched or not cols:
            out.append({"name": c.name, "type": c.type, "owner": c.owner, "columns": matched,
                        "dataset_wide": not cols})
    return out


def run_checks(baseline: Dict, df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: Config, data_file: str = "",
               baseline_label: str = "baseline", config_label: str = "defaults",
               reference: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None) -> CheckReport:
    """`reference` is the baseline's rows; when given, the learned `ml_drift` check runs too."""
    base = baseline["profile"]
    cur = profile_dataframe(df, cfg.max_categories)
    checks = {  # name -> (enabled, run)
        "schema": (any(v != "off" for v in vars(cfg.schema).values()), lambda: check_schema(base, cur, cfg)),
        "row_count": (cfg.row_count.severity != "off", lambda: check_row_count(base, cur, cfg)),
        "null_rate": (cfg.null_rate.severity != "off", lambda: check_null_rate(base, cur, cfg)),
        "categorical": (cfg.categorical.severity != "off", lambda: check_categorical(base, df, cfg)),
        "duplicates": (cfg.duplicates.severity != "off", lambda: check_duplicates(base, df, cfg)),
        "distribution": (cfg.distribution.severity != "off", lambda: check_distribution(base, cur, df, cfg)),
        "ml_drift": (cfg.ml_drift.severity != "off" and reference is not None,
                     lambda: check_ml_drift(reference, df, cfg)),
    }
    ran = [name for name, (enabled, _) in checks.items() if enabled]
    issues = [issue for name in ran for issue in checks[name][1]()]
    for i in issues:
        i.consumers = _consumers_for(i, cfg)
    issues.sort(key=lambda i: (-LEVEL[i.severity], i.check, ",".join(i.columns)))
    return CheckReport(data_file=data_file, baseline_label=baseline_label, baseline_rows=base["rows"],
                       current_rows=cur["rows"], current_columns=len(cur["columns"]), issues=issues,
                       checks_run=ran, fail_on=cfg.fail_on, max_examples=cfg.report.max_examples,
                       config_label=config_label)

"""DataPulse command line interface."""
import functools
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional

import typer
from rich.console import Console
from rich.text import Text

from . import __version__
from .agent import TriageContext, run_triage
from .ai import draft_rules, explain_issues, suggest_with_ai
from .baseline import build_baseline, describe, load_baseline, parse_baseline, save_baseline
from .checks import run_checks
from .config import DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME, STARTER_CONFIG, Config, load_config, parse_config_text
from .errors import DataPulseError
from .github import (default_api_url, detect_pr_context, path_exists_at_ref, read_file_at_ref, ref_exists,
                     upsert_pr_comment)
from .io import load_dataframe
from .profile import profile_dataframe
from .scan import attach_code_references, consumers_yaml, describe_scan, scan_repo
from .report import action_outputs, render_json, render_markdown, render_terminal
from .suggest import render_yaml, suggest_rules

app = typer.Typer(name="datapulse", no_args_is_help=True, add_completion=False,
                  help="Catch data-quality regressions in CSV and Parquet files before they break "
                       "dashboards and models.")


def _version(value: bool):
    if value:
        typer.echo(f"datapulse {__version__}")
        raise typer.Exit()


@app.callback()
def main(version: bool = typer.Option(False, "--version", callback=_version, is_eager=True,
                                      help="Show the version and exit.")):
    pass


def guarded(fn):
    """Turn DataPulseError into a readable message and exit code 2."""
    @functools.wraps(fn)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        try:
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)
        except DataPulseError as e:
            Console(stderr=True).print(Text(f"error: {e}", style="bold red"), soft_wrap=True)
            raise typer.Exit(2)
    return wrapper


CONFIG_OPT = typer.Option(None, "--config", "-c", help=f"Config file (default: ./{DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME} if present).")


@app.command("init")
@guarded
def init_cmd(force: bool = typer.Option(False, "--force", help="Overwrite an existing file.")):
    """Write a commented starter datapulse.yml in the current directory."""
    p = Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME)
    if p.exists() and not force:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{p} already exists. Use --force to overwrite it.")
    p.write_text(STARTER_CONFIG, encoding="utf-8")
    typer.echo(f"Wrote {p}. Edit it, then run: datapulse baseline <data-file>")


@app.command("baseline")
@guarded
def baseline_cmd(data_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="Known-good CSV or Parquet file."),
                 config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
                 output: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o",
                                                       help="Baseline path (default: `baseline` in the config).")):
    """Create (or bump the version of) the baseline profile from a known-good file."""
    cfg = load_config(config)
    out = output or Path(cfg.baseline)
    df = load_dataframe(data_file)
    if len(df) == 0:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{data_file} has no rows; a baseline needs data.")
    version = 1
    if out.exists():
        version = parse_baseline(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), str(out))["baseline_version"] + 1
    data = build_baseline(df, data_file, version, cfg.max_categories)
    save_baseline(out, data)
    typer.echo(f"Baseline v{version} written to {out} "
               f"({data['profile']['rows']:,} rows, {len(data['profile']['columns'])} columns).")
    if version > 1:
        typer.echo(f"Replaced previous baseline v{version - 1}. Commit {out} so CI can compare against it.")
    else:
        typer.echo(f"Commit {out} to your repository so CI can compare against it.")


def _load_check_config(config, config_ref, console):
    """Load the config from the working tree, or (hardened for CI) from a trusted git ref."""
    if not config_ref:
        cfg = load_config(config)
        if cfg.source is None:
            console.print(Text(f"No {DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME} found; using default rules.", style="dim"))
        return cfg, cfg.source or "defaults (no datapulse.yml)"
    path = config or Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME)
    if not ref_exists(config_ref):
        raise DataPulseError(f"Git ref '{config_ref}' was not found, so the trusted config could not be read. "
                             "Fetch it first (git fetch origin <branch>). Refusing to fall back to the "
                             "working-tree config silently.")
    if path_exists_at_ref(config_ref, path):
        label = f"{Path(path).as_posix()} @ {config_ref}"
        return parse_config_text(read_file_at_ref(config_ref, path), label), label
    cfg = load_config(config) if Path(path).exists() else Config()
    console.print(Text(f"{path} does not exist at {config_ref}; using "
                       f"{'the working-tree config' if cfg.source else 'default rules'}. (There is no "
                       "trusted config on that ref to compare against.)", style="yellow"))
    return cfg, f"{cfg.source or 'defaults'} (working tree; none at {config_ref})"


def _triage(report, baseline, df, cfg, console) -> None:
    ctx = TriageContext(json.loads(render_json(report)), baseline["profile"], profile_dataframe(df, cfg.max_categories),
                        consumers=[vars(c) for c in cfg.consumers])
    try:
        with console.status("AI triage agent is investigating…"):
            report.triage = run_triage(ctx, cfg, on_step=lambda tool, args: console.print(
                Text(f"  agent looked at {tool} {json.dumps(args) if args else ''}", style="dim")))
    except DataPulseError as e:
        console.print(Text(f"AI triage unavailable: {e}", style="yellow"))


def _write_report(path: Optional[Path], text: str, kind: str, console: Console) -> None:
    if path:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        console.print(Text(f"{kind} report written to {path}", style="dim"))


@app.command("check")
@guarded
def check_cmd(data_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="CSV or Parquet file to check."),
              config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
              baseline: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--baseline", help="Baseline file (overrides config)."),
              baseline_ref: Optional[str] = typer.Option(
                  None, "--baseline-ref", help="Read the baseline from this git ref (e.g. origin/main) "
                                               "instead of the working tree."),
              config_ref: Optional[str] = typer.Option(
                  None, "--config-ref", help="Read datapulse.yml from this git ref too (use the PR's target "
                                             "branch so a PR cannot loosen its own rules)."),
              markdown: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--markdown", help="Write the Markdown report here."),
              json_out: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--json", help="Also write a machine-readable JSON report "
                                                                           "(for scripts in any language)."),
              reference: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(
                  None, "--reference", help="The known-good data file itself; enables the ML drift check, which "
                                            "trains a model to tell it apart from the new file (runs locally)."),
              ai: bool = typer.Option(False, "--ai", help="Add AI explanations (aggregates only; needs an API key)."),
              triage: bool = typer.Option(False, "--triage", help="If issues are found, let the AI triage agent "
                                                                  "investigate and write the incident note."),
              discover: bool = typer.Option(False, "--discover", help="Find code in any language that references "
                                                                      "the affected columns (heuristic)."),
              scan_root: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--scan-root", help="Directory to search (default: "
                                                                                 "discovery.root, i.e. '.').")):
    """Check a data file against its baseline. Exits 1 if issues reach the `fail_on` severity."""
    console = Console()
    cfg, config_label = _load_check_config(config, config_ref, console)
    path = baseline or Path(cfg.baseline)
    if baseline_ref:
        data = parse_baseline(read_file_at_ref(baseline_ref, path), f"{baseline_ref}:{path}")
        label = describe(data, f"{path} @ {baseline_ref}")
    else:
        data = load_baseline(path)
        label = describe(data, str(path))
    df = load_dataframe(data_file)
    report = run_checks(data, df, cfg, data_file=str(data_file), baseline_label=label, config_label=config_label,
                        reference=load_dataframe(reference) if reference else None)
    if discover or cfg.discovery.enabled:
        cols = sorted(set(data["profile"]["columns"]) | set(df.columns))
        src = data.get("source", {}).get("file")
        scan = scan_repo(scan_root or Path(cfg.discovery.root), data_file, cols, cfg.discovery.exclude,
                         cfg.discovery.scope, also_names=[src] if src else [])
        attach_code_references(report, scan)
        console.print(Text("Code discovery: " + describe_scan(scan), style="dim"))
    if ai or cfg.ai.enabled:
        note = explain_issues(report, cfg)
        if note:
            console.print(Text(note, style="dim"))
    if triage and report.issues:
        _triage(report, data, df, cfg, console)
    render_terminal(report, console)
    md = render_markdown(report)
    _write_report(markdown or (Path(cfg.report.markdown) if cfg.report.markdown else None), md, "Markdown", console)
    _write_report(json_out, render_json(report), "JSON", console)
    summary = os.environ.get("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY")
    if summary:
        with open(summary, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(md + "\n")
    raise typer.Exit(report.exit_code)


def _write_new(output: Path, text: str, cfg, force: bool) -> None:
    """Write suggestions to a separate file; never to the user's config, never over a file without --force."""
    protected = {Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME).resolve()} | ({Path(cfg.source).resolve()} if cfg.source else set())
    if output.resolve() in protected:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Refusing to write to your configuration file {output}. Write to a separate "
                             "file and merge the entries you want by hand.")
    if output.exists() and not force:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{output} already exists. Use --force to overwrite it.")
    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")


@app.command("scan")
@guarded
def scan_cmd(data_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="The CSV/Parquet file whose readers to look for."),
             root: Path = typer.Option(Path("."), "--root", help="Repository directory to search."),
             scope: str = typer.Option("readers", "--scope", help="readers: only files that mention the data file "
                                                                  "(or SQL table); all: every source file."),
             exclude: Optional[List[str]] = typer.Option(None, "--exclude", help="Extra directory name to skip "
                                                                                 "(repeatable)."),
             config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
             output: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o", help="Write suggested `consumers:` YAML "
                                                                                "to this NEW file."),
             force: bool = typer.Option(False, "--force", help="Allow overwriting an existing --output file.")):
    """Recognize the languages in a repo and find code that reads a data file (heuristic text search)."""
    cfg = load_config(config)
    if scope not in ("readers", "all"):
        raise DataPulseError("--scope must be 'readers' or 'all'.")
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise DataPulseError(f"--root is not a directory: {root}")
    df = load_dataframe(data_file)
    result = scan_repo(root, data_file, list(df.columns), cfg.discovery.exclude + list(exclude or []), scope)
    typer.echo(f"Recognized: {describe_scan(result)}")
    readers = [r for r in result.refs if r.reads_dataset]
    typer.echo(f"\nFiles that read {data_file.name}: {len(readers)}" if readers else
               f"\nNo source file mentions {data_file.name}. (Try --scope all, or check --root.)")
    for r in result.refs:
        why = "reads dataset via " + r.via if r.reads_dataset else "column text match only"
        typer.echo(f"  {r.file} [{r.language}] ({why})")
        for col, lines in r.columns.items():
            typer.echo(f"      '{col}' at line {', '.join(map(str, lines))}")
    text = consumers_yaml(result)
    if output:
        _write_new(output, text, cfg, force)
        typer.echo(f"\nSuggested consumers written to {output}. Review, then merge into {cfg.source or DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME}.")
    else:
        typer.echo("\n" + text.rstrip())


@app.command("suggest")
@guarded
def suggest_cmd(data_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="CSV or Parquet file to learn rules from."),
                config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
                output: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o",
                                                      help="Write the suggested YAML to this NEW file."),
                force: bool = typer.Option(False, "--force", help="Allow overwriting an existing --output file."),
                ai: bool = typer.Option(False, "--ai", help="Also ask the AI for extra ideas (aggregates only).")):
    """Propose validation rules from the data profile. Never edits your datapulse.yml."""
    cfg = load_config(config)
    df = load_dataframe(data_file)
    if len(df) == 0:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{data_file} has no rows; nothing to learn rules from.")
    profile = profile_dataframe(df, cfg.max_categories)
    text = render_yaml(suggest_rules(profile))
    if output:
        _write_new(output, text, cfg, force)
        typer.echo(f"Suggested rules written to {output}. Review them, then merge what you want into "
                   f"{cfg.source or DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME}.")
    else:
        typer.echo(text)
        typer.echo("# Nothing was applied. Copy the blocks you want into datapulse.yml.")
    if ai:
        typer.echo("\nAI-generated suggestions (unverified; generated from aggregate statistics only):")
        typer.echo(suggest_with_ai(profile, cfg))


@app.command("draft-rules")
@guarded
def draft_rules_cmd(request: str = typer.Argument(..., help='What you want, in plain English, e.g. "order_id must '
                                                             'be unique and alert if amount shifts".'),
                    data_file: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--data", help="Data file, so the AI knows the "
                                                                                  "real column names (aggregates only)."),
                    config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
                    output: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--output", "-o", help="Write the draft to this NEW file."),
                    force: bool = typer.Option(False, "--force", help="Allow overwriting an existing --output file.")):
    """Turn a plain-English request into a validated datapulse.yml (AI). Never edits your config."""
    from .config import STARTER_CONFIG
    cfg = load_config(config)
    current = Path(cfg.source).read_text(encoding="utf-8") if cfg.source else STARTER_CONFIG
    profile = profile_dataframe(load_dataframe(data_file), cfg.max_categories) if data_file else None
    with Console(stderr=True).status("Drafting rules…"):
        draft = draft_rules(request, current, profile, cfg)
    if output:
        _write_new(output, draft["config_yaml"], cfg, force)
        typer.echo(f"Draft written to {output}. Review it, then replace {cfg.source or DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME} if you like it.")
    else:
        typer.echo(draft["config_yaml"])
    typer.echo(f"# What changed: {draft['summary']}")
    for item in draft["not_supported"]:
        typer.echo(f"# Not expressible as a rule: {item}")


@app.command("fix")
@guarded
def fix_cmd(data_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="The data file that failed its checks."),
            output: Path = typer.Option(..., "--output", "-o", help="Where to write the repaired copy (a NEW file)."),
            config: Optional[Path] = CONFIG_OPT,
            baseline: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--baseline", help="Baseline file (overrides config)."),
            reference: Optional[Path] = typer.Option(None, "--reference", help="The known-good data file, so the "
                                                                               "ML drift check is re-run too."),
            force: bool = typer.Option(False, "--force", help="Allow overwriting an existing --output file.")):
    """Let the AI repair agent fix a copy of a file, then re-check it (AI). Never changes the original."""
    from .fix import RepairSession, run_repair
    console = Console()
    cfg = load_config(config)
    if output.resolve() == data_file.resolve():
        raise DataPulseError("--output must be a new file; the original is never overwritten.")
    session = RepairSession(load_dataframe(data_file), load_baseline(baseline or Path(cfg.baseline)), cfg,
                            reference=load_dataframe(reference) if reference else None)
    with console.status("AI repair agent is working…"):
        result = run_repair(session, on_step=lambda tool, args: console.print(
            Text(f"  agent: {tool} {json.dumps(args) if args else ''}", style="dim")))
    fixed = result.pop("fixed_frame")
    _write_new(output, fixed.to_csv(index=False), cfg, force)
    console.print(Text(f"\nIssues: {result['before']['total']} -> {result['after']['total']} "
                       f"(rows {result['rows']['before']:,} -> {result['rows']['after']:,}). {result['summary']}", style="bold"))
    for op in result["operations"]:
        console.print(Text(f"  fixed: {op['effect']}  ({op['reason']})"))
    for item in result["not_fixed"]:
        console.print(Text(f"  left for a person: {item['issue']}: {item['why']}", style="yellow"))
    console.print(Text(f"Repaired copy written to {output}. Review it before using it.", style="dim"))


@app.command("github-outputs")
@guarded
def github_outputs_cmd(report_json: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="JSON report from `datapulse check --json`.")):
    """Print `key=value` lines (status, issue-count, schema-changed, affected-consumers) for $GITHUB_OUTPUT."""
    if not report_json.exists():
        raise DataPulseError(f"Report not found: {report_json}. Run `datapulse check --json {report_json}` first.")
    try:
        doc = json.loads(report_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except ValueError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{report_json} is not valid JSON: {e}")
    for key, value in action_outputs(doc).items():
        typer.echo(f"{key}={value}")


@app.command("comment")
@guarded
def comment_cmd(report_file: Path = typer.Argument(..., help="Markdown report from `datapulse check`."),
                repo: Optional[str] = typer.Option(None, "--repo", help="owner/name (default: $GITHUB_REPOSITORY)."),
                pr: Optional[int] = typer.Option(None, "--pr", help="Pull request number (default: from the event).")):
    """Create or update the DataPulse comment on a pull request (uses $GITHUB_TOKEN)."""
    token = os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN")
    if not token:
        raise DataPulseError("GITHUB_TOKEN is not set. In Actions pass `env: GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}`.")
    if not report_file.exists():
        raise DataPulseError(f"Report not found: {report_file}. Run `datapulse check` first.")
    env_repo, env_pr = detect_pr_context(os.environ)
    repo, pr = repo or env_repo, pr or env_pr
    if not repo or not pr:
        raise DataPulseError("Could not determine the repository and PR number; pass --repo and --pr.")
    action = upsert_pr_comment(report_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), repo, pr, token, default_api_url())
    typer.echo(f"PR comment {action} on {repo}#{pr}.")

FILE [70/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\compare.py

"""Their columns next to ours: what a mapping recipe does to each column, and what the rules say about the result.

For every column of the standard this reports which source column feeds it, the steps applied on the way, the
column's statistics before mapping, after mapping and in the standard, and the issues the checks raise on it.
Sample values are only included when the project's report settings allow showing values.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd

from .checks import run_checks
from .config import Config
from .fix import OPERATIONS, RepairError, _counts
from .profile import NUMERIC_KINDS, column_kind, numeric_values

ROW_STEPS = ("remove_duplicates", "drop_rows_missing")
SAMPLES = 3


def compare_mapping(df: pd.DataFrame, baseline: Dict[str, Any], cfg: Config, recipe: List[Dict[str, Any]],
                    reference: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    profile = baseline["profile"]
    lineage: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {c: {"source": c, "steps": []} for c in df.columns}
    dropped: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    table_steps: List[str] = []
    problems: List[str] = []
    mapped = df
    for n, op in enumerate(recipe, 1):
        name, args = op["operation"], op["args"]
        try:
            mapped, effect = OPERATIONS[name](mapped, profile, **args)
        except (RepairError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
            problems.append(f"Step {n} ({name.replace('_', ' ')}) no longer applies: {e}")
            continue
        step = {"operation": name, "effect": effect}
        if name == "rename_column":
            entry = lineage.pop(args["column"])
            entry["steps"].append(step)
            lineage[args["new_name"]] = entry
        elif name == "drop_column":
            dropped.append({"name": lineage.pop(args["column"])["source"], "effect": effect})
        elif name in ROW_STEPS:
            table_steps.append(effect)
        elif args.get("column") in lineage:
            lineage[args["column"]]["steps"].append(step)

    after = run_checks(baseline, mapped, cfg, reference=reference)
    before = run_checks(baseline, df, cfg, reference=reference)
    by_column: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, str]]] = {}
    for issue in after.issues:
        for col in issue.columns:
            by_column.setdefault(col, []).append({"check": issue.check, "severity": issue.severity,
                                                  "title": issue.title, "detail": issue.detail})
    show = cfg.report.show_values

    columns = []
    for name, standard in profile["columns"].items():
        entry = lineage.get(name)
        issues = by_column.get(name, [])
        if entry is None:
            columns.append({"name": name, "source": None, "status": "missing", "steps": [], "issues": issues,
                            "standard": _from_profile(standard), "before": None, "after": None})
            continue
        steps = entry["steps"]
        changed = [s for s in steps if s["operation"] != "rename_column"]
        status = ("flagged" if any(i["severity"] != "info" for i in issues)
                  else "converted" if changed else "renamed" if entry["source"] != name else "same")
        columns.append({
            "name": name, "source": entry["source"], "status": status, "steps": steps, "issues": issues,
            "standard": _from_profile(standard),
            "before": _stats(df[entry["source"]], show),
            "after": _stats(mapped[name], show),
        })
    extra = [{"name": entry["source"], "as": current, "after": _stats(mapped[current], show)}
             for current, entry in lineage.items() if current not in profile["columns"]]
    return {
        "columns": columns, "extra": extra, "dropped": dropped, "table_steps": table_steps, "problems": problems,
        "rows": {"before": len(df), "after": len(mapped), "standard": profile["rows"]},
        "before": _counts(before.issues), "after": _counts(after.issues),
        "table_issues": [{"check": i.check, "severity": i.severity, "title": i.title, "detail": i.detail}
                         for i in after.issues if not i.columns],
        "show_values": show,
    }


def _number(x: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    if x is None:
        return None
    x = float(x)
    return round(x, 4) if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _from_profile(info: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    num = info.get("numeric") or {}
    top = sorted((info.get("categories") or {}).items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])[:SAMPLES]
    return {"kind": info["kind"], "null_rate": _number(info["null_rate"]), "mean": _number(num.get("mean")),
            "min": _number(num.get("min")), "max": _number(num.get("max")), "top": [k for k, _ in top]}


def _stats(s: pd.Series, show_values: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    kind = column_kind(s)
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {"kind": kind, "null_rate": _number(s.isna().mean()) if len(s) else None,
                           "mean": None, "min": None, "max": None, "top": [], "samples": []}
    if kind in NUMERIC_KINDS:
        values = numeric_values(s)
        if not values.empty:
            out.update(mean=_number(values.mean()), min=_number(values.min()), max=_number(values.max()))
    elif kind == "string":
        out["top"] = [str(v) for v in s.dropna().astype(str).value_counts().head(SAMPLES).index]
    if show_values:
        out["samples"] = [str(v) for v in s.head(SAMPLES).tolist()]
    return out

"""Loading and strict validation of datapulse.yml."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import yaml

from .errors import DataPulseError

SEVERITIES = ("critical", "warning", "info")
LEVEL = {"info": 0, "warning": 1, "critical": 2}
FAIL_ON_CHOICES = ("critical", "warning", "info", "never")
DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME = "datapulse.yml"


@dataclass
class SchemaCfg:
    missing_columns: str = "critical"
    added_columns: str = "warning"
    type_changes: str = "critical"


@dataclass
class RowCountCfg:
    severity: str = "warning"
    max_change_pct: float = 20.0
    min_rows: Optional[int] = None


@dataclass
class NullRateCfg:
    severity: str = "critical"
    max_increase: float = 0.05
    columns: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class CategoricalCfg:
    severity: str = "warning"
    max_unexpected_rate: float = 0.0
    columns: Optional[List[str]] = None


@dataclass
class DuplicatesCfg:
    severity: str = "critical"
    max_duplicate_rate: float = 0.0
    columns: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class DistributionCfg:
    severity: str = "warning"
    psi_threshold: float = 0.2
    max_mean_shift_std: float = 1.0
    columns: Optional[List[str]] = None


@dataclass
class MLDriftCfg:
    severity: str = "warning"
    max_auc: float = 0.7  # 0.5 = the model can't tell the files apart; 1.0 = it always can
    max_rows: int = 5000
    exclude: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Consumer:
    name: str
    type: str
    columns: List[str]
    owner: str = ""
    description: str = ""


@dataclass
class ReportCfg:
    markdown: Optional[str] = "datapulse-report.md"
    max_examples: int = 5
    show_values: bool = True


@dataclass
class DiscoveryCfg:
    enabled: bool = False
    root: str = "."
    scope: str = "readers"  # readers: only files that mention the data file; all: every source file
    exclude: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class AICfg:
    enabled: bool = False
    model: str = "claude-sonnet-5-5"
    api_key_env: str = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"


@dataclass
class Config:
    baseline: str = ".datapulse/baseline.json"
    fail_on: str = "critical"
    max_categories: int = 50
    schema: SchemaCfg = field(default_factory=SchemaCfg)
    row_count: RowCountCfg = field(default_factory=RowCountCfg)
    null_rate: NullRateCfg = field(default_factory=NullRateCfg)
    categorical: CategoricalCfg = field(default_factory=CategoricalCfg)
    duplicates: DuplicatesCfg = field(default_factory=DuplicatesCfg)
    distribution: DistributionCfg = field(default_factory=DistributionCfg)
    ml_drift: MLDriftCfg = field(default_factory=MLDriftCfg)
    consumers: List[Consumer] = field(default_factory=list)
    report: ReportCfg = field(default_factory=ReportCfg)
    ai: AICfg = field(default_factory=AICfg)
    discovery: DiscoveryCfg = field(default_factory=DiscoveryCfg)
    source: Optional[str] = None


STARTER_CONFIG = """\
# DataPulse configuration. Docs: README.md
version: 1

# Where `datapulse baseline` stores (and `datapulse check` reads) the baseline profile.
baseline: .datapulse/baseline.json

# Exit with a nonzero code when an issue at or above this severity is found.
# One of: critical | warning | info | never
fail_on: critical

checks:
  # Each severity is one of: critical | warning | info | off
  schema:
    missing_columns: critical
    added_columns: warning
    type_changes: critical
  row_count:
    severity: warning
    max_change_pct: 20        # allowed % change in row count vs. baseline
  null_rate:
    severity: critical
    max_increase: 0.05        # allowed absolute increase (0.05 = 5 percentage points)
    # columns:
    #   customer_id: 0.0      # per-column override
  categorical:
    severity: warning
    max_unexpected_rate: 0.0  # share of rows with a value not seen in the baseline
  duplicates:
    severity: critical
    max_duplicate_rate: 0.0
    # columns: [order_id]     # key columns that must be unique (omit for whole-row duplicates)
  distribution:
    severity: warning
    psi_threshold: 0.2        # Population Stability Index limit
    max_mean_shift_std: 1.0   # allowed mean shift, in baseline standard deviations
  ml_drift:                   # a model learns to tell baseline rows from new rows; needs the baseline file
    severity: warning
    max_auc: 0.7              # 0.5 = indistinguishable (no drift), 1.0 = completely different

# Map columns to the things that read them so reports can show who is affected.
# consumers:
#   revenue_dashboard:
#     type: dashboard
#     owner: finance-analytics
#     columns: [amount, created_at]

report:
  markdown: datapulse-report.md   # set to null to skip writing the Markdown report
  max_examples: 5
  show_values: true               # false hides example data values from reports

# Find code (any language) that reads the data file and references its columns, and show it in reports.
# Heuristic text search; also available as `datapulse check --discover` and `datapulse scan`.
discovery:
  enabled: false
  root: .
  scope: readers                  # readers | all
  exclude: []                     # extra directory names to skip

ai:
  enabled: false                  # or pass --ai; needs the API key env var below
  model: claude-sonnet-5-5
  api_key_env: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
"""


def _fail(msg: str):
    raise DataPulseError(f"Invalid configuration: {msg}")


def _mapping(raw, path: str, allowed) -> dict:
    if raw is None:
        return {}
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        _fail(f"'{path}' must be a mapping")
    bad = sorted(str(k) for k in raw if k not in allowed)
    if bad:
        _fail(f"unknown key(s) {bad} in '{path}'. Allowed keys: {sorted(allowed)}")
    return raw


def _choice(*choices):
    def check(v, path):
        if v not in choices:
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be one of {list(choices)}, got {v!r}")
        return v
    return check


def _number(lo: Optional[float] = None, hi: Optional[float] = None):
    def check(v, path):
        if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float)):
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be a number, got {v!r}")
        if lo is not None and v < lo:
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be >= {lo}, got {v!r}")
        if hi is not None and v > hi:
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be <= {hi}, got {v!r}")
        return float(v)
    return check


def _int(lo: int = 0):
    def check(v, path):
        if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, int):
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be an integer, got {v!r}")
        if v < lo:
            _fail(f"'{path}' must be >= {lo}, got {v!r}")
        return v
    return check


def _bool(v, path: str) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(v, bool):
        _fail(f"'{path}' must be true or false, got {v!r}")
    return v


def _str(v, path: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not v.strip():
        _fail(f"'{path}' must be a non-empty string, got {v!r}")
    return v


def _str_list(v, path: str) -> List[str]:
    if not isinstance(v, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in v):
        _fail(f"'{path}' must be a list of column names")
    return list(v)


def _rate_map(v, path: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
    return {str(c): _number(0, 1)(x, f"{path}.{c}") for c, x in _mapping(v, path, v or {}).items()}


def _nullable(check):
    """A validator whose explicit `null` is stored (by default `null` keeps the default value)."""
    check.nullable = True
    return check


SEVERITY = _choice(*SEVERITIES, "off")
MIN_ML_ROWS = 100
RATE = _number(0, 1)
# section path -> (attribute on Config, {key: validator})
SECTIONS = {
    "checks.schema": ("schema", {"missing_columns": SEVERITY, "added_columns": SEVERITY, "type_changes": SEVERITY}),
    "checks.row_count": ("row_count", {"severity": SEVERITY, "max_change_pct": _number(0), "min_rows": _int()}),
    "checks.null_rate": ("null_rate", {"severity": SEVERITY, "max_increase": RATE, "columns": _rate_map}),
    "checks.categorical": ("categorical", {"severity": SEVERITY, "max_unexpected_rate": RATE, "columns": _str_list}),
    "checks.duplicates": ("duplicates", {"severity": SEVERITY, "max_duplicate_rate": RATE, "columns": _str_list}),
    "checks.distribution": ("distribution", {"severity": SEVERITY, "psi_threshold": _number(0),
                                             "max_mean_shift_std": _number(0), "columns": _str_list}),
    "checks.ml_drift": ("ml_drift", {"severity": SEVERITY, "max_auc": _number(0.5, 1), "max_rows": _int(MIN_ML_ROWS),
                                     "exclude": _str_list}),
    "report": ("report", {"markdown": _nullable(_str), "max_examples": _int(), "show_values": _bool}),
    "discovery": ("discovery", {"enabled": _bool, "root": _str, "scope": _choice("readers", "all"),
                                "exclude": _str_list}),
    "ai": ("ai", {"enabled": _bool, "model": _str, "api_key_env": _str}),
}
TOP = {"baseline": _str, "fail_on": _choice(*FAIL_ON_CHOICES), "max_categories": _int(1)}


def _apply(target, raw: dict, path: str, spec: dict) -> None:
    for key, check in spec.items():
        if key not in raw:
            continue
        value = raw[key]
        if value is None:
            if getattr(check, "nullable", False):
                setattr(target, key, None)
            continue
        setattr(target, key, check(value, f"{path}.{key}" if path else key))


def parse_config(raw) -> Config:
    top = _mapping(raw, "<root>", {"version", "checks", "consumers", *TOP, *SECTIONS})
    if top.get("version", 1) != 1:
        _fail(f"unsupported 'version' {top.get('version')!r} (this release supports 1)")
    cfg = Config()
    _apply(cfg, top, "", TOP)
    checks = _mapping(top.get("checks"), "checks", {p.split(".")[1] for p in SECTIONS if p.startswith("checks.")})
    for path, (attr, spec) in SECTIONS.items():
        parent, _, name = path.rpartition(".")
        raw_section = (checks if parent else top).get(name)
        _apply(getattr(cfg, attr), _mapping(raw_section, path, spec), path, spec)

    cons = top.get("consumers")
    if cons is not None and not isinstance(cons, dict):
        _fail("'consumers' must be a mapping of consumer name -> definition")
    for name, spec in (cons or {}).items():
        p = f"consumers.{name}"
        spec = _mapping(spec, p, {"type", "columns", "owner", "description"})
        if "columns" not in spec:
            _fail(f"'{p}.columns' is required")
        cfg.consumers.append(Consumer(name=str(name), type=_str(spec.get("type", "other"), f"{p}.type"),
                                      columns=_str_list(spec["columns"], f"{p}.columns"),
                                      owner=str(spec.get("owner", "")), description=str(spec.get("description", ""))))
    return cfg


def load_config(path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Config:
    """Load `path` (must exist), or ./datapulse.yml if present, else defaults."""
    explicit = path is not None
    p = Path(path) if explicit else Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_NAME)
    if not p.exists():
        if explicit:
            raise DataPulseError(f"Config file not found: {p}")
        return Config()
    try:
        text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Could not read {p}: {e}")
    return parse_config_text(text, str(p))


def parse_config_text(text: str, origin: str) -> Config:
    """Parse and validate configuration text (a file, or a file read from a git ref)."""
    try:
        raw = yaml.safe_load(text)
    except yaml.YAMLError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{origin} is not valid YAML: {e}")
    try:
        cfg = parse_config(raw)
    except DataPulseError as e:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{origin}: {e}")
    cfg.source = origin
    return cfg

FILE [72/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\destinations.py
================================================================================

"""Destinations: write a unified table into the company's own database.

The connection URL works like the "Database" source (PostgreSQL/Supabase, MySQL, SQL Server, MongoDB, any SQLAlchemy
database). Only the one named table (or collection) is ever written: "replace" swaps its contents for the new data,
"append" adds the new rows. Errors never show the password.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from .sources.base import SourceError, _scrub, _secrets, error_text
from .sources.database import Database, type_label

MODES = ("replace", "append")
TABLE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][\w$]*(\.[A-Za-z_][\w$]*)?$")  # table or schema.table (MongoDB: database.collection)
CHUNK_ROWS = 1000


def check_settings(settings: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
    if not settings.get("url"):
        raise SourceError("Paste your database's connection URL.")
    table = (settings.get("table") or "").strip()
    if not TABLE.match(table):
        raise SourceError("Name one table, like unified_orders or analytics.unified_orders (letters, numbers, _).")
    if settings.get("mode") not in MODES:
        raise SourceError("Choose replace or append.")
    return {"url": settings["url"].strip(), "table": table, "mode": settings["mode"]}


def _sql_engine(url: str):
    """A SQLAlchemy engine for a connection URL, built by the same plug-in the Database source would pick."""
    found, inner = Database().target({"url": url, "query": "unused"})
    return found.engine(inner)


def _mongo(url: str, table: str):
    found, inner = Database().target({"url": url, "query": table})
    client = found.client(inner)
    return client, found.database(client, inner)[inner["collection"]]


def _split(table: str) -> Tuple[Any, str]:
    schema, _, name = table.rpartition(".")
    return schema or None, name


def _guarded(settings: Dict[str, str], action):
    """Run a database step; errors are labelled with the database type and never contain the password."""
    label = type_label(settings["url"])
    try:
        return action()
    except SourceError as e:
        raise SourceError(_scrub(str(e), _secrets("database", settings))) from None
    except Exception as e:
        raise SourceError(f"{label}: {_scrub(error_text(e), _secrets('database', settings))}") from None


def _records(df: pd.DataFrame):
    """Rows as plain JSON-able values (NaN -> None, timestamps -> ISO text) for MongoDB."""
    return json.loads(df.to_json(orient="records", date_format="iso"))


def describe_target(settings: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """Connect and say what is there now, without writing anything."""
    settings = check_settings(settings)
    label = type_label(settings["url"])

    def run():
        if Database().target({"url": settings["url"], "query": settings["table"]})[0].kind == "mongodb":
            client, collection = _mongo(settings["url"], settings["table"])
            try:
                count = collection.estimated_document_count()
            finally:
                client.close()
            return f"{label}: connected. '{settings['table']}' has {count:,} documents."
        from sqlalchemy import inspect, text

        schema, name = _split(settings["table"])
        engine = _sql_engine(settings["url"])
        try:
            with engine.connect() as conn:
                if not inspect(conn).has_table(name, schema=schema):
                    return f"{label}: connected. '{settings['table']}' doesn't exist yet; the first send creates it."
                quoted = ".".join(conn.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote(p) for p in settings["table"].split("."))
                rows = conn.execute(text(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {quoted}")).scalar()
        finally:
            engine.dispose()
        return f"{label}: connected. '{settings['table']}' has {rows:,} rows."
    return _guarded(settings, run)


def send(settings: Dict[str, str], df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    """Write df into the destination table; returns a short summary of what was written."""
    settings = check_settings(settings)
    label, table, mode = type_label(settings["url"]), settings["table"], settings["mode"]
    if df.empty:
        raise SourceError("There are no rows to send.")

    def run():
        if Database().target({"url": settings["url"], "query": table})[0].kind == "mongodb":
            client, collection = _mongo(settings["url"], table)
            try:
                if mode == "replace":
                    collection.delete_many({})
                collection.insert_many(_records(df))
            finally:
                client.close()
        else:
            schema, name = _split(table)
            engine = _sql_engine(settings["url"])
            try:
                with engine.begin() as conn:  # one transaction: all rows or none
                    df.to_sql(name, conn, schema=schema, if_exists=mode, index=False, chunksize=CHUNK_ROWS)
            finally:
                engine.dispose()
        verb = "Replaced the contents of" if mode == "replace" else "Added the rows to"
        return f"{verb} '{table}' in {label}: {len(df):,} rows."
    return _guarded(settings, run)

class DataPulseError(Exception):
    """A user-facing error (bad file, bad config, unreachable service...)."""
