"""One "Database" source for every database: paste a connection URL and the right driver is picked from it.

PostgreSQL (and Supabase, Neon, Railway, RDS, Redshift), MySQL/MariaDB, SQL Server, MongoDB (and Atlas,
DocumentDB, Cosmos DB), and anything else with a SQLAlchemy driver (Snowflake, BigQuery, Oracle, Databricks...).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Dict, Tuple

from .base import REGISTRY, Field, Source, SourceError, error_text, plugin, register

# URL scheme (before any "+driver") -> plug-in that reads it
SCHEMES = {
    "mongodb": "mongodb",
    "postgres": "postgres", "postgresql": "postgres", "redshift": "postgres", "timescaledb": "postgres",
    "mysql": "mysql", "mariadb": "mysql",
    "mssql": "sqlserver", "sqlserver": "sqlserver",
}


def detect(url: str) -> str:
    """The plug-in kind for a connection URL: one of SCHEMES' values, or "sql" for any other SQLAlchemy URL."""
    scheme = url.strip().split("://", 1)[0].lower() if "://" in url else ""
    if not scheme:
        raise SourceError("Paste a connection URL, like postgresql://user:password@host:5432/database")
    if scheme.split("+")[0] == "sqlite":
        raise SourceError("SQLite files can't be reached from the server; upload the data as a File instead.")
    return SCHEMES.get(scheme.split("+")[0], "sql")


def type_label(url: str) -> str:
    """What the URL points at, for display: "PostgreSQL", "MongoDB"... ("Database" if it can't be told)."""
    try:
        kind = detect(url)
    except SourceError:
        return "Database"
    return "SQL database" if kind == "sql" else REGISTRY[kind].label


@register
class Database(Source):
    kind, label, group = "database", "Database", "Databases"
    description = "Any database: PostgreSQL, Supabase, MySQL, SQL Server, MongoDB, Snowflake, BigQuery, Oracle…"
    fields = (
        Field("url", "Connection URL", "postgresql://user:password@host:5432/database", secret=True,
              help="Paste it from your database's dashboard. DataPulse works out the type from it."),
        Field("query", "Table, query or collection", "orders", multiline=True,
              help="A table name or one SELECT query. For MongoDB: a collection, or database.collection."),
    )

    def target(self, settings: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[Source, Dict[str, str]]:
        kind = detect(settings["url"])
        found = plugin(kind)  # explains which package is missing, if one is
        if kind == "mongodb":
            database, _, collection = settings["query"].strip().rpartition(".")
            return found, {"url": settings["url"], "database": database, "collection": collection}
        return found, {"url": settings["url"], "query": settings["query"]}

    def run(self, settings, action):
        """Run a step on the detected plug-in; driver errors are labelled with the detected type."""
        found, inner = self.target(settings)
        try:
            return action(found, inner)
        except SourceError:
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            raise SourceError(f"{type_label(settings['url'])}: {error_text(e)}") from None

    def test(self, settings):
        return f"{type_label(settings['url'])}: {self.run(settings, lambda found, inner: found.test(inner))}"

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        return self.run(settings, lambda found, inner: found.fetch(inner, max_rows))

"""An uploaded file: CSV, Excel, JSON or Parquet, or a document the AI reads at upload. Edit the source to replace it."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

from .base import Field, Source, SourceError, register

STORED = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{32}/[^/\\]+$")  # "<upload id>/<original file name>"


def files_dir() -> Path:
    """Where uploaded source files are kept; the server sets DATAPULSE_SOURCE_FILES."""
    folder = os.getenv("DATAPULSE_SOURCE_FILES")
    if not folder:
        raise SourceError("File sources are not set up on this server (DATAPULSE_SOURCE_FILES).")
    return Path(folder).resolve()


def stored_path(value: str) -> Path:
    root = files_dir()
    path = (root / value).resolve()
    if not STORED.match(value) or root not in path.parents or not path.is_file():
        raise SourceError("The uploaded file is missing; upload it again.")
    return path


@register
class File(Source):
    kind, label, group = "file", "File", "Files & links"
    description = ("Upload a spreadsheet (CSV, Excel, JSON, Parquet) or a document (PDF, Word, email, text, HTML, image). "
                   "A PDF of a table is read as that table. Use the sample orders file to try it. "
                   "Upload a newer version by editing the source.")
    has_sample = True
    fields = (Field("file", "File", "CSV, Excel, JSON, PDF, Word, email, text, or image · up to 25 MB", input="file"),)

    def test(self, settings):
        df = self.fetch(settings, 0)
        return f"Read the file: {len(df):,} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns."

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        from .connected import read_any

        path = stored_path(settings["file"])
        return read_any(path, path.name)

"""Download a public file URL: data files (CSV / JSON / JSONL / Parquet / Excel) or documents (PDF, Word…)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.io import ARCHIVES, INPUTS
from datapulse.sources.http import fetch_bytes, require_str

SUPPORTED = tuple(e for e in INPUTS if e not in ARCHIVES)

# Content-Type → extension when the URL has no useful suffix.
_CONTENT_TYPES = {
    "text/csv": ".csv",
    "application/csv": ".csv",
    "application/json": ".json",
    "application/x-ndjson": ".jsonl",
    "application/parquet": ".parquet",
    "application/vnd.apache.parquet": ".parquet",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet": ".xlsx",
    "application/vnd.ms-excel": ".xls",
    "application/pdf": ".pdf",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": ".docx",
    "text/html": ".html",
    "text/plain": ".txt",
    "text/markdown": ".md",
    "message/rfc822": ".eml",
    "image/png": ".png",
    "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
    "image/webp": ".webp",
}


def _ext_from_disposition(header: str) -> str | None:
    match = re.search(r'filename\*?=(?:UTF-8\'\')?"?([^";]+)"?', header, re.I)
    if not match:
        return None
    name = unquote(match.group(1).strip())
    suffix = Path(name).suffix.lower()
    return suffix if suffix in SUPPORTED else None


def _ext_from_url(url: str) -> str | None:
    path = unquote(urlparse(url).path)
    suffix = Path(path).suffix.lower()
    return suffix if suffix in SUPPORTED else None


def _ext_from_content_type(ctype: str) -> str | None:
    base = ctype.split(";")[0].strip().lower()
    return _CONTENT_TYPES.get(base)


class FileLinkSource:
    kind = "file_link"

    def fetch(self, config: dict, secrets: dict, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
        url = require_str(config, "url", label="File URL")
        body, headers, final = fetch_bytes(url)
        if not body:
            raise DataPulseError("Downloaded file is empty")

        ext = (
            _ext_from_disposition(headers.get("content-disposition", ""))
            or _ext_from_url(final)
            or _ext_from_url(url)
            or _ext_from_content_type(headers.get("content-type", ""))
        )
        if not ext:
            # Peek at bytes for JSON array / NDJSON heuristics.
            sample = body.lstrip()[:1]
            if sample in (b"[", b"{"):
                ext = ".json"
            else:
                raise DataPulseError(
                    "Could not detect file type. Use a URL ending in .csv, .json, .jsonl, .parquet, .xlsx, "
                    ".pdf, .docx, .txt, .md or .html"
                )

        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = dest_dir / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{ext}"
        path.write_bytes(body)
        return path

FILE [93/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\sources\google_drive.py

"""Download a publicly shared Google Drive file."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

import pandas as pd

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.sources.base import Field, Source, register
from datapulse.io import INPUTS
from datapulse.sources.file_link import SUPPORTED
from datapulse.sources.google_sheets import GoogleSheetsSource, parse_sheet_url
from datapulse.sources.http import fetch_bytes, require_str

_FILE_ID = re.compile(r"/file/d/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)")
_CONFIRM = re.compile(r"confirm=([0-9A-Za-z_-]+)")


def parse_drive_file_id(url: str) -> str:
    url = url.strip()
    match = _FILE_ID.search(url)
    if match:
        return match.group(1)
    qs = parse_qs(urlparse(url).query)
    if "id" in qs and qs["id"][0]:
        return qs["id"][0]
    raise DataPulseError(
        "Not a Google Drive file URL. Paste a link like "
        "https://drive.google.com/file/d/…/view or …/open?id=…"
    )


def _download_url(file_id: str, confirm: str | None = None) -> str:
    base = f"https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id={file_id}"
    return f"{base}&confirm={confirm}" if confirm else base


def _ext_from_disposition(header: str) -> str | None:
    match = re.search(r'filename\*?=(?:UTF-8\'\')?"?([^";]+)"?', header, re.I)
    if not match:
        return None
    suffix = Path(match.group(1).strip().strip('"')).suffix.lower()
    return suffix if suffix in SUPPORTED else None


class GoogleDriveSource:
    kind = "google_drive"

    def fetch(self, config: dict, secrets: dict, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
        url = require_str(config, "url", label="Google Drive URL")
        # A Sheets link pasted into the Drive form should still work.
        try:
            parse_sheet_url(url)
        except DataPulseError:
            pass
        else:
            return GoogleSheetsSource().fetch({"url": url}, secrets, dest_dir)

        file_id = parse_drive_file_id(url)
        body, headers, _ = fetch_bytes(_download_url(file_id))

        # Large files get an interstitial HTML page with a confirm token.
        if b"confirm=" in body[:8000] and b"<html" in body[:500].lower():
            token_match = _CONFIRM.search(body.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore"))
            if not token_match:
                raise DataPulseError(
                    "Google Drive asked for a download confirmation we could not parse. "
                    "Make sure the file is shared as 'Anyone with the link can view'."
                )
            body, headers, _ = fetch_bytes(_download_url(file_id, token_match.group(1)))

        if b"<html" in body[:500].lower() and (
            b"accounts.google.com" in body[:2000].lower() or b"Sign in" in body[:2000]
        ):
            raise DataPulseError(
                "This Google Drive file is not publicly readable. "
                "Share it as 'Anyone with the link can view', then try again."
            )
        if not body:
            raise DataPulseError("Downloaded Drive file is empty")

        ext = _ext_from_disposition(headers.get("content-disposition", "")) or ".csv"
        # Prefer an extension the loader understands; fall back to .bin → reject.
        if body.startswith(b"%PDF"):
            ext = ".pdf"
        if ext not in INPUTS:
            # Heuristic: JSON-looking content.
            sample = body.lstrip()[:1]
            if sample in (b"[", b"{"):
                ext = ".json"
            else:
                raise DataPulseError(
                    f"Unsupported Drive file type '{ext}'. "
                    "Share a data file (CSV, JSON, Parquet, Excel) or a document (PDF, Word, text, image)."
                )

        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = dest_dir / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{ext}"
        path.write_bytes(body)
        return path


@register
class DriveFile(Source):
    kind, label, group = "google_drive", "Google Drive", "Files & links"
    description = "A spreadsheet or document (CSV, Excel, JSON, PDF, Word, email, text) shared as 'Anyone with the link can view'."
    requires = ("httpx",)
    fields = (Field("url", "Share link", "https://drive.google.com/file/d/…/view"),)

    def _table(self, settings) -> pd.DataFrame:
        from datapulse.sources.connected import download  # data files and documents alike; temp folder removed

        return download(GoogleDriveSource(), {"url": settings["url"]}, {}, "the Google Drive file")

    def test(self, settings):
        df = self._table(settings)
        return f"Reached the file: {len(df):,} rows, {df.shape[1]} columns."

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        return self._table(settings).head(max_rows + 1)

FILE [94/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\sources\google_sheets.py

"""Pull a publicly shared Google Sheet as CSV."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.sources.http import fetch_bytes, require_str

_SHEET_ID = re.compile(r"/spreadsheets/d/([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)")


def parse_sheet_url(url: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Return (spreadsheet_id, gid). gid defaults to '0' (first tab)."""
    url = url.strip()
    match = _SHEET_ID.search(url)
    if not match:
        raise DataPulseError(
            "Not a Google Sheets URL. Paste a link like "
            "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/…/edit#gid=0"
        )
    sheet_id = match.group(1)
    parsed = urlparse(url)
    qs = parse_qs(parsed.query)
    gid = "0"
    if "gid" in qs:
        gid = qs["gid"][0]
    elif parsed.fragment:
        frag = parse_qs(parsed.fragment.replace(";", "&"))
        if "gid" in frag:
            gid = frag["gid"][0]
    return sheet_id, gid


def export_csv_url(sheet_id: str, gid: str = "0") -> str:
    return f"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/{sheet_id}/export?format=csv&gid={gid}"


def _looks_like_login_html(body: bytes) -> bool:
    head = body[:2000].lower()
    return b"<html" in head and (
        b"accounts.google.com" in head
        or b"sign in" in head
        or b"serviceLogin" in head
        or b"Enable cookies" in head
    )


class GoogleSheetsSource:
    kind = "google_sheets"

    def fetch(self, config: dict, secrets: dict, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
        url = require_str(config, "url", label="Google Sheets URL")
        sheet_id, gid = parse_sheet_url(url)
        # Prefer an explicit gid from config when the user picks a tab.
        if config.get("gid") not in (None, ""):
            gid = str(config["gid"])
        export = export_csv_url(sheet_id, gid)
        body, headers, _ = fetch_bytes(export)
        ctype = headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
        if _looks_like_login_html(body) or "text/html" in ctype:
            raise DataPulseError(
                "This Google Sheet is not publicly readable. "
                "Share it as 'Anyone with the link can view', then try again."
            )
        if not body.strip():
            raise DataPulseError("The sheet export was empty")
        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = dest_dir / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.csv"
        path.write_bytes(body)
        return path

FILE [95/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\sources\http.py

"""Safe HTTP downloads: http/https only, SSRF blocks, size cap, timeouts."""
from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import socket
from typing import Any, Dict, Mapping, Optional, Tuple
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError

# Match the server upload limit so a pull cannot fill the disk larger than a user upload.
MAX_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
TIMEOUT = 30.0


def require_str(config: dict, key: str, *, label: str | None = None) -> str:
    value = config.get(key)
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise DataPulseError(f"{label or key} is required")
    return value.strip()

_PRIVATE = (
    ipaddress.ip_network("0.0.0.0/8"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("10.0.0.0/8"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("127.0.0.0/8"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("169.254.0.0/16"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("172.16.0.0/12"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("192.168.0.0/16"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("::1/128"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("fc00::/7"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("fe80::/10"),
)


def _check_host(hostname: str) -> None:
    """Reject localhost and private/link-local addresses (SSRF)."""
    host = hostname.lower().rstrip(".")
    if host in ("localhost", "metadata.google.internal"):
        raise DataPulseError("URL host is not allowed (localhost / metadata)")
    try:
        ips = {info[4][0] for info in socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)}
    except socket.gaierror as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Could not resolve host '{hostname}': {exc}") from exc
    if not ips:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Could not resolve host '{hostname}'")
    for raw in ips:
        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(raw)
        if any(ip in net for net in _PRIVATE):
            raise DataPulseError(f"URL host resolves to a private address ({ip}); blocked for safety")


def validate_url(url: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(url, str) or not url.strip():
        raise DataPulseError("URL is required")
    url = url.strip()
    parsed = urlparse(url)
    if parsed.scheme not in ("http", "https"):
        raise DataPulseError("Only http and https URLs are allowed")
    if not parsed.hostname:
        raise DataPulseError("URL is missing a host")
    _check_host(parsed.hostname)
    return url


# Tests set this to an httpx.MockTransport so connectors never hit the network.
_TRANSPORT = None


def _client():
    try:
        import httpx
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise DataPulseError("httpx is required for remote sources. Install with: pip install 'datapulse[web]'") from exc
    return httpx


def fetch_bytes(
    url: str,
    *,
    method: str = "GET",
    headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,
    follow_redirects: bool = True,
    max_bytes: int = MAX_BYTES,
    timeout: float = TIMEOUT,
) -> Tuple[bytes, Dict[str, str], str]:
    """GET/POST a URL and return (body, response_headers_lowercased, final_url).

    Raises DataPulseError on bad URL, private hosts, non-2xx, or oversize body.
    """
    httpx = _client()
    url = validate_url(url)
    method = (method or "GET").upper()
    if method not in ("GET", "POST", "HEAD"):
        raise DataPulseError(f"HTTP method '{method}' is not supported")

    try:
        with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=follow_redirects, timeout=timeout, transport=_TRANSPORT) as client:
            # Re-check the host after each redirect hop.
            req_headers = dict(headers or {})
            with client.stream(method, url, headers=req_headers) as response:
                final = str(response.url)
                if follow_redirects:
                    validate_url(final)
                if response.status_code >= 400:
                    raise DataPulseError(f"Request failed with HTTP {response.status_code} for {url}")
                chunks: list[bytes] = []
                total = 0
                for chunk in response.iter_bytes():
                    total += len(chunk)
                    if total > max_bytes:
                        raise DataPulseError(f"Download exceeds the {max_bytes // (1024 * 1024)} MB limit")
                    chunks.append(chunk)
                body = b"".join(chunks)
                hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in response.headers.items()}
                return body, hdrs, final
    except DataPulseError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:  # network errors, timeouts…
        raise DataPulseError(f"Could not fetch {url}: {exc}") from exc


def fetch_text(url: str, **kwargs: Any) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str], str]:
    body, headers, final = fetch_bytes(url, **kwargs)
    # Google often returns HTML login pages for private sheets; callers check content.
    charset = "utf-8"
    ctype = headers.get("content-type", "")
    if "charset=" in ctype.lower():
        charset = ctype.split("charset=", 1)[1].split(";")[0].strip() or "utf-8"
    try:
        return body.decode(charset), headers, final
    except LookupError:
        return body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), headers, final

"""A file at a link: CSV, Excel, JSON or Parquet (or a document) over HTTPS, including Google Sheets share links.

Also the simplest example of a plug-in; copy this file to add a new kind of source.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import ipaddress
import re
import socket
import tempfile
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import pandas as pd

from ..io import ARCHIVES, DOCUMENTS, EXCEL

from .anyfile import frame_from_bytes
from .base import Field, Source, SourceError, register

MAX_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024
SHEET = re.compile(r"https://docs\.google\.com/spreadsheets/d/([\w-]+)(?:/[^#?]*)?(?:[?#].*?gid=(\d+))?")
DOC = re.compile(r"https://docs\.google\.com/document/d/([\w-]+)")


def download_url(url: str) -> str:
    """Share links that point at a page rather than the file itself, rewritten to the file."""
    m = SHEET.match(url)
    if m:
        return f"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/{m.group(1)}/export?format=csv&gid={m.group(2) or 0}"
    m = DOC.match(url)
    if m:
        return f"https://docs.google.com/document/d/{m.group(1)}/export?format=docx"
    return url


def _public(host: str) -> None:
    """Refuse links to the server's own network (cloud metadata, internal services)."""
    try:
        addresses = {info[4][0] for info in socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)}
    except socket.gaierror:
        raise SourceError(f"Can't find the host '{host}'.")
    for a in addresses:
        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(a)
        if ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved:
            raise SourceError("Links to private or internal addresses are not allowed.")


class _CheckedRedirects(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        parts = urlsplit(newurl)
        if parts.scheme != "https" or not parts.hostname:
            raise SourceError("The link redirects to a non-https address.")
        _public(parts.hostname)
        return super().redirect_request(req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl)


# Types that are documents even when the URL has no file extension (a Google Doc export, a PDF download).
# text/plain and text/html are left out: a CSV is often served as plain text, and an HTML body is usually a login page.
_DOC_TYPES = {
    "application/pdf": ".pdf",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": ".docx",
    "message/rfc822": ".eml",
    "text/markdown": ".md",
    "application/zip": ".zip",
    "image/png": ".png",
    "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
    "image/webp": ".webp",
    "image/gif": ".gif",
}


def _document_name(url: str, content_type: str, data: bytes) -> str | None:
    """A file name when these bytes are a document rather than a table. None for tables and login pages."""
    suffix = Path(urlsplit(url).path).suffix.lower()
    if suffix in DOCUMENTS or suffix in ARCHIVES:
        return Path(urlsplit(url).path).name
    ctype = content_type.split(";")[0].strip().lower()
    if ctype in _DOC_TYPES:
        return "download" + _DOC_TYPES[ctype]
    if data.startswith(b"%PDF"):
        return "download.pdf"
    return None


def read_bytes(data: bytes, url: str, content_type: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    document = _document_name(url, content_type, data)
    if document:
        return frame_from_bytes(data, document)
    path = urlsplit(url).path.lower()
    ext = Path(path).suffix
    if ext in EXCEL or ext in DOCUMENTS or "application/pdf" in content_type:
        from .connected import read_any  # spreadsheets and documents are read from a file
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="datapulse-") as folder:
            target = Path(folder) / f"download{ext if ext in EXCEL + DOCUMENTS else '.pdf'}"
            target.write_bytes(data)
            return read_any(target, url)
    if path.endswith((".parquet", ".pq")) or data[:4] == b"PAR1":
        return pd.read_parquet(io.BytesIO(data))
    if path.endswith((".jsonl", ".ndjson")):
        return pd.read_json(io.BytesIO(data), lines=True, convert_dates=False)
    if path.endswith(".json") or "json" in content_type:
        return pd.read_json(io.BytesIO(data), orient="records", convert_dates=False)
    if data.lstrip()[:15].lower().startswith((b"<!doctype html", b"<html")):
        raise SourceError("That link opens a web page, not a data file. For Google Sheets, share it as "
                          "'Anyone with the link'.")
    return pd.read_csv(io.BytesIO(data))


@register
class Link(Source):
    kind, label, group = "link", "Link", "Files & links"
    description = ("A public file: CSV, JSON, Parquet, Excel, PDF, Word, email, or a Google Sheet or Google Doc "
                   "shared as 'Anyone with the link'.")
    has_sample = True
    fields = (Field("url", "Link", "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/…  or  https://example.com/orders.csv"),)

    def get(self, settings):
        url = download_url(settings["url"])
        parts = urlsplit(url)
        if parts.scheme != "https" or not parts.hostname:
            raise SourceError("Use an https:// link.")
        _public(parts.hostname)
        request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "DataPulse"})
        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_CheckedRedirects)
        with opener.open(request, timeout=30) as response:  # https only, public hosts only, redirects re-checked
            data = response.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
            content_type = response.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
        if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
            raise SourceError("The file is larger than 50 MB.")
        return data, url, content_type

    def test(self, settings):
        df = self.fetch(settings, 0)
        return f"Reached the file: {len(df):,} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns."

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        return read_bytes(*self.get(settings))

"""MongoDB (and MongoDB Atlas, Amazon DocumentDB, Azure Cosmos DB for MongoDB): documents flattened into columns."""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import json
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict

from .base import Field, Source, SourceError, flatten, register


def plain(value: Any) -> Any:
    """BSON values as plain Python: ObjectId -> text, Decimal128 -> float, nested documents kept for flattening."""
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: plain(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [plain(v) for v in value]
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool, dt.datetime)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, Decimal) or type(value).__name__ == "Decimal128":
        return float(str(value))
    return str(value)  # ObjectId, UUID, binary, timestamps...


@register
class MongoDB(Source):
    kind, label, group = "mongodb", "MongoDB", "Databases"
    description = ("Also works for MongoDB Atlas, Amazon DocumentDB and Azure Cosmos DB. Nested fields become "
                   "columns like address.city; lists are kept as text.")
    requires = ("pymongo",)
    hidden = True  # offered through the single "Database" source
    fields = (
        Field("url", "Connection URL", "mongodb+srv://user:password@cluster.mongodb.net/", secret=True),
        Field("database", "Database", "shop", required=False, help="Leave empty to use the one in the URL."),
        Field("collection", "Collection", "orders"),
        Field("filter", "Filter (optional)", '{"status": {"$ne": "test"}}', required=False, multiline=True,
              help="A MongoDB query as JSON, to pick which documents to check."),
    )

    def client(self, settings):
        from pymongo import MongoClient

        return MongoClient(settings["url"], serverSelectionTimeoutMS=10_000, connectTimeoutMS=10_000,
                           socketTimeoutMS=120_000)

    def database(self, client, settings):
        if settings.get("database"):
            return client[settings["database"]]
        try:
            return client.get_default_database()
        except Exception:
            raise SourceError("Name the database (the connection URL doesn't include one).")

    def test(self, settings):
        client = self.client(settings)
        try:
            client.admin.command("ping")
            db = self.database(client, settings)
            names = db.list_collection_names()
            if settings["collection"] not in names:
                raise SourceError(f"Connected, but database '{db.name}' has no collection '{settings['collection']}'. "
                                  f"Collections: {', '.join(sorted(names)[:10]) or 'none'}")
            count = db[settings["collection"]].estimated_document_count()
        finally:
            client.close()
        return f"Connected. '{settings['collection']}' has about {count:,} documents."

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        query: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        if settings.get("filter"):
            try:
                query = json.loads(settings["filter"])
            except ValueError as e:
                raise SourceError(f"The filter isn't valid JSON: {e}")
            if not isinstance(query, dict):
                raise SourceError("The filter must be a JSON object, like {\"status\": \"paid\"}.")
        client = self.client(settings)
        try:
            docs = list(self.database(client, settings)[settings["collection"]].find(query, limit=max_rows + 1))
        finally:
            client.close()
        if not docs:
            raise SourceError("No documents matched.")
        return flatten([plain(d) for d in docs])

"""A PDF whose pages are the rows of a table, so a reader can recover the original data."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import List

import pandas as pd


def table_pdf(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> bytes:
    """The rows of `frame` as a PDF. Each line is one CSV row, so the text is the table."""
    lines = frame.to_csv(index=False).splitlines()
    return _lines_pdf(lines)


def _lines_pdf(lines: List[str]) -> bytes:
    per_page = 55
    chunks = [lines[i:i + per_page] for i in range(0, len(lines), per_page)] or [[]]
    font_id = 3 + 2 * len(chunks)  # catalog, pages, then page + stream pairs, then the font
    objects: List[bytes] = [
        b"<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>",
        b"",  # pages object, filled once the kids are known
    ]
    kids = []
    for n, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        page_id = 3 + 2 * n
        stream_id = page_id + 1
        kids.append(f"{page_id} 0 R".encode())
        objects.append(
            f"<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 612 792] /Contents {stream_id} 0 R "
            f"/Resources << /Font << /F1 {font_id} 0 R >> >> >>".encode()
        )
        stream = _page_stream(chunk)
        objects.append(b"<< /Length %d >>\nstream\n" % len(stream) + stream + b"\nendstream")
    objects.append(b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Courier >>")
    objects[1] = b"<< /Type /Pages /Kids [" + b" ".join(kids) + f"] /Count {len(chunks)} >>".encode()
    return _assemble(objects)


def _page_stream(lines: List[str]) -> bytes:
    commands = ["BT", "/F1 9 Tf", "36 760 Td"]
    for n, line in enumerate(lines):
        escaped = line.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("(", "\\(").replace(")", "\\)")
        move = "" if n == 0 else "0 -12 Td "
        commands.append(f"{move}({escaped}) Tj")
    commands.append("ET")
    return "\n".join(commands).encode()


def _assemble(objects: List[bytes]) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray(b"%PDF-1.4\n")
    offsets = []
    for n, body in enumerate(objects, 1):
        offsets.append(len(out))
        out += f"{n} 0 obj\n".encode() + body + b"\nendobj\n"
    xref = len(out)
    out += f"xref\n0 {len(objects) + 1}\n0000000000 65535 f \n".encode()
    for offset in offsets:
        out += f"{offset:010d} 00000 n \n".encode()
    out += f"trailer\n<< /Size {len(objects) + 1} /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n{xref}\n%%EOF\n".encode()
    return bytes(out)

"""Generic REST API plug-in: paste a URL, get JSON rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.sources.base import Field, Source, flatten, register
from datapulse.sources.http import fetch_bytes, require_str


def _dig(data: Any, path: str) -> Any:
    """Follow a dotted path like 'data.items' through dicts."""
    cur = data
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(cur, dict) or part not in cur:
            raise DataPulseError(f"records_path '{path}' not found in the JSON response")
        cur = cur[part]
    return cur


def _first_list_of_objects(data: Any) -> list | None:
    """Walk a JSON value and return the first list whose items are all objects."""
    if isinstance(data, list):
        if data and all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in data):
            return data
        return None
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        for value in data.values():
            found = _first_list_of_objects(value)
            if found is not None:
                return found
    return None


def extract_records(payload: Any, records_path: str | None) -> list:
    if records_path:
        payload = _dig(payload, records_path.strip())
    if isinstance(payload, list):
        return payload
    if isinstance(payload, dict):
        # Single object → one-row table.
        found = _first_list_of_objects(payload)
        if found is not None:
            return found
        return [payload]
    raise DataPulseError("JSON response must be an object or an array of objects")


class RestSource:
    kind = "rest"

    def fetch(self, config: dict, secrets: dict, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
        url = require_str(config, "url", label="API URL")
        method = str(config.get("method") or "GET").upper()
        records_path = config.get("records_path") or None
        if records_path is not None and not isinstance(records_path, str):
            raise DataPulseError("records_path must be a string like 'data.items'")

        # Non-secret headers from config + secret headers (Authorization, etc.).
        headers: dict[str, str] = {}
        for bucket in (config.get("headers"), secrets.get("headers")):
            if not bucket:
                continue
            if not isinstance(bucket, dict):
                raise DataPulseError("headers must be an object of string → string")
            for k, v in bucket.items():
                if not isinstance(k, str) or not isinstance(v, str):
                    raise DataPulseError("Each header name and value must be a string")
                headers[k] = v

        body, response_headers, _ = fetch_bytes(url, method=method, headers=headers or None)
        ctype = response_headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
        if "json" not in ctype and not body.lstrip()[:1] in (b"{", b"["):
            raise DataPulseError(
                f"Expected a JSON response but got Content-Type '{ctype or 'unknown'}'"
            )
        try:
            payload = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
            raise DataPulseError(f"Response is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc

        records = extract_records(payload, records_path)
        if not records:
            raise DataPulseError("No rows found in the response")
        if not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in records):
            raise DataPulseError("Each row must be a JSON object")
        # Nesting is kept: the unifier decides how nested fields map to output fields.
        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = dest_dir / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.jsonl"
        path.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in records) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return path


def _headers(raw: str) -> dict:
    if not raw.strip():
        return {}
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise DataPulseError('Headers must be JSON, like {"Authorization": "Bearer …"}') from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict) or any(not isinstance(k, str) or not isinstance(v, str) for k, v in parsed.items()):
        raise DataPulseError("Each header name and value must be a string")
    return parsed


def records_frame(url: str, records_path: str = "", headers: str = "") -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The JSON at url as a table. Used by the Add source form."""
    body, response_headers, _ = fetch_bytes(url, headers=_headers(headers) or None)
    ctype = response_headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
    if "json" not in ctype and body.lstrip()[:1] not in (b"{", b"["):
        raise DataPulseError(f"Expected a JSON response but got Content-Type '{ctype or 'unknown'}'")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(f"Response is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc
    records = extract_records(payload, records_path.strip() or None)
    if not records or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in records):
        raise DataPulseError("No rows found in the response")
    return flatten(records)


@register
class RestAPI(Source):
    kind, label, group = "rest", "REST API", "Files & links"
    description = "Paste a URL that returns JSON. Use test data to try a public users API."
    requires = ("httpx",)
    has_sample = True
    fields = (
        Field("url", "URL", "https://jsonplaceholder.typicode.com/users"),
        Field("records_path", "Records path", "data.items", required=False,
              help="Dot path to the list of rows, when the rows are nested. Leave empty to find them."),
        Field("headers", "Headers (JSON)", '{"Authorization": "Bearer …"}', required=False, secret=True, multiline=True,
              help="Optional. Sent with every request and stored only on the server."),
    )

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        return records_frame(settings["url"], settings.get("records_path", ""), settings.get("headers", "")).head(max_rows + 1)

    def test(self, settings):
        df = records_frame(settings["url"], settings.get("records_path", ""), settings.get("headers", ""))
        return f"Reached the API: {len(df):,} rows, {df.shape[1]} columns."

FILE [100/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\sources\sql.py

"""SQL databases through SQLAlchemy: one plug-in class, one entry per database in the menu.

Queries are read-only by construction: a table name becomes `SELECT * FROM <table>`, anything else must be a
single SELECT/WITH statement, and it runs in a transaction that is always rolled back (and is marked read-only
where the database supports it). Use a database user with read-only permissions anyway.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from .base import Field, Source, SourceError, register

IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][\w$]*(\.[A-Za-z_][\w$]*){0,2}$")  # table, schema.table, db.schema.table
QUERY_FIELD = Field("query", "Table or SQL query", "orders  or  SELECT * FROM orders WHERE created_at > now() - interval '1 day'",
                    multiline=True, help="A table name, or one SELECT query.")


def select_sql(query: str, quote) -> str:
    q = query.strip().rstrip(";").strip()
    if IDENTIFIER.match(q):
        return "SELECT * FROM " + ".".join(quote(part) for part in q.split("."))
    body = re.sub(r"--[^\n]*|/\*.*?\*/", " ", q, flags=re.S).strip()
    if not re.match(r"(?i)^(select|with)\b", body):
        raise SourceError("Only a table name or a SELECT query can be used; DataPulse never changes your data.")
    if ";" in body:
        raise SourceError("Use a single SELECT query (no ';').")
    return q


class SQLDatabase(Source):
    group = "Databases"
    hidden = True  # offered through the single "Database" source, which picks one of these from the URL
    requires: Tuple[str, ...] = ("sqlalchemy",)
    schemes: Tuple[str, ...] = ()   # URL schemes this entry accepts (empty: any except SQLite)
    driver = ""                     # SQLAlchemy driver used when the URL doesn't name one
    connect_args: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    fields = (Field("url", "Connection URL", "", secret=True), QUERY_FIELD)

    def url(self, settings: Dict[str, str]):
        from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url
        from sqlalchemy.exc import ArgumentError

        try:
            url = make_url(settings["url"])
        except ArgumentError:
            raise SourceError(f"That doesn't look like a connection URL. Example: {self.fields[0].placeholder}")
        backend, _, driver = url.drivername.partition("+")
        backend = "postgresql" if backend == "postgres" else backend  # Heroku/Railway style
        if backend == "sqlite":
            raise SourceError("Use the SQLite file source for SQLite databases.")
        if self.schemes and backend not in self.schemes:
            raise SourceError(f"{self.label} URLs start with {self.schemes[0]}://")
        if not driver and self.driver:
            return url.set(drivername=self.driver)
        return url.set(drivername=backend + (f"+{driver}" if driver else ""))

    def engine(self, settings: Dict[str, str]):
        from sqlalchemy import create_engine
        from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool

        return create_engine(self.url(settings), poolclass=NullPool, connect_args=dict(self.connect_args))

    def begin(self, conn) -> None:
        """Hook to make the transaction read-only where the database supports it."""

    def run(self, settings, rows: int):
        """Run the source's query read-only; returns (rows, column names)."""
        from sqlalchemy import text

        engine = self.engine(settings)
        try:
            with engine.connect() as conn:
                sql = select_sql(settings["query"], conn.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote)
                trans = conn.begin()
                try:
                    self.begin(conn)
                    result = conn.execute(text(sql))
                    return result.fetchmany(rows), list(result.keys())
                finally:
                    trans.rollback()
        finally:
            engine.dispose()

    def test(self, settings):
        _, columns = self.run(settings, 1)
        shown = ", ".join(columns[:8]) + ("…" if len(columns) > 8 else "")
        return f"Connected, and the query works: {len(columns)} column(s) ({shown})."

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        rows, columns = self.run(settings, max_rows + 1)
        return pd.DataFrame.from_records([tuple(r) for r in rows], columns=columns)


@register
class Postgres(SQLDatabase):
    kind, label = "postgres", "PostgreSQL"
    description = "Also works for Supabase, Neon, Railway, Amazon RDS/Aurora, Redshift and TimescaleDB."
    requires = ("sqlalchemy", "psycopg")
    schemes, driver = ("postgresql", "redshift"), "postgresql+psycopg"
    connect_args = {"connect_timeout": 10, "options": "-c statement_timeout=120000"}
    fields = (Field("url", "Connection URL", "postgresql://user:password@host:5432/database", secret=True), QUERY_FIELD)

    def begin(self, conn):
        from sqlalchemy import text
        conn.execute(text("SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY"))


@register
class MySQL(SQLDatabase):
    kind, label = "mysql", "MySQL / MariaDB"
    description = "Also works for PlanetScale, Amazon RDS/Aurora MySQL and Google Cloud SQL."
    requires = ("sqlalchemy", "pymysql")
    schemes, driver = ("mysql", "mariadb"), "mysql+pymysql"
    connect_args = {"connect_timeout": 10, "read_timeout": 120}
    fields = (Field("url", "Connection URL", "mysql://user:password@host:3306/database", secret=True), QUERY_FIELD)


@register
class SQLServer(SQLDatabase):
    kind, label = "sqlserver", "Microsoft SQL Server"
    description = "Also works for Azure SQL Database."
    requires = ("sqlalchemy", "pymssql")
    schemes, driver = ("mssql",), "mssql+pymssql"
    connect_args = {"login_timeout": 10, "timeout": 120}
    fields = (Field("url", "Connection URL", "mssql://user:password@host:1433/database", secret=True), QUERY_FIELD)


@register
class OtherSQL(SQLDatabase):
    kind, label = "sql", "Other SQL database"
    description = ("Snowflake, BigQuery, Oracle, Databricks, DuckDB… any database with a SQLAlchemy driver "
                   "installed on the server. The URL names the driver.")
    fields = (Field("url", "Connection URL", "snowflake://user:password@account/database/schema", secret=True,
                    help="The driver's SQLAlchemy URL."), QUERY_FIELD)


@register
class SQLite(SQLDatabase):
    kind, label = "sqlite", "SQLite file"
    description = "A .sqlite/.db file on the server, inside a folder listed in DATAPULSE_SQLITE_DIR."
    fields = (Field("path", "File path", "/data/shared/sales.db"), QUERY_FIELD)

    def url(self, settings):
        from sqlalchemy.engine import URL

        roots = [Path(d).resolve() for d in os.getenv("DATAPULSE_SQLITE_DIR", "").split(os.pathsep) if d]
        if not roots:
            raise SourceError("SQLite files are off. Set DATAPULSE_SQLITE_DIR on the server to the folder they live in.")
        path = Path(settings["path"]).expanduser().resolve()
        if not any(root in path.parents for root in roots) or not path.is_file():
            raise SourceError(f"No SQLite file at that path inside {', '.join(map(str, roots))}.")
        return URL.create("sqlite", database=f"file:{path}?mode=ro", query={"uri": "true"})

"""Read a table that was written into a document as text (a PDF of a spreadsheet, for example)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from datapulse.io import normalize_frame

_PAGE = re.compile(r"^\[page \d+\]$")
# Comma first: that is how a spreadsheet saved as a PDF of its rows is written.
_DELIMITERS = (",", "\t", "|", ";")


def document_frame(path, label: str) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    """The spreadsheet inside a PDF, Word, text or HTML file, or None when it is prose, a scan, or a picture."""
    from datapulse.unifier.documents import full_text

    # Not read_documents: its text cap is sized for an AI prompt and would cut the table's rows off.
    text = full_text(Path(path), label)
    return None if text is None else table_from_text(text, label)


def table_from_text(text: str, label: str = "document") -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    """A DataFrame when `text` is a header plus rows in one delimiter. None for prose."""
    lines = [line.strip() for line in text.splitlines()]
    lines = [line for line in lines if line and not _PAGE.match(line)]
    if len(lines) < 2:
        return None
    delimiter = _delimiter(lines)
    if delimiter is None:
        return None
    rows = list(csv.reader(lines, delimiter=delimiter))
    width = len(rows[0])
    if width < 2 or any(len(row) != width for row in rows):
        return None
    header = [cell.strip() for cell in rows[0]]
    if len(set(header)) != len(header) or any(not cell or len(cell) > 60 or len(cell.split()) > 4 for cell in header):
        return None
    # The same inference a CSV upload uses, so types match the spreadsheet the table was copied from.
    frame = pd.read_csv(io.StringIO("\n".join(lines)), sep=delimiter)
    if list(frame.columns) != header:
        return None
    return normalize_frame(frame, label)


def _delimiter(lines: list[str]) -> str | None:
    for delimiter in _DELIMITERS:
        widths = [len(row) for row in csv.reader(lines, delimiter=delimiter)]
        if widths and min(widths) >= 2 and len(set(widths)) == 1:
            return delimiter
    return None

"""Fetch a web page; the AI unifier extracts records from its content."""
from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.sources.base import Field, Source, register
from datapulse.sources.http import fetch_bytes, require_str
from datapulse.unifier.documents import html_to_text


class WebPageSource:
    kind = "web_page"

    def fetch(self, config: dict, secrets: dict, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
        url = require_str(config, "url", label="Web page URL")
        body, headers, _ = fetch_bytes(url, headers={"User-Agent": "DataPulse/0.1 (+data unifier)"})
        if not body.strip():
            raise DataPulseError("The web page was empty")
        ctype = headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
        ext = ".pdf" if "application/pdf" in ctype else ".txt" if ctype.startswith("text/plain") else ".html"
        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = dest_dir / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{ext}"
        path.write_bytes(body)
        return path


def page_frame(url: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row describing a page: its address, title and text."""
    body, headers, final = fetch_bytes(url, headers={"User-Agent": "DataPulse/0.1 (+data unifier)"})
    if not body.strip():
        raise DataPulseError("The web page was empty")
    ctype = headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
    if "html" not in ctype and not body.lstrip()[:15].lower().startswith((b"<!doctype html", b"<html")):
        text, title = body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip(), ""
    else:
        title, text = html_to_text(body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    text = text.strip()
    if not text and not title:
        raise DataPulseError("The page has no readable text")
    return pd.DataFrame([{"url": final or url, "title": title[:300], "text": text[:50_000]}])


@register
class WebPage(Source):
    kind, label, group = "web_page", "Web page", "Files & links"
    description = "A public page. DataPulse reads its title and text into one row. Use test data to try example.com."
    requires = ("httpx",)
    has_sample = True
    fields = (Field("url", "Page URL", "https://example.com"),)

    def fetch(self, settings, max_rows):
        return page_frame(settings["url"]).head(max_rows + 1)

    def test(self, settings):
        row = page_frame(settings["url"]).iloc[0]
        title = f": {row['title']}" if row["title"] else ""
        return f"Read the page{title} ({len(str(row['text']).split()):,} words)."

"""Data-source plug-ins. Every module in this package is loaded, so adding a source is adding one file.

See base.py for the contract and link.py for the simplest example.
"""
import importlib
import pkgutil

from .base import (REGISTRY, Field, Source, SourceError, catalog, clean_settings, fetch_source, flatten,
                   public_settings, register, try_connection)

for _module in pkgutil.iter_modules(__path__):
    if _module.name != "base":
        importlib.import_module(f"{__name__}.{_module.name}")


def type_of(kind: str, settings: dict) -> str:
    """What a source is, for display: "PostgreSQL", "MongoDB", "File"... (a Database source is told by its URL)."""
    from .database import type_label
    if kind == "database":
        return type_label(settings.get("url", ""))
    return REGISTRY[kind].label if kind in REGISTRY else kind

__all__ = ["REGISTRY", "Field", "Source", "SourceError", "catalog", "clean_settings", "fetch_source", "flatten",
           "public_settings", "register", "try_connection", "type_of"]

FILE [104/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\unifier\agent.py

"""The AI unifier for tabular and nested data: reads sample records and writes a map spec.

Unlike the triage agent, this one sees real values - up to SAMPLE_RECORDS records, each string cut to
MAX_VALUE_CHARS - because it has to understand what the fields mean. Code then applies the spec to every row.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from datapulse.ai import create, get_client, json_format, text_of
from datapulse.config import Config
from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError

from .spec import TRANSFORMS, TYPES, UnifySpec, record_paths

SAMPLE_RECORDS = 25
MAX_VALUE_CHARS = 200

SYSTEM = f"""You are the data unifier for a data platform. Records arrive from databases, spreadsheets, \
Google Sheets, CSV/Excel/Parquet files and JSON APIs, often messy. Write a mapping that turns every record \
into clean, well-typed JSON with clear field names. Code applies your mapping to every row, so you only describe it.

For each output field give:
- name: snake_case. Keep an original name that is already clear snake_case unchanged (users write rules \
against these names); only rename unclear or messy ones ("Order #" -> "order_id", "Cust Email" -> "customer_email").
- from: the exact key or dotted path in the raw record ("customer.email", "items.0.sku"). Copy keys exactly.
- type: one of {", ".join(TYPES)}. Pick the most precise type the values support.
- transform: null or one of {", ".join(TRANSFORMS)}. Use parse_number for "$1,200.50" or "12%", \
parse_date/parse_datetime with a strptime `format` for non-ISO dates such as "03/07/2026", split with a \
`separator` for delimited lists, lowercase_trim for emails and codes. Never use a transform to hide bad \
data: "5 units" is not a number.
- description: one plain-English sentence about what the field means.

Include every meaningful raw field, and nothing that is not in the data. Unpack nested objects into separate \
fields when their parts mean different things. If a previous mapping is given, the source changed shape: keep \
every existing field name and type that still applies, map moved fields to their new location, add new fields, \
and drop fields that disappeared. Also write a one-sentence description of the dataset."""

_NULLABLE = lambda t: {"type": [t, "null"]}
SPEC_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["description", "fields"],
    "properties": {
        "description": {"type": "string"},
        "fields": {"type": "array", "items": {
            "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
            "required": ["name", "from", "type", "transform", "format", "separator", "description"],
            "properties": {
                "name": {"type": "string"}, "from": {"type": "string"},
                "type": {"type": "string", "enum": list(TYPES)},
                "transform": {"anyOf": [{"type": "string", "enum": list(TRANSFORMS)}, {"type": "null"}]},
                "format": _NULLABLE("string"), "separator": _NULLABLE("string"),
                "description": {"type": "string"}}}},
    },
}


def _truncate(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value if len(value) <= MAX_VALUE_CHARS else value[:MAX_VALUE_CHARS] + "…"
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: _truncate(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_truncate(v) for v in value[:10]]
    return value


def sample_records(records: List[Dict[str, Any]], n: int = SAMPLE_RECORDS) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Evenly spaced records, so the sample is not just the first rows of a sorted export."""
    if len(records) <= n:
        picked = records
    else:
        step = len(records) / n
        picked = [records[int(i * step)] for i in range(n)]
    return [_truncate(r) for r in picked]


def propose_spec(records: List[Dict[str, Any]], previous: Optional[UnifySpec], cfg: Config,
                 client=None, attempts: int = 2, source_label: str = "") -> UnifySpec:
    """Ask the AI for a map spec. Invalid output goes back to the model once before giving up."""
    client = client or get_client(cfg)
    context = {
        "source": source_label or "unknown",
        "total_records": len(records),
        "raw_field_paths": record_paths(records)[:300],
        "sample_records": sample_records(records),
        "previous_mapping": previous.to_dict() if previous else None,
    }
    messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [{"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(context, default=str)}]
    error = ""
    for _ in range(attempts):
        response = create(client, cfg, system=SYSTEM, output_config=json_format(SPEC_SCHEMA), messages=messages)
        try:
            draft = json.loads(text_of(response))
            return UnifySpec.from_dict({**draft, "mode": "map"})
        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, DataPulseError) as e:
            error = str(e)
            messages += [{"role": "assistant", "content": response.content},
                         {"role": "user", "content": f"That mapping is invalid: {error}\nReturn a corrected mapping."}]
    raise DataPulseError(f"The AI could not produce a valid mapping ({error}).")

"""Read unstructured inputs - PDF, images, Word, Markdown/text, email, HTML, zip - into Documents."""
from __future__ import annotations

import email
import email.policy
import io as _io
import zipfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple

from datapulse.errors import DataPulseError
from datapulse.io import ARCHIVES, DOCUMENTS, INPUTS, SUPPORTED

MAX_DOCUMENTS = 50
MAX_TOTAL_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_TEXT_CHARS = 100_000
IMAGE_TYPES = {".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".webp": "image/webp",
               ".gif": "image/gif"}


@dataclass
class Document:
    name: str
    kind: str  # pdf | image | docx | text | email | html
    text: str = ""
    data: Optional[bytes] = None  # PDFs and images go to the AI as files, so it can read scans
    media_type: Optional[str] = None
    pages: Optional[int] = None
    size: int = 0
    truncated: bool = False


def _cap(doc: Document) -> Document:
    if len(doc.text) > MAX_TEXT_CHARS:
        doc.text = doc.text[:MAX_TEXT_CHARS]
        doc.truncated = True
    return doc


# ---------- per format ----------

def _pdf(name: str, data: bytes) -> Document:
    text, pages = "", None
    try:
        from pypdf import PdfReader

        reader = PdfReader(_io.BytesIO(data))
        pages = len(reader.pages)
        text = "\n\n".join(f"[page {n}]\n{(page.extract_text() or '').strip()}"
                           for n, page in enumerate(reader.pages, 1))
    except ImportError:
        pass  # the AI still reads the PDF itself; only the offline fallback loses its text
    except Exception as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{name} is not a readable PDF: {exc}")
    return Document(name=name, kind="pdf", text=text, data=data, media_type="application/pdf", pages=pages,
                    size=len(data))


def _docx(name: str, data: bytes) -> Document:
    try:
        import docx
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise DataPulseError("Reading .docx files needs python-docx: pip install python-docx") from exc
    try:
        d = docx.Document(_io.BytesIO(data))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{name} is not a readable Word document: {exc}")
    parts = [p.text for p in d.paragraphs if p.text.strip()]
    for table in d.tables:
        parts.append("\n".join("\t".join(cell.text.strip() for cell in row.cells) for row in table.rows))
    return Document(name=name, kind="docx", text="\n".join(parts), size=len(data))


class _TextFromHTML(HTMLParser):
    SKIP = {"script", "style", "noscript", "template", "svg", "head"}
    BLOCK = {"p", "div", "br", "li", "tr", "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "section", "article", "table",
             "ul", "ol", "header", "footer", "pre", "blockquote"}

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=True)
        self.parts: List[str] = []
        self.title = ""
        self._skip = 0
        self._in_title = False

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        if tag == "title":
            self._in_title = True
        if tag in self.SKIP:
            self._skip += 1
        elif tag in ("td", "th"):
            self.parts.append("\t")
        elif tag in self.BLOCK:
            self.parts.append("\n")

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag == "title":
            self._in_title = False
        if tag in self.SKIP and self._skip:
            self._skip -= 1
        elif tag in self.BLOCK:
            self.parts.append("\n")

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if self._in_title:
            self.title += data
        elif not self._skip:
            self.parts.append(data)

    def text(self) -> str:
        lines = []
        for line in "".join(self.parts).splitlines():
            cells = [" ".join(cell.split()) for cell in line.split("\t")]  # tabs separate table cells
            lines.append("\t".join(cell for cell in cells if cell))
        return "\n".join(line for line in lines if line)


def html_to_text(html: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    parser = _TextFromHTML()
    parser.feed(html)
    parser.close()
    return parser.title.strip(), parser.text()


def _decode(data: bytes) -> str:
    for encoding in ("utf-8-sig", "utf-16"):
        try:
            return data.decode(encoding)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
    return data.decode("latin-1")


def _html(name: str, data: bytes) -> Document:
    title, text = html_to_text(_decode(data))
    return Document(name=name, kind="html", text=(f"{title}\n\n{text}" if title else text), size=len(data))


def _email(name: str, data: bytes) -> List[Document]:
    msg = email.message_from_bytes(data, policy=email.policy.default)
    header = "\n".join(f"{h}: {msg[h]}" for h in ("Subject", "From", "To", "Cc", "Date") if msg[h])
    body = msg.get_body(preferencelist=("plain", "html"))
    text = ""
    if body is not None:
        content = body.get_content()
        text = html_to_text(content)[1] if body.get_content_subtype() == "html" else content
    docs = [Document(name=name, kind="email", text=f"{header}\n\n{text.strip()}", size=len(data))]
    for part in msg.iter_attachments():
        filename = part.get_filename()
        if not filename or Path(filename).suffix.lower() not in DOCUMENTS:
            continue
        payload = part.get_payload(decode=True) or b""
        docs.extend(_read_one(f"{name} / {filename}", payload, Path(filename).suffix))
    return docs


def _read_one(name: str, data: bytes, ext: str) -> List[Document]:
    ext = ext.lower()
    if ext == ".pdf":
        return [_cap(_pdf(name, data))]
    if ext in IMAGE_TYPES:
        return [Document(name=name, kind="image", data=data, media_type=IMAGE_TYPES[ext], size=len(data))]
    if ext == ".docx":
        return [_cap(_docx(name, data))]
    if ext in (".md", ".markdown", ".txt"):
        return [_cap(Document(name=name, kind="text", text=_decode(data), size=len(data)))]
    if ext in (".html", ".htm"):
        return [_cap(_html(name, data))]
    if ext == ".eml":
        return [_cap(d) for d in _email(name, data)]
    raise DataPulseError(f"Unsupported document type '{ext}' for {name}")


def full_text(path: Path, name: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """All the text of one PDF, Word, text or HTML file, without the AI prompt cap. None for other kinds."""
    path = Path(path)
    ext = path.suffix.lower()
    if ext not in (".pdf", ".docx", ".md", ".markdown", ".txt", ".html", ".htm"):
        return None
    if path.stat().st_size > MAX_TOTAL_BYTES:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{name} is over the 25 MB limit for one upload or pull")
    data = path.read_bytes()
    if ext == ".pdf":
        return _pdf(name, data).text
    if ext == ".docx":
        return _docx(name, data).text
    if ext in (".html", ".htm"):
        return html_to_text(_decode(data))[1]
    return _decode(data)


def read_documents(files: List[Tuple[Path, str]]) -> Tuple[List[Document], List[dict]]:
    """files: (path on disk, name the user knows). Returns (documents, skipped [{name, reason}])."""
    docs: List[Document] = []
    skipped: List[dict] = []
    total = 0
    for path, name in files:
        data = Path(path).read_bytes()
        total += len(data)
        if total > MAX_TOTAL_BYTES:
            raise DataPulseError("Documents exceed the 25 MB limit for one upload or pull")
        try:
            docs.extend(_read_one(name, data, Path(path).suffix))
        except DataPulseError as exc:
            skipped.append({"name": name, "reason": str(exc)})
    if len(docs) > MAX_DOCUMENTS:
        skipped.extend({"name": d.name, "reason": f"over the {MAX_DOCUMENTS}-document limit"}
                       for d in docs[MAX_DOCUMENTS:])
        docs = docs[:MAX_DOCUMENTS]
    if not docs:
        reasons = "; ".join(f"{s['name']}: {s['reason']}" for s in skipped[:3])
        raise DataPulseError(f"No readable documents{f' ({reasons})' if reasons else ''}")
    return docs, skipped


def expand_zip(path: Path, dest: Path, label: str) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[Path, str]], List[dict]]:
    """Unpack a zip's supported files into dest. Guards against zip bombs and path tricks."""
    out: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []
    skipped: List[dict] = []
    try:
        archive = zipfile.ZipFile(path)
    except zipfile.BadZipFile as exc:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{label} is not a valid zip file: {exc}")
    with archive:
        members = [m for m in archive.infolist() if not m.is_dir() and not Path(m.filename).name.startswith(".")
                   and "__MACOSX" not in m.filename]
        if sum(m.file_size for m in members) > MAX_TOTAL_BYTES:
            raise DataPulseError(f"{label} unpacks to more than 25 MB")
        for m in members:
            inner = Path(m.filename).name  # flatten folders; never write outside dest
            ext = Path(inner).suffix.lower()
            if ext not in INPUTS or ext in ARCHIVES:
                skipped.append({"name": f"{label} / {m.filename}", "reason": f"unsupported file type '{ext}'"})
                continue
            target = dest / f"{len(out):03d}-{inner}"
            target.write_bytes(archive.read(m))
            out.append((target, f"{label} / {m.filename}"))
    if not out:
        raise DataPulseError(f"{label} has no supported files")
    return out, skipped


def is_document(path_or_name) -> bool:
    return Path(str(path_or_name)).suffix.lower() in DOCUMENTS


def is_tabular(path_or_name) -> bool:
    return Path(str(path_or_name)).suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED


================================================================================
FILE [106/142]: D:\my_git\folder_extractor\Datapulse-main (1)\Datapulse-main\src\datapulse\unifier\extract.py
